- ruling-kind-lint-scope: The lint checks each Plan line present and that a Home exists, not which platforms a ruling needs nor that the home enforces it. Decision: rulings-declare-kind
- ruling-kind-write-judge-misses: The Write and Edit prompt judges are models and can miss a case. only the Write judge and the lint check a whole record. Decision: rulings-declare-kind
- one-shared-record-stamp-left-out: The stamp engine leaves out build steps, debts, the flat-file fallback when ORDER.json is missing, branch-side reads (--stale, --unclaim, --landed, --porcelain, merging()), and folder/prefix forms with no pending form. Decision: one-shared-record-stamp
- stale-safe-force-push-sha-lease: A lease with a sha guards only the one ref it names. the design accepts this, and git's own lease plus --force-if-includes still protects where the guard reads no push. Decision: stale-safe-force-push
- stall-watch-windows-stop-check: Windows is not covered by the stall hook (it returns nothing when os.name is not posix). the Stop-time check reports a background shell left open. Decision: stall-watch
- stall-watch-quiet-job: A command that prints progress forever is never reported, even when stuck. A quiet healthy job gets a STALL report, and the wrapper keeps exit 3 (owner ruling, 2026-10-18 15:15 CDT). Decision: stall-watch
- stall-watch-log-and-child: After a stall report the temp-directory log stays and nothing removes it; janitor/temp_sweep.py has no stall-watch rule. a command that backgrounds its own child is not watched (the guard refuses that shape). Decision: stall-watch
- stall-watch-test-scratch: The SCRATCH folder in hooks/test_stall_watch.py uses tempfile.mkdtemp, not the run-folder standard in lint/_test_temp.py. Decision: stall-watch
- stall-watch-backslash: A trailing backslash continuation or an odd run of backslashes at the end of a command: the hook probes /bin/sh and doubles the last backslash when sh keeps it literal. Decision: stall-watch
- build-dirs-reap-limits: A crashed agent's build output waits for a later agent end in the same repository. Scratchpad derived data keeps until build-disk-guard lands. An agent resumed after 30 idle min rebuilds cold. After the last agent end in a repository, the ending agent's own tree keeps its build output until a later agent end there. on Windows a link swapped in between rename and delete is not covered. Decision: build-dirs-reaped-at-agent-end
- cwd-under-checkout-realpath-error: `sweep._cwd_under_checkout` returns False when realpath raises, which reads as "no process" in the worktree and build checks. Decision: build-dirs-reaped-at-agent-end
- build-keep-repeats-worktree-checks: `build_keep` (janitor/agent_end_reap.py) repeats the lock, session and process checks of `sweep.decide_worktree` with one changed lock policy, so the two can drift. Decision: build-dirs-reaped-at-agent-end
- all-cwds-drops-refused-pids: `all_cwds` (janitor/agent_end_reap.py) drops a pid whose cwd read is refused (Windows PEB, another user), so a build there is invisible to the build check; keeping on it would keep every tree forever. Decision: build-dirs-reaped-at-agent-end
- ignored-sources-not-edits: the build check's edit read skips git-ignored files, so a fresh edit to an ignored but real source (.env, generated code) does not keep the build folders. Decision: build-dirs-reaped-at-agent-end
- build-dirs-reopen-triggers: Reopen the build-folder reap on a hook_cancelled or timedOut:true end, a folder deleted outside the checks, a link followed, or trash names that nothing deletes. Decision: build-dirs-reaped-at-agent-end
- plain-diff-untracked-files: The plain-diff mod diffs against HEAD or the empty tree. untracked files are not shown. Decision: plain-diff-mod-limits
- impeccable-guard-limits: The impeccable-review-only guard allows 'audit then polish' in one call and does not read through the launchers pnpx, bun x, timeout N, cmd /c or powershell -Command. Decision: design-skills
- waiter-monitor-failures-unmeasured: Unmeasured whether Monitor still errors. reopen on a counted rise of Monitor failures in agent reports, or a waiter count in the guard-log table that does not fall. Decision: agents-wait-with-monitor
- waiter-subshell-bodies-unparsed: bash -c bodies and subshells such as (sleep 30) and $(sleep 30) are not parsed. the owner accepted this. Decision: agents-wait-with-monitor
- waiter-sleep-multi-arg-reads-first: sleep 20 20 reads as 20 s, not 40 s, and passes. an agent may still burn up to 25 s per call on one pause. Decision: agents-wait-with-monitor
- waiter-loop-flag-spans-command: the loop flag spans the whole command, so a pause after any unrelated loop is refused. a loop with no loop word, such as a PowerShell ForEach with Start-Sleep, is unseen. Decision: agents-wait-with-monitor
- waiter-watch-false-alarms: echo {sleep}, --watch=false and gh pr checks 5 -R --watch are refused, all false alarms. no test pins --repo o/r, --repo=o/r, -Ro/r or the gh run -R watch list guard. Decision: agents-wait-with-monitor
- main-protection-skipped-job-passes: a skipped job counts as passing, so a draft run that skips satisfies every required check. the merge tool refuses that head, GitHub does not. Decision: main-branch-protection
- main-protection-strict-false: strict is false, so a PR green on an old base can merge over a newer main. the merge tool read and CI on main after the push cover it. Decision: main-branch-protection
- frozen-var-open-value-shapes: rule 7 misses a variable value built by $(...), backticks, read, ${D:-x}, a loop variable or eval, and a variable set in another tool call. Decision: frozen-path-resolves-shell-vars
- frozen-var-interpreter-and-psenv: rule 7 misses a write by an interpreter (python -c) and a PowerShell $env:D assignment. Unmeasured: how often a session reaches a frozen path by an open shape. Decision: frozen-path-resolves-shell-vars
- frozen-var-loose-token-match: the token match is loose. echo D=<frozen>, a commit message, URL or heredoc body counts as an assignment, so a later write to "$D" is refused. The owner accepted this false alarm. Decision: frozen-path-resolves-shell-vars
- worktree-remove-quoted-link-name: a symlink whose name git quotes, such as one with a space or an accent, still counts as work when the guard judges a worktree remove. Decision: agent-worktree-discard-is-noted
- ci-templates-wrappers-unmeasured: the all-jobs-passed and ci-alarm checks are proved on their scripts, not on a hosted runner. the action.yml wrappers are unmeasured on a real run. Decision: opt-in-ci-templates-composite
- silent-undo-flag-print-no-cap-unrecorded: The 'print every flagged line, no cap' choice left the decision and is unverified in lint/check_silent_undo.py. Decision: silent-undo-check-design
- finished-agent-lock-holds-tree: A finished agent's worktree can hold a lock whose pid is the live host, so the tree stays until the host exits. Decision: none
- stall-open-job-no-age-ceiling: Open jobs in hooks/stall_watch.py have no age ceiling, so a job left from a resumed session reads as still running. Decision: stall-watch
- guard-modules-stay-flat: installers copy files, not folders, and .github/scripts/ci-scope.sh and hooks/test_install_src.sh glob hooks/guard_*.py, so guard code in a subfolder is missed. Decision: none
- undo-rename-below-pair: check_silent_undo reads a rename below git's 50% similarity as a delete and an add, so a small file renamed and rewritten with a pin bump still reads as `hunk reversed` (lint/check_silent_undo.py renamed). Decision: silent-undo-check-design
- undo-rename-earlier-side: the earlier commits are read --no-renames, so main renaming X to Y while a PR rewrites all of a small X reads as `Y: hunk reversed` (lint/check_silent_undo.py Commit.files). Decision: silent-undo-check-design
- undo-rename-history-parent: history mode reads a rename between base and parent as lines that parent added. A later merge then reads as a drop. Check mode skips it through the refs filter (lint/check_silent_undo.py lost_in_parent). Decision: silent-undo-check-design
- git-h-stash-reads-bare: `git -h stash` allows on main. The guard skips -h as a git option and reads a bare git stash, which passes over a clean tree. `bash -c "git stash pop"` allows too, the known wrapper gap in guard-shell-wrap-discard-unread (hooks/guard_core.py _git_subcommand_index). Decision: stash-stack-is-a-shared-subject (the stash stack is a shared subject)
- catalog-cell-pipes-over-cap: cell() cuts a cell to CELL_CAP before it writes each pipe as \|, so a cut cell can render a few chars over the cap. Test e13 goes red on any cut cell, so no cell is cut today (lint/hook_catalog.py). Decision: catalog-cells-never-cut (catalog cells are never cut)
- comment-narration-limits: lint/check_comment_narration.py, also run by actions/code-check in every pinning repo, has no suppression marker. It flags these legit lines: a hex id; "formerly" in domain text, such as "Columns formerly named amt are still read"; "changed from a draft to a shipped state"; "Previously used tokens are rejected" at a sentence start; "Rows added after review stay pending". It also flags "the old behaviour" before an irregular participle, such as "used by callers". The history phrase pattern names fixed word forms, so it misses other history wording: "previously" inside a sentence, "Changed from list to set", "Added in response to review", "This used to return None", and "the old behaviour" before a past verb with by, for, on, in, at, with or from after it, such as "failed on". It misses a dated history line after until, effective, valid or expires, such as "Until 2026-10-30 this returned None". A moved block shows as added. An unreadable file does not stop the scan: the other files are read, their hits print, and the run exits 2 as unknown. The JS, TS and Astro part needs npx and the registry (or tsc on PATH); without them the run exits 3 and code-check goes red. This holds even for a change that only deletes JS lines, since the file is listed before its hunks are read. No test pins a node per-file error row, or the Astro attribute-expression, is:inline, typed script and void-tag cases (probes pass). New vendored code in a pinning repo cannot be cleared: the exclude list may only shrink. Decision: none It also passes history whose noun is snapshot or run, such as "changed from the old snapshot format to JSON".
- quoted-re-elision-residue: lint/report_guard.py QUOTED_RE treats only listed elisions ('til, 'cause, 'em, '25 and kin) as lone. An unlisted one ('cuz, 'fore) still pairs with a later quote and can hide a Deviations admission. A real quote that opens with a two-digit number reads as unquoted. Decision: mistake-means-missing-guard.
- start-freshness-lagging-main: hooks/session_start.sh counts freshness as main..origin/main, so a pointer detached at origin/main's tip with a lagging local main prints "N commits behind origin/main" though it runs the newest rules.
- linked-tree-cites-old-decision: install.sh, install.ps1, hooks/test_install_src.sh and install.test.ps1 cite merge-reinstalls-from-main for the rule that a linked worktree is allowed only at <clone>-live, detached at main. That rule now lives in install-refuses-linked-tree. The off-main line comment in hooks/test_install_src.sh is the same case. Fix: re-point the comments that state these rules. Decision: install-refuses-linked-tree (the installer refuses a linked worktree, except the live pin)
- stall-list-no-child-cap: the STALL report lists every process under the job with no cap, so a job with hundreds of children prints hundreds of rows and one long pid list. The orphan and TERM-trap limits are in the ruling (hooks/stall_watch.py job_tree, report). Decision: stall-watch (a stalled background command is reported, never stopped)
- measured-statements-no-home: hooks/guard_env.py holds "29 false `env-file` refusals" in a docstring, and the GIT_ENV_REASON refusal text in hooks/guard_kill.py holds a "Measured in q_max on 2026-10-14" clause. No decision holds either measurement. Fix: move each to its decision, or drop it. Decision: none for the first; git-env-set-refused (a command that sets a git location variable is refused) for the second
- guard-family-twin-elsewhere: five duplicate cases cut in known-cuts-tests keep their twin in another GUARD_FAMILY (wsl and push-delete twins sit in stream; ask-alone twins sit in shared-tree and push-delete). A run of one of those families alone skips those inputs; the full suite and CI keep them. The delete-scanner ALLOW header in hooks/test_guard.py still says it holds the same constructs, but its lone-apostrophe case now lives in stream. Decision: none
- stall-test-exec-loses-mark: a test job that execs (`exec sleep N`, or zsh's last command) loses the shell's `$0` mark, so reap() finds it only through the pid its report names (hooks/test_stall_watch.py run_rewritten). Fix if one leaks: kill the job's process group. Decision: none
- sweep-fetch-fail-wrong-cause: when the fetch fails but ls-remote works, a tree reads `unpushed` with no fetch-failed mark (janitor/sweep.py sweep_repo). Kept, so safe. Fix: also mark reasons starting with unpushed. Decision: when-a-worktree-may-be-removed
- sweep-fetch-fail-wrong-cause: when the fetch fails but ls-remote works, a tree reads `unpushed` with no fetch-failed mark (janitor/sweep.py sweep_repo). Kept, so safe. Fix: none — the tree is kept either way; only the reason label differs, and the decision limits the fetch-failed mark to unreadable-subject. Decision: when-a-worktree-may-be-removed
- sweep-preview-prunes-refs: the preview header says no branch or worktree is deleted, but the fetch prunes remote-tracking branches in preview (janitor/sweep.py preview header in main). Decision: when-a-worktree-may-be-removed
- sweep-fetch-untested: no case pins `: fetch failed`, `--no-tags` or the deadline cap, and no mutant covers the fetch or head-drop code. Decision: when-a-worktree-may-be-removed
- sweep-head-drop-widens-merged-read: a remote head the clone lacks is dropped, so trees main kept as unreadable now reach the merged read. Its reverted-patch risk then reaches more trees. This happens only when the fetch fails or a head sits outside the refspec (janitor/sweep.py _live_remote_heads). Decision: when-a-worktree-may-be-removed
- sweep-fetch-eats-sessionend-budget: the fetch can take up to 10 s of the SessionEnd budget, so a slow remote gives more `not judged: deadline` keeps; unmeasured (janitor/sweep.py _fetch_prune). Decision: when-a-worktree-may-be-removed
- test-sweep-origin-head-platform: janitor/test_sweep.py give_remote runs `init --bare` with no `-b`, so origin's HEAD is master on Linux and main on macOS (Apple git sets init.defaultBranch). A fixture that clones origin without `-b main` fails on Linux only. Decision: none
- watch-flat-names-win: config_watch puts its own folder before the guard.d set on the import path, so a flat shared_names.py left stale by a failed install wins over the set copy (hooks/config_watch.py). Decision: config-watch-fast-exit
- watch-reads-guard-each-call: the watch reads guard.py for its INSTALL_ID on every call, even on the fast exit; small cost (hooks/config_watch.py _add_guard_set_dir). Decision: config-watch-fast-exit
- watch-escaped-cap-key: a JSON-escaped cap key outside env is a lift to the guard but not to the fast path, so its expiry check is skipped; only an attacker writes it (hooks/config_watch.py lift_candidate). Decision: config-watch-fast-exit
- watch-bad-json-mixed-cap: in unparseable JSON the last text cap assignment wins, so opus then sonnet reads as Sonnet; such a file loads no settings (hooks/config_watch.py). Decision: config-watch-fast-exit
- watch-sonnet-1m-reported: a first-sight cap of sonnet[1m] reads as unknown, a false alarm in the safe direction (hooks/config_watch.py). Decision: config-watch-fast-exit
- config-dir-copies: config_dir logic is copied in daily_note, override, precompact_handoff and sim_boot_record; shared_names is its home (hooks/shared_names.py). Decision: config-watch-fast-exit
- drops-body-nested-fence: a 4-backtick fence that holds a 3-backtick fence closes on the 3 backticks, so lines after it read as body text. Decision: silent-undo-check-design (one check catches a merge or branch that silently undoes earlier work)
- drops-body-fence-in-comment: a fence opener inside an `<!-- -->` comment drops a real trailer after it (false alarm). Decision: silent-undo-check-design (one check catches a merge or branch that silently undoes earlier work)
- drops-body-edit-no-commit: a PR body edit excuses a drop without a new commit, so the head does not change. Decision: silent-undo-check-design (one check catches a merge or branch that silently undoes earlier work)
- drops-body-read-at-run-start: `confirm` reads the PR body once at run start, so an edit during the wait is not read. Decision: silent-undo-check-design (one check catches a merge or branch that silently undoes earlier work)
- sweep-failed-fetch-stale-reap: when the SessionEnd fetch fails with time left, decide_branch still reaps on the stale on-remote read, as on main and as janitor/GUIDE.md documents (janitor/sweep.py _fetch_prune). Decision: when-a-worktree-may-be-removed
- ci-wake-prefilter-unicode-escape: the ci_wake shell prefilter misses a "ready" written as a JSON unicode escape (ready), which python decodes; Claude Code never escapes ASCII letters (settings.json ci_wake row). Decision: none
- verdict-prune-reused-temp-log: a caller that reuses a VERDICT_LOG named verdict-*.log inside the temp folder can lose it to another run's prune once it is 7 days idle (bin/verdict prune). Decision: none
- delete-rule-quoted-text: the first DESTRUCTIVE_DELETE pattern has no UnquotedStart wrapper, so quoted text such as `echo "rm -r -f ."` or `echo "del -Recurse -Force C:/"` is refused as a delete; the second pattern skips quoted text (hooks/guard_push_delete.py). Checked on the patterns and the guard's refusal loop, not through a full guard run. Decision: rm-path-wrapper (an rm shim on PATH, with the guard holding the line on Windows)
- rm-guard-home-parents-pass: the guard refuses a recursive delete of the working folder and every folder above it. It does not refuse a folder above home when the working folder is outside it, so `rm -r C:/Users` passes from D:/proj. The Mac and Linux shim refuses it (hooks/guard_push_delete.py _parent_target, hooks/guard_rm_var.py _rm_target_bad). Checked on the two functions, not through a full guard run. Decision: rm-path-wrapper (an rm shim on PATH, with the guard holding the line on Windows)
- eol-scan-bom-env-u: the eol scan reads line 1 as ASCII. A UTF-8 BOM then hides a #! line. The scan also reads the name after `env -u` (as in `#!/usr/bin/env -S -u NAME bash`) as the interpreter. An extensionless shell script with either form is not scanned for CR, and a .sh name still is (install-bin-eol.test.ps1 Get-FirstLine, Get-Interpreter). Measured with the real functions on fixtures, 2026-10-22. Decision: shell-scripts-check-out-lf (shell scripts check out with LF)
- pointer-windows-eol-step-cap-unmeasured: the 3 min cap on the pointer-windows step "Install bin eol test, Windows PowerShell 5.1" has no measured step time; decision ci-job-caps (CI job caps) measures whole jobs only (.github/workflows/checks.yml). Unmeasured: no CI step read was made. Decision: ci-job-caps
- scratchpad-shared-per-session: the guard keys a scratchpad by session id, and an agent carries its parent's id. So every agent of one session shares one scratchpad. A test-author may overwrite another agent's folder in it. Only another session's scratchpad is refused (hooks/guard.py role_write_hit, hooks/guard_shared_tree.py under_session_scratchpad). A test-author overwrite was reported, not re-observed. Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop (an agent outside its home tree must stop)
- question-agent-hook-block-unmeasured: what a refusal from the AskUserQuestion agent hook does to the turn is unmeasured, and no test runs the hook against a live defect (settings.json PreToolUse AskUserQuestion). Decision: question-reviewer-agent-hook (one agent hook reviews each question)
- deadline-floor-no-stamp: a repo with no .github/stamp.json waits the 45-minute default, not the largest job cap plus 5 (merge/merge.py load_config). Decision: none
- ste-lint-safepath-env: actions/ste-lint/test_action.py run() inherits os.environ, so with PYTHONSAFEPATH=1 the old python3 -c form passes the planted-module cases; CI does not set it. Decision: none
- janitor-git-timeout-load: janitor/test_sweep.py runs fixture git with a 10 s timeout, which a loaded Windows host exceeds (measured 2026-10-22, two local runs aborted). Decision: none
- cap-floor-base-only: the merge wait floor reads the base branch's workflows. A PR that adds or raises a job cap is not floored in its own merge. The tool stops with "Still pending" and a re-run resumes (merge/merge.py load_config). Decision: none (skill ci-hygiene step 5)
- cap-label-no-step-data: the "re-cap or split" label needs only a cancel at the full cap, so a red CI step followed by a hung always() CI step gets it too (merge/merge.py checks). Decision: none (skill ci-hygiene step 5)
- cap-floor-counts-all-jobs: the floor counts every workflow job, required or not, so a long nightly job lengthens every merge wait and the lock TTL (merge/merge.py load_config). Decision: none (skill ci-hygiene step 5)
- job-caps-scan-limits: job_caps keeps a trailing "# comment" in a job name's id, and a 4-space-indented workflow gives no floor and no warning; other repos' workflows were not read (merge/merge.py job_caps). Decision: none (skill ci-hygiene step 5)
- temp-sweep-copies-session-reader: janitor/temp_sweep.py live_session_ids copies guard_core.session_records, and a truncated record of a live session lets its idle folder go. Decision: windows-temp-sweep (the janitor removes an idle session's temp folder)
- temp-sweep-untested-paths: no test pins the unreadable sessions folder, the deadline stop or _sweep_roots returning 1, and the temp sweep's stderr lines and exit code name no reader (janitor/test_sweep.py, docs/decisions/windows-temp-sweep.md). Decision: windows-temp-sweep (the janitor removes an idle session's temp folder)
- refresh-prune-skips-dirty-tree: prune_trees now keeps an old pin tree with tracked changes for good and logs nothing, where main force-removed it; the cost is disk only (merge/install_from_main.sh prune_trees). Decision: merge-reinstalls-from-main (the pin follows local main)
- refresh-child-survival-unmeasured: whether the desktop app kills the SessionStart refresh's detached child on Windows is unmeasured, and phantom line-ending dirt on another machine's pin is unmeasured (settings.json SessionStart refresh). Decision: merge-reinstalls-from-main (the pin follows local main)
- refresh-runs-clone-helper: the refresh runs the clone's checked-out merge/install_from_main.sh, which on a lane branch is unreviewed code; main ran the pin's install.sh (settings.json SessionStart refresh). Decision: merge-reinstalls-from-main (the pin follows local main)
- refresh-gcm-prompt: GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0 does not stop a Git Credential Manager window on Windows, so the refresh fetch may wait on one (settings.json SessionStart refresh). Decision: none
- install-hkcu-msix-copy: an install.ps1 started from a Claude-spawned shell on a packaged (MSIX) Claude may write its HKCU Path change to the package's private copy (install.ps1 Path write; machine note this-machine-msix-appdata). Unproven. Decision: none
- temp-helpers-predate-standard: existing test temp helpers in this repo, such as actions/stamp/test_stamp.mjs withTempDir, do not follow the run-folder standard; the rule binds new or touched temp code. Decision: test-temp-standard (one run folder per test run)
- test-guard-root-predates-standard: hooks/test_guard.py keeps its own run folder and sweep, a second copy of lint/_test_temp.py. Move it onto lint/_test_temp.py when a lane touches it. Decision: test-temp-standard
- gh-api-write-unguarded: `gh api` with a field and no method sends a POST, and no guard refuses it now, an API merge included; `gh pr merge` keeps its own guard. Owner, 2026-10-25 16:07 CDT, accepted this when the rule was culled. Decision: cull-rules (how a cull runs)
- ios-ship-install-case-gaps: lint/test_ios_ship.sh has no install case for the late copy_in_check, a half-made copy, or the launch stdin from /dev/null. No case reads $ARCHIVE in after_archive. Decision: ios-ship-is-one-shared-tool (one shared tool to ship iOS apps)
- ios-ship-install-term-runs-on: on TERM to bin/ios-ship install, only the run_app subshell traps; the build and install go on, and the copy stays until the build ends. Predates the tool's port. Decision: ios-ship-is-one-shared-tool (one shared tool to ship iOS apps)
- ios-ship-recheck-skips-untracked: bin/ios-ship require_untouched reads tracked files only, so a prebuild that writes an untracked, non-ignored file passes; the next run's clean check refuses it. Archive and after_archive steps are not re-checked. Decision: ios-ship-is-one-shared-tool (one shared tool to ship iOS apps)
- ios-ship-kill-misses-children: bin/ios-ship tf_kill signals the step process only, with no wait. A `bash -c "a && b"` step or an xcodebuild helper can outlive the tool, and a late export write could land after the EXIT .ipa scrub. Unmeasured with real xcodebuild. Decision: ios-ship-is-one-shared-tool (one shared tool to ship iOS apps)
- ios-ship-newline-splits-step: bin/ios-ship reads prebuild and after_archive with jq -r and read -r, so a step string with a newline runs as separate bash -c calls and loses cd/export state between them. Decision: ios-ship-is-one-shared-tool (one shared tool to ship iOS apps)
- ios-ship-test-env-leaks: lint/test_ios_ship.sh tool_exec scrubs the five ID variables and PHONE_APPS_JSON. A caller's exported REINSTALL_HOME, LOG_DIR or XCODEBUILD_* still reaches cases that do not set them. So an owner shell that exports one can turn cases red. Decision: ios-ship-is-one-shared-tool (one shared tool to ship iOS apps)
- merge-revert-by-message-only: merge/merge.py lane_tips finds a dropped lane only by git revert's "This reverts commit <full sha>" line. A hand-reworded revert message, or a revert of the lane's own commits instead of its merge, still deletes the lane branch; it can be rebuilt from the printed tip. Decision: merge-deletes-batch-lanes (the merge tool deletes the batch's lane branches)
- merge-stacked-dropped-lane-kept: a dropped lane whose commits stay live through a later lane in the same batch is kept, one extra branch. Decision: merge-deletes-batch-lanes (the merge tool deletes the batch's lane branches)
- merge-rest-windows-latency: hooks/guard_merge.py MERGE_READ_TIMEOUT (25 s) budgets 5 REST calls at the 3.5 s measured for GraphQL on Windows. REST latency on Windows is unmeasured; Linux cloud measured 0.3-0.7 s per call. Decision: standing-merge-grant (the merge grant and the merge tool)
- merge-blocked-lag: merge/merge.py Host.merge refuses a PR that reads BLOCKED or BEHIND right before the PUT, as gh's own merge did. If GitHub's mergeable_state lags after the checks go green, the run reverts its claim and must run again; it never merges wrongly. The lag is unmeasured. Decision: standing-merge-grant (the merge grant and the merge tool)
- merge-preread-stop-silent: when Host.merge's PR read before the PUT fails with no network error, the run stops, sends nothing and keeps the claim. Its text does not say so. A re-run resumes it. Decision: standing-merge-grant (the merge grant and the merge tool)
- own-lock-frees-quiet-running-agent: janitor/sweep.py frees the caller's own agent lock from any sweep its session runs. A running agent whose tree is clean, merged and quiet 30 min loses its tree, and a resumed agent then stops. Same 30 min trade as the agent-end reap. Decision: the-janitor-is-one-machine-wide-sweep (the janitor is one machine-wide sweep)
- own-lock-climb-reaches-parent-claude: the own-lock ancestor climb runs to pid 1, so a nested `claude -p` session's sweep also frees its parent session's locks, under the same clean, merged and idle tests. Decision: the-janitor-is-one-machine-wide-sweep (the janitor is one machine-wide sweep)
- own-lock-reused-pid-no-start: an own lock with no start time whose pid an ancestor reuses is freed; the tree is still clean, merged and idle. Decision: the-janitor-is-one-machine-wide-sweep (the janitor is one machine-wide sweep)
- own-lock-idle-floor-untested: no case pins the 30 min own-lock floor from above (31 min attended reaps), or a fast run with no batch keeping an own lock (janitor/test_sweep.py OwnAgentLockTests)
- skill-fallback-unpinned: a non-.md path under skills/plain-english-map/, or package*.json, turns `mods` on only through ci-scope.sh's fallback to every flag. No test pins it. A narrower arm that forgets `mods` would skip the skill tests. Decision: none
- inline-copy-names-skill-path: lint/test_windows_split.py INLINE_COPY flags any run command that names plain-english-map, so a later lint or copy step on that path is a false red. Decision: none
- hook-stubs-one-path-form: lint/check_hook_stubs.py sees only the `${CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR:-$HOME/.claude}/<path>` form and assumes each installed path equals the repo path. A hook written another way, or an install that maps a folder differently, passes unchecked. Every settings.json in the window uses that form today. Decision: guard-hooks-fail-closed (guard hooks fail closed)
- merge-tree-removal-no-idle-wait: merge/merge.py cut_branch removes the merged branch's tree with no idle wait. A follow-up to a finished builder after the merge finds its tree and its ignored files gone. No committed work is lost. Decision: when-a-worktree-may-be-removed (when a worktree may be removed)
- merge-tree-base-is-pr-head: cut_branch judges the tree against the PR head, which holds only under the merge method "merge". A squash or rebase method needs the new main commit as the base. Decision: when-a-worktree-may-be-removed (when a worktree may be removed)
- merge-tree-stale-lock-goes: cut_branch follows the sweep's stale agent lock rule, so a tree locked by a dead host is unlocked and removed. Decision: the-janitor-is-one-machine-wide-sweep (the janitor is one machine-wide sweep)
- lane-local-failed-delete-untested: no case pins that a lane whose remote delete fails keeps its local branch and tree (`stays.add` in merge/merge.py cut_lanes), or a keepBranches name whose remote head is already gone. Decision: merge-deletes-batch-lanes (the merge tool deletes the batch's lane branches)
- kill-tag-agent-flag-widened: hooks/guard.py judge counts a truthy agent_type alone as an agent. That flag also feeds playwright_install_hit, so a main session started with `--agent` (unmeasured) loses both the tag kill and the main-session Playwright install. Fails closed. Decision: a-kill-by-pid-needs-an-owned-pid (a kill by pid needs an owned pid)
- kill-tag-owns-shared-daemons: the session tag marks any PPID-1 process forked from this session's tool shell, including a shared daemon (Gradle, XCBBuildService) another session may now use. The main session can stop it by literal pid. Decision: a-kill-by-pid-needs-an-owned-pid (a kill by pid needs an owned pid)
- kill-tag-platform-binary-blind: macOS sysctl shows no environment for an Apple platform binary (/bin/sleep measured), so such an orphan reads foreign and is never owned or reaped. Fails closed. Decision: a-kill-by-pid-needs-an-owned-pid (a kill by pid needs an owned pid)
- layout-check-join-forms: lint/check_layout.py sees a bare record folder name only in a `join` call or a `/`; `Path(root, "decisions")`, `joinpath`, a tuple passed to `join` and `os.sep` concatenation slip past. A hook that builds a non-record `plans` folder by `/` would be flagged. Neither is in the code today. Decision: one-layout-every-repo (one layout for every repo)
- install-set-lock1-race: pointer-windows, install-set.test.ps1 case lock1: ReadAllText on a child's output file threw 'being used by another process' once (run 40332614245, Oct 26); passed on re-run. Becomes a debt if it recurs. Decision: none
- design-review-context-autoload: design-review Reviewer B: a 'Design Context' section in a repo's CLAUDE.md still auto-loads into Reviewer B (from c68, Oct 26). A note until it causes a real incident. Decision: none
- lanecap-quoted-longer-phrase: lint/compliance_probes.py lanecap exempts a cap only when a quote pair wraps the match exactly. So a quoted longer phrase ("keep at most 3 lanes open") is still a hit to read; "(owner" and owner-plus-date can pass a deliberate form. False alarms on citations, which the reader sees. Decision: none
- design-probe-vendored-skill-shot: lint/compliance_probes.py design reports skills/impeccable/scripts/modern-screenshot.umd.js in claude-settings as an own screenshot command, because the vendored skill sits in a non-dot folder. Child repos are unaffected. Decision: shoot-is-one-shared-command (one shared screenshot command)
- playwright-probe-dash-sibling-joins: for `- run: >` the folded block ends at the dash column, so a sibling key such as `env:` joins the command; a hit only if that text names --with-deps (lint/compliance_probes.py folded). Decision: one-browser-setup-no-apt.
- playwright-probe-folded-prose-hits: a folded `description: >` that names `playwright install --with-deps` is a hit, like a quoted echo (lint/compliance_probes.py). Decision: one-browser-setup-no-apt.
- playwright-probe-root-devcontainer: a root `.devcontainer.json` is not read; only the `.devcontainer/` folder is (lint/compliance_probes.py PW_FILE). Decision: one-browser-setup-no-apt.
- blind-brief-word-list-misses: the keep and current word lists miss paraphrases with no sign of today's product, such as "Keep the app's layout", "Match the app" or "Keep the three views"; the spawn hook knows no view names (hooks/blind_brief.py). Decision: blind-briefs-use-a-template.
- blind-brief-count-unchecked: `Blind lanes: <n>` is the orchestrator's own count, never compared with the blind briefs spawned (hooks/blind_brief.py). Decision: blind-briefs-use-a-template.
- pick-guard-drive-letters: a drive name such as "the A: drive and the B: drive" in an Input Needed item reads as two lettered options (lint/report_guard.py OPTION_INLINE_RE). Decision: decisions-reach-a-clickable-pick.
- pick-guard-way-word: a plain ask with an inline numbered list and the word "way", "option" or "choose", such as "Is there a way to run 1. tests 2. lint?", reads as a pick (lint/report_guard.py PICK_WORD_RE). Decision: decisions-reach-a-clickable-pick.
- ci-wake-rest-404-is-nopr: a mistyped -R repo gives a REST 404, read as no PR. So the watch is silent where it said unknown. `gh pr ready` fails the same way, so the error still shows (hooks/ci_wake.py read_pr)
- ci-wake-hash-selector-misses: `gh pr ready #12` takes the branch route (`head=owner:#12`) and ends with no watch; fix is to strip `#` before the digit test (hooks/ci_wake.py read_pr)
- ci-wake-branch-route-narrow: the branch and empty selectors match only open PRs with head `<owner>:<local branch name>`, so a fork head, a branch pushed under another name, or a closed PR gives no watch (hooks/ci_wake.py read_pr)
- ci-wake-branch-metachar-windows: a branch name with `&`, `|`, `<` or `>` reaches cmd through a .cmd gh launcher in the `-F head=` value. Such a name breaks the `gh pr ready` line first (hooks/ci_wake.py read_pr)
- ci-job-ste-ignores-inputs: ci-job runs the checks STE step as `bin/ste-changed <base>` and reads neither its `if:` nor its `with:`, so a `full_ste_audit` dispatch (scope all, fail false) is not mirrored locally. The local run is stricter, never looser (bin/ci-job is_ste_action)
- waiting-opt-shared-slug: two options that share one mockup slug open the first one's sheet and label on "Open full size", as `openDesign` takes the first match (skills/waiting-page/page.html openDesign)
- waiting-opt-sel-untested: no test asserts the `.sel` class after a pick that keeps the option frames, so a stale highlight from a dropped attribute copy would pass (skills/waiting-page/tests/page.test.mjs)
- waiting-opt-id-namespace: the trade-off id `t-<item>-<key>` shares a namespace with the title id `t-<item>`, so items `foo` and `foo-a` would both own `t-foo-a` (skills/waiting-page/page.html optCard)
- waiting-sheet-brand-border: with a brand colour the Designs sheet's 50% `--on-hdr` button border can fall under 3:1 (about 18% of 648 sampled brands); the ring stays 4.5:1 or better (skills/waiting-page/page.html .dsh-head .btn)
- scorecard-indented-caution-row: an indented `x: y` line, such as an indented Caution line, becomes a `finding` row on the group before it, not a Notes line. Nothing is dropped (lint/compliance_probes.py FINDING_LINE)
- try-marker-sunset: hooks/guard.py still reads the old builder marker `Try 3 of 3` beside `Round 3 of 3`, so a brief sent before the rename is not refused. Remove `"try"` from BUILDER_MARKERS, its mutant and its cases on or after 2026-11-12, 14 days after the planned merge (move the date if the merge slips). Decision: agent-model-cap
- step-up-log-restarts: guard.log rows for the third-round step-up name the old id (its row in lint/test_retired_names.py RETIRED) before the o13 rename and `roles-third-round-steps-up` after it, so a cull that counts one id sees a short history. Count both ids at the next cull (decision building-guard-needs-incident)
- round-marker-role-cases: no guard case pins `Round 3 of 3` for a test-author or general-purpose role on Opus, or mid-line in a builder brief. The regex and the role check are shared with `Try 3 of 3`, whose cases and mutants cover them (hooks/test_guard.py rule9)
- step-up-quoted-marker: a builder brief on Sonnet that quotes a `Round 3 of 3` line at a line start is refused, as the `Try 3 of 3` marker was. So is one that quotes it as the leftmost marker on a first line that starts with none. The refusal names the fix (hooks/guard.py builder_third_round). Decision: agent-model-cap
- waiting-designs-mixed-shape: an item that lists `designs` has its options' `design` slugs ignored, so an option whose slug is not in the list warns "No picture" even when its HTML exists (skills/waiting-page/page.html designsOf)
- waiting-viewer-option-letter: an option design from an item with no `designs` list is labelled by `o.label` alone, so the viewer head does not name the option letter (skills/waiting-page/page.html allDesigns)
- waiting-drawn-infinity: `drawn: Infinity` passes the `> 0` check and gives a blank frame; 0, negative, NaN and missing fall back to 390 (skills/waiting-page/page.html fitFrame)
- waiting-fit-cache-drawn: the `FIT` cache key is `slug|focus` and leaves out `drawn`, so two entries for one slug at two widths share one cached box (skills/waiting-page/page.html FIT)
- waiting-cap-font-swap: a web font that swaps in after the viewer opens without changing the title's box leaves the caption state stale until the next fit (skills/waiting-page/page.html capFit)
- waiting-cap-left-wide: at 1440 the viewer caption sits left at x=20 while the mockup is centred (skills/waiting-page/page.html .dcap)
- waiting-dpos-stale-form: after a resize, `#dPos` keeps its old form ("1/3" or "1 of 3") until the next page turn (skills/waiting-page/page.html showDesign)
- waiting-head-midwidth-untested: no test runs the viewer head at 521-900 px with a wide font, where the buttons keep their long labels (skills/waiting-page/tests/page.test.mjs)
- shared-owner-git-case: the owner check rejects a trailing ".git" in lower case only, so "me/x.GIT" passes (lint/layout.py shared_resources)
- shared-name-padding: a name with padding (" a" beside "a") counts as unique (lint/layout.py shared_resources)
- shared-dotted-owner-untested: no green case for a dotted owner such as "me/home.app" (lint/test_check_shared_resources.py)
- port-question-url-forms: the guard matches the waiting-page url as an exact string, so a query string, a fragment or the claude.ai/code/artifact form is not judged (hooks/guard.py port-question; decision port-keeps-behaviour)
- port-question-cwd-untested: no guard case runs a trailing-slash url, a subfolder cwd or a worktree cwd, though the code handles each (hooks/test_guard.py port-question)
- port-question-redesign-word: "redesign" in a blocking item that ports nothing is refused until the writer sets `port: false` (hooks/question_form.py PORT_WORD; decision port-keeps-behaviour)
- main-ref-url-forms-untested: no test covers an scp-style or credentialed origin URL in the FETCH_HEAD match; a wrong normalisation refuses, the old behaviour (githooks/main-ref/reference-transaction fetched)
- other-checkout-git-pointer: the `.git` exemption covers `<owner>/.git` for every checkout, so from the primary a Write to a linked worktree's `.git` pointer file passes; exempting only `git_common_dir` would close it (hooks/guard.py other_checkout_hit)
- other-checkout-cp-source: `cd <sibling> && cp a.txt /tmp/o` is refused because the shell write reader treats every `cp` argument as a target, so a copy out of another checkout reads as a write into it (hooks/guard_frozen.py _shell_write_hit)
- other-checkout-mkdir: `mkdir -p` into another checkout passes, because the shell write reader does not read `mkdir` targets (hooks/guard_frozen.py _shell_write_hit)
- red-run-root-lone-amp: `cp x y & rm -rf <own red>` passes, because `split_segments` does not cut on a lone `&`, so the whole segment reads as the `cp` root carve-out (hooks/guard_core.py split_segments, hooks/guard.py `RED_RUN_ROOT_VERBS`). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop
- red-run-cp-source-refused: `cp <file in primary or a sibling> <own red>/` is refused, but the same copy to `<own red>/f` passes, because `_copy_reads` does not see the root carve-out (hooks/guard_frozen.py _copy_reads). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop
- red-run-bare-clone-primary: in a bare clone `clone_checkouts` drops the bare entry, so `roots[0]`, which the red-run match takes as the primary, is the first linked worktree (hooks/git_reads.py clone_checkouts). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop
- red-run-powershell-stranded: after `Set-Location <own red>` in PowerShell the cwd stays there, and every later call is refused except a lone move home (hooks/guard.py worktree_home_hit). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop
- red-run-clone-order-unpinned: in `elsewhere` the separate-clone test runs before the red-run exemption. No test or mutant pins that order: no clone case carries an `agent_id`, and no red-run case sets `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR`. With the order swapped, a stranded cwd's write into its own red-run folder would pass (hooks/guard.py other_checkout_hit). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop
- cloud-pipx-pep668: install.sh --cloud runs `python3 -m pip install --user pipx`; on an image whose python3 is an externally-managed (PEP 668) system Python, pip refuses it every run, so pipx stays missing behind an honest WARNING. Not hit on today's image (python3 3.13; only 3.12 is marked). Fix if images change: fall back to apt or `--break-system-packages`.
- accent-url-scheme-relative: an unquoted `url(//host/x)` reads as a `//` comment and blanks the rest of its line, so minified CSS with the accent after it on one line gives "unknown"; untested (lint/repo_colour.py _uncommented)
- accent-lone-backtick: a lone backtick in .astro text opens a template literal to the next backtick and can hide a comment, so a commented-out accent could read as the value; untested (lint/repo_colour.py _uncommented)
- waiting-shown-fallback-sibling: two designs of one item share slug and state, and the shown one is renamed under a kept preview; the slug+state fallback opens its sibling. Fix: fall back only on exactly one match (skills/waiting-page/page.html openShown)
- waiting-stale-preview-dead-button: a design removed or changed under a kept front preview leaves the preview stale, and its "Open this state" now does nothing; the stale keep is old (skills/waiting-page/page.html patch, openShown)
- waiting-shown-fallback-untested: no test covers openShown's slug+state fallback or its no-match branch (skills/waiting-page/tests/page.test.mjs)
- waiting-stage-key-label: the stage frame key includes the label. So the Designs tab and an option's View build two frames for one design. A live rename leaves a hidden frame. Harm: extra iframes (skills/waiting-page/page.html showDesign)
- blind-brief-token-edge-refusals: the blind brief value checks refuse some plain job text and miss some forms. A font or typeface pattern spans a newline ("font" at a line end, next line "Uses ..."). Shorthand hex after a colour word has no id guard ("Replace ink cartridge #1234"). The id guard needs one space after the id word ("Order ID: #123456", "Orders #123456"). Words like "12 pixels of padding" pass. A rewrite or an override recovers each refusal (hooks/blind_brief.py PATTERNS, decision blind-briefs-use-a-template)
- lock-boot-shared-by-restored-vms: VMs restored from one snapshot may share a boot id and pid namespace, so a live lock held on another VM could read dead and be taken over. Unmeasured; the lease swap makes the cost a rerun (merge/merge.py boot_stamp). Decision: none
- checks-shoot-required-unproved-offline: the lint tests no longer prove the shoot job is a required check, because the list lives in branch protection, which no offline test reads (lint/test_windows_split.py). Decision: main-branch-protection
- layout-no-such-kind: the layout cannot say "this repo has no such kind", so a repo with no rules or specs keeps a placeholder README, and its "none yet" line goes stale (lint/layout.json, lint/layout.py)
- readme-links-unreachable-forms: the link check still fetches odd IP forms (127.1, decimal, octal), CGNAT 100.64/10 and single-label hosts, and a bracketed IPv6 URL crashes it (the URL regex stops at `]`); main does the same (actions/readme-links/readme_links.py skip_reason, alive)
- glossary-check-template-field-quote: a `'` inside a regex or comment in a `${...}` field of a JS template literal never closes, so later identifiers in the file are not scanned (lint/check_glossary.py template_end). Miss only; a full JS tokenizer would close it
- glossary-check-subshell-heredoc-depth: `((cd a; make) && echo ok)` opens an arithmetic depth that never closes, so a later here-document body reads as code and a retired word in it fails; `glossary-ok` clears it (lint/check_glossary.py)
- glossary-check-platform-name-in-message: a hooks/*.py message with a space that names a platform value (`SubagentStop`, `subagent_type`, `subagents`) fails on the retired word inside it; PLATFORM_NAMES matches whole names only. Three such strings exist in hooks/ today (lint/check_glossary.py)
- uniform-pick-update-no-options: an update that sets `pick` without `options` cannot see the stored options, so a pick of a widening option passes; the writer carries `options` again (hooks/question_form.py widens_pick_hit; decision uniform-over-variants)
- batch-guard-config-from-cwd: the guard takes its pinned merge.requiredChecks and merge.ignoreChecks lists from the cwd's repo even with -R, and skips the tool's pin-drift check (hooks/guard_merge.py pinned_required). Decision: no-merge-during-batch-ci.
- batch-guard-empty-protection-counts-none: an empty protection list counts no required names in guard mode, while the tool refuses it (merge/merge.py batch_running). Decision: no-merge-during-batch-ci.
- batch-stuck-pending-status-blocks: a required status stuck pending on a ready PR blocks every merge to that base until it ends or the PR goes draft (merge/merge.py batch_running). Decision: no-merge-during-batch-ci.
- batch-guard-calls-grow: the guard's call count grows by three for each other ready PR, against its one 25 s budget; Windows timing is unmeasured (hooks/guard_merge.py MERGE_READ_TIMEOUT). Decision: no-merge-during-batch-ci.
- batch-guard-unprotected-status-blocks: with no branch protection, any pending status on a ready PR blocks guarded merges, so `renovate/stability-days` can hold them for days (merge/merge.py batch_running). Decision: no-merge-during-batch-ci.
- batch-guard-403-reads-unknown: a protection read that gets HTTP 403 (unmeasured) reads unknown, so the guard denies `gh pr merge` while another ready PR waits (merge/merge.py protection_names). Decision: no-merge-during-batch-ci.
- ios-mockup-any-system-family: one system family anywhere in the CSS passes system-font, so a mockup whose h1 uses Georgia still passes (lint/check_ios_mockup.py faults). Decision: blind-briefs-use-a-template
- ios-web-screen-needs-duo: in an iOS repo, a designer brief for a web screen must still name the Duo poses and ios-apps. The hook reads the repo's device list, not the screen (hooks/blind_brief.py ios_skill_faults). Decision: blind-briefs-use-a-template
- blind-brief-current-refuses-ios-app: the `current` pattern refuses "the iOS app" in a blind brief, so a Device section cannot say the lane draws an iOS app in those words (hooks/blind_brief.py PATTERNS). Decision: blind-briefs-use-a-template
- ios-duo-order-filler-window: the Duo order check flips only on "after" or "before" followed by filler words, so "Draw the Duo unfolded after drawing the Duo folded." gets a false duo-order and "Draw the Duo folded after you draw the Duo unfolded." passes; lint/check_ios_mockup.py duo-frames backs it up (hooks/blind_brief.py _order_word_between). Decision: blind-briefs-use-a-template
- press-link-unnumbered: an unnumbered press passes with any link; the exact-page check runs only on a `#N`, to avoid false alarms (hooks/question_form.py _press_field_ok). Decision: act-in-place
- command-in-item-tasks-only: a decision option that points at a file's command is not judged; the step scoped the rule to tasks (hooks/question_form.py command_in_item_hit). Decision: act-in-place
- command-in-item-broad: with no `command` on the item, any file step plus "run/paste ... command" is refused; one retry. "Run the /doctor command" after a file step is refused too; "Paste the script from plans/x.md" passes (hooks/question_form.py command_in_item_hit). Decision: act-in-place
- command-in-item-str-replace: a `str_replace` is not judged by command-in-item, so one on `steps` would pass if the tool takes it on a list field (unmeasured) (hooks/question_form.py item_hit). Decision: act-in-place
- gh-login-str-replace-alone: a `str_replace` on `command` is judged on `new_str` alone. A new gh line after a switch in the stored text is refused; one retry puts the switch in `new_str` (hooks/question_form.py item_hit). Decision: act-in-place
- gh-login-unquoted-backslash: a PowerShell `str_replace` new_str with an unquoted trailing backslash (`Set-Location C:\repo\`) reads as a bash line continuation, so the switch line after it is unseen and the block is refused. One retry quotes the path (hooks/question_form.py gh_login_hit). Decision: act-in-place
- gh-login-str-replace-subfield: a `str_replace` on `command.mac` or `command.powershell`, if the tool accepts one, skips gh-login-switch (unmeasured) (hooks/question_form.py item_hit). Decision: act-in-place
- gh-login-ps-block: in powershell, a gh command inside { } (foreach, try, ForEach-Object, & { }) with no switch passes. The reader does not see into PowerShell script blocks (hooks/question_form.py _gh_script_hit). Decision: act-in-place
- gh-login-substitution: in mac, gh inside an assignment substitution (`n=$(gh pr list)`), backticks or `bash -c "gh ..."` with no switch passes (hooks/question_form.py _gh_script_hit). Decision: act-in-place
- press-link-title-context: when the steps name no page, the task title is the page. A title that names a PR then makes "Press Copy" a press, refused until a link is added (hooks/question_form.py press_link_hit). Decision: act-in-place
- press-link-unlabelled-link: the guard counts a link with a url and no label, which the page does not draw (hooks/question_form.py press_link_hit, skills/waiting-page/page.html isLink). Decision: act-in-place
- ios-probe-diag-false-gap: the `test diagnostics` item gives a false gap on a flag in a Make variable (`$(FLAGS)`) or a quoted `"never"`. An echo or a step name that names `xcodebuild test` also gives one, as in `slow tests` (lint/compliance_probes.py probe_ios).
- ios-probe-diag-misses: the `test diagnostics` item misses `$(XCODEBUILD) test`, a Makefile in a subfolder and a script with no `.sh` suffix (lint/compliance_probes.py probe_ios).
- splice-unclosed-script-drops-block: an unclosed `<script>` before the roadmap block makes the scan stop, so `--carry` reads the live page as having no block and drops it with exit 0. It needs a malformed live page (skills/waiting-page/splice.py scan). Step: c136.
- send-bar-covers-rail: the fixed "Send answers" bar shows on the Status tab. When the page is scrolled, it can cover the rail's second row (skills/waiting-page/page.html `.bar`). Step: c136.
- rail-one-row-clips-count: at a rail box of 640 to 647 px, the one-row rail clips the "0/1" count of the current phase. It happens only with the DejaVu Sans fallback font, with Organize current. Fix idea: keep the name and count stacked up to box 692, and sweep the rail test to 760. Step: c136.
- write-refusal-keeps-old-out: when `stale_pages.py --write` refuses (exit 2), a file already at OUT stays, so a batch that reuses one OUT path could republish the last page's file. It needs a malformed live page (skills/waiting-page/stale_pages.py single_page; SKILL.md "Update a repo's pages"). Step: c136.
- roadmap-update-republishes-live-code: "Update the roadmap block" splices into the live page and republishes its own code, not main's `<out>`. Both blocks survive, and the next wake moves the page to main's code (skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md). Step: c136.
- batch-check-reads-designdata-only: after a batch republish, the SKILL's check compares only the designData block, so a lost roadmap block passes it. `--write` now carries both blocks (skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md "Update a repo's pages"). Step: c136.
- task-names-check-line-not-truth: the guard sees the Why yours line, not whether its check is true, so a task with a false check passes (hooks/question_form.py task_check_hit; decision task-names-its-check). Fix: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing decision), part (v).
- task-names-check-update-no-kind: an update that carries `context` but no `kind` is not judged, since the guard cannot see the stored kind (hooks/question_form.py task_check_hit; decision task-names-its-check)
- task-names-check-line-start-only: a Why yours line in a list or in bold ("- Why yours:", "**Why yours:**") is refused; the refusal names the form, so one retry fixes it (hooks/question_form.py WHY_YOURS_LINE; decision task-names-its-check)
- task-names-check-old-task-update: a whole-item update of a task written before the rule is refused when its context has no Why yours line; a status-only update closes it (hooks/question_form.py task_check_hit; decision task-names-its-check)
- task-names-check-file-path-no-cwd-join: `file_path` data is read with no cwd join, and an unreadable file passes (hooks/question_form.py _write_data; decision task-names-its-check)
- task-check-reason-only-removal: a context str_replace that removes only the Why yours reason passes, since the guard cannot read the stored line (hooks/question_form.py task_str_replace_hit). Decision: task-names-its-check. Fix: none — needs the stored item; a blank-line rule refused plain prose edits.
- owner-judge-line-not-truth: the guard sees the Problem line, not whether its stake is true, so a decision with a false stake passes (hooks/question_form.py decision_form_hit; decision owner-judge-test)
- owner-judge-update-no-kind: an update that carries `context` but no `kind` is not judged, since the guard cannot see the stored kind (hooks/question_form.py decision_form_hit; decision owner-judge-test)
- owner-judge-old-decision-update: a whole-item update of an open decision written before the rule is refused when its context has no Problem line; a status-only update closes it (hooks/question_form.py decision_form_hit; decision owner-judge-test)
- owner-judge-line-form: a Problem line in a list or in bold is refused, and so is a stake with a colon or a plural ("risk:", "risks"). The refusal names the form, so one retry fixes it (hooks/question_form.py PROBLEM_LINE; decision owner-judge-test)
- owner-judge-stands-unchecked: `stands: true` passes with no `pick` or with a `group`; the page then shows it as an ordinary question (page.html standsOk) (hooks/question_form.py decision_form_hit; decision owner-judge-test)
- option-letter-keys-limit-26: more than 26 options always refuse, since the page repeats its letters after Z and the key then repeats (hooks/question_form.py option_letter_hit; decision option-keys-are-letters)
- option-letter-keys-text-by-shape: item text names an option by a letter-and-digit key after option, pick or key; an option named by a word or another shape passes. "Pick _E2_", "~E2~", "option #E2" and "options a, E2" pass too (hooks/question_form.py KEY_NAMED; decision option-keys-are-letters)
- rework-off-page-phrase-only: only "being reworked", "being re-worked", "under rework" and "under re-work" are read; "under revision" and the like pass (hooks/question_form.py REWORKED; decision rework-leaves-page)
- rework-off-page-history-text: "was being reworked" history text is refused; accepted (hooks/question_form.py REWORKED; decision rework-leaves-page)
- option-letter-keys-id-shape: an id or cell with a key's shape after option, pick or key is refused, such as c188, key d4, p1 or A1. Prose such as "key Q4" or "option v1.2" is refused too. Only D<digits> and t<digits> pass (hooks/question_form.py KEY_NAMED). Fix: none — owner decides: main refuses "key Q4" and "key d4" by one pattern, and the note wants d4 refused. Decision: option-keys-are-letters
- text-rules-str-replace-unseen: a str_replace that adds a key-named option or "being reworked" is refused even on a closed item, a task or a note, since a str_replace carries no status or kind (hooks/question_form.py item_hit; decisions option-keys-are-letters, rework-leaves-page)
- cloud-tarball-curl-no-cap: after a cloud clone fails at its 40 s cap, the tarball fallback's `curl -fsSL` has no `--max-time`. The run can then pass the hook's 60 s cap. Files land whole, so the old install stays (install.sh tarball fallback).
- cloud-mirror-sha-write-fails: if the `claude-settings.mirrorSha` write fails after a good checkout, the log says "could not refresh" though main moved, and later resumes warn of local commits and keep main (install.sh cloud resume).
- checkout-at-raw-origin: `checkout_at` reads the raw origin URL, so an alias origin such as `gh:owner/repo` with insteadOf no longer matches in a piped local install, which then fetches the tarball (install.sh checkout_at).
- cloud-mirror-skip-not-cloud-only: the freshness skip reads only the cloudMirror marker, so a marked clone stays silent on staleness when the refresh did not run. Main's tarball copy had no check either (hooks/session_start.sh).
- cloud-swap-old-left: a run killed between the two `mv` calls of the clone swap leaves `claude-settings.old.<pid>`, which the stale sweep (`claude-settings.clone.*` only) never removes (install.sh cloud clone).
- open-box-no-wake-to-page: a session in an open question box with nothing running gets no wake, so its question reaches the page only once the owner answers. Decision: questions-go-to-owner
- waiting-page-gutter-cancel-keyboard-untested: no test covers the keyboard-click pass (`detail === 0`) of the picture's scrollbar-press cancel; removing it leaves the suite green (skills/waiting-page/page.html optPic). Spec: docs/design/c204-sizes.md item 4
- waiting-page-gutter-cancel-script-click: a left scrollbar press with no click keeps the cancel armed. Then a scripted or assistive-tech click with `detail` > 0 and no pointerdown is swallowed; a mouse or touch cannot make one (skills/waiting-page/page.html optPic). Spec: docs/design/c204-sizes.md item 4
- step-grant-revoke-stale: a grant that triage removes on origin/roadmap still lifts the Opus spawn refusal in a lane that has not fetched since (hooks/override.py _take_step). Decision: agent-model-cap
- step-grant-one-bad-entry: one malformed entry in a step's `grants` blocks every grant on that step, a fail-safe false ask (hooks/override.py _take_step). Decision: agent-model-cap
- probe-design-screens-by-route-count: lint/compliance_probes.py probe_design grades "screens named" by route count, so a shoot.json with one route passes when an app has three views.
- glossary-prepared-cache-by-identity: check_glossary `_PREPARED` caches by table identity and length, so an in-place edit of a table that keeps its length reads stale names. Production builds the table once (lint/check_glossary.py prepared).
- words-deadline-late-by-one-scan: WORDS_DEADLINE is checked between files and inside name_problems, not inside one file's scan, so a stop can run late by one scan: 23 s on a 42 MB JSON (lint/compliance_probes.py probe_words).
- ios-probe-quoted-name-with-space: the iOS probe reads quote marks as nothing, so `-scheme "Tally UI"` reads as `Tally`; when `Tally` is a unit-only scheme, a UI run passes. The starter bars spaces in names, so only a legacy repo hits it (lint/compliance_probes.py xcodebuild_runs).
- ios-probe-quoted-separator: a quoted `;`, `|` or `&` inside an xcodebuild argument splits the command, so `-destination "a;b" test` drops `test`. No xcodebuild flag takes one (lint/compliance_probes.py xcodebuild_runs).
- ios-probe-quoted-substitution-open: a lone `$(` in quotes, as in `-derivedDataPath '$(' test`, opens a group and drops `test`. Fix: none — no xcodebuild argument holds a lone `$(`, and quoted text must stay read as commands for `bash -c '...'` (lint/compliance_probes.py xcodebuild_runs).
- session-home-unknown-dir-write: under a lost session-home record, `cd "$WT" && echo x > f` is refused while `cd "$WT" && git commit` passes; an unknown folder counts as the primary for shell writes only (hooks/guard.py session_home_hit). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop
- session-home-agent-named-lane: a worktree named `agent-<x>` by EnterWorktree is read as an agent tree, so the session gets no record and stays unguarded (hooks/guard.py session_home_agent_tree). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop
- session-home-worktrees-folder: a git verb run from `<primary>/.claude/worktrees` itself, outside any lane, acts on the primary and passes (hooks/guard.py session_home_hit `in_primary`). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop
- merge-role-skips-raw-merge: raw `gh pr merge` (permission-allowed) and the GitHub MCP merge tool skip the merge role check (hooks/guard_merge.py). Decision: session-tiers.
- merge-remedy-hits-role-refusal: the remedy text of hooks/guard_merge.py (`merge <pr> --confirm`) now sends a cloud lane into the merge role refusal (hooks/guard_merge.py). Decision: session-tiers.
- card-scroll-margin-no-safe-area: .card, .tier and .bundle scroll-margin-top is 64px with no top safe-area inset, while .hdr sits at that inset (skills/waiting-page/page.html)
- ste-changed-no-checkout-msg: when the installed hooks lack guard_pointer.py, ste-changed swallows the ImportError and says no checkout was found, blaming the pointer, not the missing module (bin/ste-changed find_root). Decision: action-pins-follow-sessions
- ste-changed-root-order-untested: the install cases in actions/ste-lint/test_action.py cannot tell own-checkout-first from pointer-first, since both checkouts hold the same history; they also add a third test-temp sweep proof. Decision: action-pins-follow-sessions
- shoot-seed-fail-open: when the localStorage write throws, the shot runs unseeded and can pass on the wrong screen; Chromium's top frame on http(s) does not throw (bin/shoot seedStorage). Decision: shoot-is-one-shared-command
- shoot-loading-race-2s: the "no steps holds the loading screen" fixture swaps content 2 s after load, so a runner that stalls longer turns it red with no defect (lint/test_shoot.mjs). Decision: shoot-is-one-shared-command
- shoot-own-context-newpage: a seeded or stepped route's own context stays open until browser close when newPage throws; the run aborts with exit 2, so nothing leaks (bin/shoot main). Decision: shoot-is-one-shared-command
- install-move-mid-operation: install.sh moves a clean, detached checkout even with a bisect, rebase or cherry-pick stopped on it; skip when rebase-merge, rebase-apply, BISECT_LOG, CHERRY_PICK_HEAD, REVERT_HEAD or MERGE_HEAD is present (install.sh). Decision: session-tiers.
- install-move-status-exit: the checkout move reads `git status --porcelain` with its exit code ignored, so a failing status reads as clean (install.sh). Decision: session-tiers.
- install-move-ignored-file: the checkout move overwrites an ignored local file when main tracks that path (install.sh). Decision: session-tiers.
- install-move-test-gaps: caseM1-M4 have no shallow detached checkout, no failed-fetch case and no local-mode case (hooks/test_install_src.sh). Decision: session-tiers.
- win-rest-runner-hang: batch 23 #840 checks-windows-rest: the Windows runner stopped mid-job twice (no log, step timeout never fired); same head passed on re-run; cause outside repo code, unmeasured.
- layout-check-deleted-cwd: lint/check_layout.py with no --root and a deleted cwd (a removed worktree) prints a Traceback, exit 1, not its one refusal line; it never passes. Decision: one-layout-every-repo.
- layout-test-worktree-prune: lint/test_layout.py Identity runs `git worktree prune` on the real repo's git folder, which also drops stale worktree records of other sessions; unmeasured race. Decision: one-layout-every-repo.
- wp-fit-w-scan-equal-value: the FIT_W restatement scan (skills/waiting-page/tests/page.test.mjs) flags an unrelated number equal to FIT_W. Its message gives no remedy for that case. 0 hits at 390, 2-43 at other common widths (measured). Decision: none.
- feedback-guard-agent-burst: hooks/feedback_guard.py classes an agent hand-back as an owner burst and blocks Stop until it gets an item line (two blocks in lane wp-fit-w-one-home). Fix: none — rare and harmless: main already skips hand-back prompts (hooks/local_time.py:205), and the incident prompt is not in the repo. Decision: none.
- wp-more-context-focus: the "More context" button on a waiting-page card has no id, so Enter drops focus to the page body on repaint (the reply button got `b-morerp-<id>` for the same defect). Decision: item-field-rendered.
- wp-field-warning-margin: the unknown-field disclosure's `<p>` keeps browser margins; `.status .cmd-more p { margin: 0 }` would tighten it. Decision: item-field-rendered.
- wp-field-list-template-only: the item-field-rendered guard reads the template page.html, not the live page; an empty or dropped block silently stops it (CI's real-page case goes red). Decision: item-field-rendered.
- chat-press-scan-after-lose: the chat press scan strips from `Gain:` to the end of the description, so a press written after `Lose:` passes with no link (hooks/question_form.py). Decision: question-form.
- mockup-replacestate-sandbox: nine docs/design mockups (c135-multi, c190-option-links, c190-task-command, c193-pics, c193-rail, c193-receipt, c193-sent, one-page, waiting-page) call history.replaceState with no catch. It throws in the card's sandboxed srcdoc frame, so a design with a `state` draws nothing there. Fixed in c204-scroll only (lane c204-pics). Decision: none.
- role-handoff-watch-none-wording: role-handoff.md should say `Watch: none` only when the role owns no page; PR #858 (triage watches its page) allows it whenever `Open items: none` (skills/project-ops/templates/role-handoff.md). Decision: none.
- op-model-hand-off-file-pinned: operating-model.md keeps the spelling "hand-off file" because skills/project-ops/tests/test_scaffold.py pins it; renaming it to "handoff" needs those pins moved in the same change. Decision: none.
- report-guard-model-name-next: lint/report_guard.py flags an agent named only under Next as a Done item with no model, even as "(builder sonnet)". It fired 4 times in lane handoff-no-copied-lists, all false alarms. Decision: none.
- op-model-wait-with-home: operating-model.md rotation step 3 says the file names each wait it handed over. A wait with a home, such as CI on a PR, can read as a copy. Decision: none.
- none-direction-note-only-answer: `is_reask` still reads a task answer that is a note with no `done` (skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md, `answers/<id>`) written with new `options` as a re-ask, so the page judge gets the none rule there. Kept: the fix covers a `done` answer only (hooks/call_judge.py). Decision: question-reviewer-agent-hook
- check-handoff-id-letter-prefix: skills/project-ops/scripts/check_handoff.py passes `<URL> A12, B14` and `<URL> #A12`, since its ID pattern needs no word character before the digits. Decision: none.
- check-handoff-bracket-after-url: skills/project-ops/scripts/check_handoff.py checks brackets only in the first URL, so `<URL> <placeholder>` passes where main refused it. A real home is still named. Decision: none.
- wp-ruling-draft-note: skills/waiting-page/page.html setDraft merges, so an unsent note typed while an item was open joins its overturn once it is a ruling; the ruling card shows no note box. Found by reading, not run. Decision: session-tiers.
- wp-rulings-never-leave: a closed arbiter ruling stays on the waiting page for good, so the "Arbiter rulings" section grows on long runs. Decision: session-tiers.
- designer-steps-test-one-sentence: hooks/test_blind_brief.py DesignerSteps wants the critique order as one sentence with `run`, `impeccable` and `critique`. A split, "invoke" for "run", or the order joined to the blind-lane Setup skip goes red, with a clear message. Decision: design-skills.
- frontend-design-cloud-missing: the `frontend-design` plugin is on in settings.json but not installed in cloud containers, so the Skill tool does not know it. Designers and builders read its SKILL.md file from the marketplace folder (lane d4-designer-critique). Decision: design-skills.
- designer-worktree-auto-removed: a designer that writes only to the scratchpad leaves its worktree unchanged, so the harness removes it at the end of the run. Guard `worktree-home` then refuses a resume, and each fix round needs a fresh designer (lane d4-designer-critique). Decision: none.
- blind-lane-on-redraw: the blind-brief hook refuses a directed designer with no `Blind lanes:` line, also on a redraw of a concept the owner picked. The lane must add a blind lane or quote the owner (lane d4-designer-critique). Decision: none.
- scratchpad-shared-across-lanes: designer lanes of one session share its scratchpad, so a blind lane saw the directed lane's contrast script with its colours. It did not use them (lane d4-designer-critique). Decision: none.
- impeccable-launcher-refused: guard `impeccable-review-only` refuses the impeccable launcher and detector. So the designer's critique runs in one context with no detector (lane d4-designer-critique). Decision: design-skills.
- wp-field-list-arbiter-unpinned: no test pins `arbiter` or `ruled` in the itemFields block (hooks/test_guard.py IF_REQUIRED, page.test.mjs field check), and the ruling card never warns on an unknown field. Decision: item-field-rendered.
- reviewer-model-claim: two lane reviewers reported themselves as haiku, though the spawn calls set model sonnet (Oct 30; lane coord-other-repo-compliance was round 1, so the frontmatter model may have won; lane wp-fit-w-one-home round unknown); whether the spawn's model held is unverified. Decision: agent-model-cap.
- page-write-without-n: the feedback guard counts a writer's ArtifactData write as a page send only when it holds the item's `n`. A write by `file_path`, or a close `update items/<id> {"status":"closed"}`, carries no `n` and false-blocks a writer session (lane page-answer-send, round 3). Decision: owner-feedback-inbox.
- page-queued-shape-assumed: the guard anchors an owner message queued mid-turn on its `queued_command` record. That shape was read only from task notices; an owner-typed one is not yet seen, and no case covers a skipped queued notice (lane page-answer-send). Decision: owner-feedback-inbox.
- page-block-no-closed-hint: the block reason for a `[page]` item with no send does not name `[closed]` as the way out when no send is possible, such as a dead writer (lane page-answer-send). Decision: owner-feedback-inbox.
- still-after-tap-cannot-catch-scroll: the 8 stillAfterTap cases fire DOM clicks, and no page code scrolls on a tap, so they catch a page-code scroll only and never a native focus scroll (skills/waiting-page/tests/page.test.mjs)
- send-bar-tall-covers-note: for 20 s after a Send, the send bar is 221px tall at 390 wide and 165px at 1280 (closeTimer). Scroll-padding-bottom stays 96px, so a focused note can sit under the bar. This predates lane note-always-open (skills/waiting-page/page.html)
- near-cards-comments-stale: the NEAR_CARDS cases name a 64px option scroll margin and a header gap guard that the page lacks, so their 10px cases pass trivially (skills/waiting-page/tests/page.test.mjs)
- nao-builder-ran-opus: in lane note-always-open, the round-2 builder ran opus. The model table sets sonnet for round 2 and opus only on a third try, and the brief named no model (PR #872)
- stale-step-partial-walk: the roadmap stale-step row cannot tell a one-page `list_sessions` copy from a full walk (`has_more` is true after 30 rows), so lanes on later pages read stale; SKILL.md says "every page". A fix needs a completeness field in the live file (lane roadmap-matches-lanes, review round 3). Decision: none.
- merge-role-proof-crash-fails-open: a crash in judge() fails open (hooks/guard.py main), so with the exact `merge --role` allow rules a crash in role_proof_hit leaves no other judge on the call (hooks/guard_merge.py). It becomes a debt after a real incident. Decision: session-tiers.
- merge-role-proof-trusts-tag: guard rule `merge-role-proof` trusts the session's cs-build tag, so a tag set by mistake, with set_session_tags or copied at spawn, gives the role (hooks/guard_merge.py role_proof_hit). Decision: session-tiers.
- dispatch-head-blocks-batch: `batch_running` in merge/merge.py counts a ready PR whose head has only a skipped `all-jobs-passed` (a dispatch-only head) as a blocker for every other merge. Needs a ready PR with no pull_request run on its head (lane all-jobs-passed-rollup, review round 2). Decision: main-branch-protection.
- same-name-check-order-unmeasured: a dispatch run on a PR head posts a skipped `all-jobs-passed` beside an earlier red one. merge.py reads red; how GitHub's own protection orders two same-name check runs is unmeasured (lane all-jobs-passed-rollup). Decision: main-branch-protection.
- head-read-dispatch-remedy: `head_read` in merge/merge.py calls `classify` with no required names, so the guard's detail for a draft head still names `gh workflow run`, which skips `all-jobs-passed`. The merge tool then prints the right fix (lane all-jobs-passed-rollup, review round 3). Decision: main-branch-protection.
- draft-skip-check-copy: `DRAFT_SKIP_CHECK` in merge/merge.py copies the job name `all-jobs-passed` that the lint tests hold. The merge tool serves many repos, so it cannot read the name from this repo's workflow (lane all-jobs-passed-rollup). Decision: main-branch-protection.
- question-form-goal-not-first: prose before the Goal line passes, because only the order of the three form lines is checked (hooks/question_form.py). Decision: question-form.
- ci-scope-question-form-unpinned: lint/test_ci_scope.py pins git_reads.py in the guard arm, but not question_form.py or precompact_handoff.py. Decision: question-form.
- question-form-import-fails-open: an import defect in hooks/question_form.py or hooks/precompact_handoff.py turns off the six page rules (guard-crash) and the whole chat judge. Decision: question-form.
- command-in-item-str-replace: an `ArtifactData` str_replace on a task skips guard rule `command-in-item`, so a one-string `command` can still be written that way (lane command-per-os). Decision: act-in-place.
- command-scan-lone-backtick: a lone backtick earlier in a step pairs with the opening backtick of a real span, so the command in that span passes (lane command-per-os). Decision: act-in-place.
- command-scan-plain-text: a command in plain step text, a `$ ` prompt span, an indented block or a `~~~` fence passes the step scan (lane command-per-os). Decision: act-in-place.
- command-scan-false-alarms: a backticked `Read-Only`, `Actions | Workflows` or `ok; ship it` reads as a shell line. A reword clears each (lane command-per-os). Decision: act-in-place.
- command-null-refused: `command: null` on an update is refused, so a session clears a command by writing both shells again (lane command-per-os). Decision: act-in-place.
- command-steps-one-string: task `steps` given as one string skip the step scan, as on main, and the page cannot draw them (lane command-per-os). Decision: act-in-place.
- mac-holds-powershell: a `mac` string that holds a PowerShell cmdlet passes (lane command-per-os). Decision: act-in-place.
- owner-message-grant-variant: the classifier reads "already granted" by wording, so a misread task can ask an owner session for a variant of a granted act. The target keeps its own permissions. Decision: auto-mode-owner-acts.
- owner-message-default-priority: whether `send_message` with no `priority` interrupts the target's running turn is unverified. Decision: auto-mode-owner-acts.
- owner-pr-close-any-author: the PR bound is the `shivinate7` repo, not the PR author, so a contributor's named PR can be closed. A reopen undoes it. Decision: auto-mode-owner-acts.
- auto-mode-entries-unrun: the tests pin entry text only. No classifier run shows an incident allowed or a "Never covers" form refused. Decision: auto-mode-owner-acts.
- role-model-late-round-line: in guard rule agent-role-model, any line that starts `Round 2 of 3` or `Round 3 of 3` lets a reviewer run Sonnet, even inside a round 1 brief that quotes a later round. Decision: agent-model-cap.
- role-model-env-any-value: a spawn with no model passes when `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL` holds any value, opus too. The guard passes an Explore that names no model whenever the variable is set. `agents/explore.md` pins haiku, so that Explore runs haiku. An env write already asks. Decision: agent-model-cap.
- role-model-qualifier-copies: `EXPLORE_AUDIT_MARKER` (in hooks/shared_names.py) and `ROLE_REVIEWER_FREE_ROUNDS` (in hooks/guard.py) copy the meaning of the audit-Explore and reviewer round 2-3 table rows; they are not read from the table. Decision: agent-model-cap.
- role-model-unranked-reviewer-clause: in guard rule agent-role-model, the `named < top` clause on the reviewer round 2-3 exemption has no test and no mutant; step-up already denies the plain spelling. Decision: agent-model-cap.
- role-model-unranked-opus-row: an unranked model such as `fable` on Plan, designer or design-reviewer passes with no ask, as on main; the record and tests do not say so. Decision: agent-model-cap.
- role-model-spelling-other-rules: the row check and the Opus refusal's Explore `Audit:` grant fold role case and space; the haiku floor, the step-up and Opus refusal's other grants still match the role exactly. Decision: agent-model-cap.
