- role-model-unranked-branch-no-mutant: the unranked-model branch of guard rule agent-role-model has no mutant in hooks/mutate_guard.py; the suite still catches its removal (3 red cases). Decision: agent-model-cap.
- map-uses-bare-name-any-import: a bare name counts as a use when the cited file is imported for side effects only, so `import {notes} from "./other"; import "./notes"; notes` passes `uses ...:notes`, in JS and Python (skills/plain-english-map/scripts/lib/fact-readers.mjs). Decision: map-facts-are-checked.
- map-uses-py-deep-chain: Python counts `retry.cfg.X` as a use of `X` from retry, but JS rejects `m.other.notes` (skills/plain-english-map/scripts/lib/py-facts.py). Decision: map-facts-are-checked.
- map-uses-renamed-import: a renamed import (`from retry import X as Z`, `{ notes as n }`) reads "never uses" (2 of 681 uses in this repo). Decision: map-facts-are-checked.
- map-uses-reexport-python: a Python re-export (hooks/guard_core.py re-exports 22 git_reads names) reads "never uses", while JS `export {x} from` reads as a use. Decision: map-facts-are-checked.
- map-blankshell-limits-unstated: blankShell reads `let y=1<<2` as a heredoc and never ends a CRLF heredoc (`EOF\r`), so the rest of the file reads gone. An unclosed `((` stops heredoc reading, and a quote inside `$()` in a double-quoted string ends that string. None of these is in the code or the decision's Limits section (skills/plain-english-map/scripts/lib/source-text.mjs). Decision: map-facts-are-checked.
- map-js-uses-limits-unstated: `require(x).sym`, `{ default as lib }`, `ns["x"]` and a shadowed alias are not read, and the code and the decision do not say so (skills/plain-english-map/scripts/lib/fact-readers.mjs). Decision: map-facts-are-checked.
- map-blankshell-paths-untested: no test case covers `<<-`, a quoted heredoc word, two heredocs on one line, `$'\''`, or `#` after `;&|()<>`, all proved by probe only. The test names at skills/plain-english-map/tests/fact-lines.test.mjs R1 and R2 say "today", which goes stale. Decision: map-facts-are-checked.
- map-pin-two-senses: "pins the install link" and the quoted "pinned copies" use "pin" in two senses; "links the skill, never copies it" reads plainer (docs/decisions/map-skill-lives-here.md, three debt glosses). Decision: map-skill-lives-here.
- map-installer-copies-unstated: cloud and refused-symlink installs still make a copy, and skills/plain-english-map/SKILL.md treats that copy as supported; the record does not say such copies are installer-made and belong to debt skill-copy-no-typescript. Decision: map-skill-lives-here.
- map-older-copy-means-version: skills/plain-english-map/SKILL.md says "an older copy of the skill" where it means an older version. Decision: map-skill-lives-here.
- question-departs-edges: `lint/report_guard.py` LABEL_RE skips a bold "**Departs from:**" line, so one outside Input Needed can give a Done item its PR ref or sit before Done. A change side of only "<what changes>." with a period passes. A `str_replace` with no label in old or new is not judged. A label in mid-line is refused. Decision: port-keeps-behaviour.
- trust-card-in-build-same-message-order: a `get_session` and a `set_session_tags` in one message can be read in the wrong order, so the guard misjudges the tags. Decision: session-tiers.
- trust-card-in-build-denial-no-cause: a fail-closed denial logs no cause in guard.log, so the reason is lost. Decision: session-tiers.
- set-waiting-page-usage-exit: `layout.py set-waiting-page` with the wrong number of arguments exits 1, the code that also means "already set". Decision: session-tiers.
- set-waiting-page-new-root: a mistyped root makes `<root>/.claude/layout-extras.json` in a new folder and exits 0. Decision: session-tiers.
- record-branch-null-stops: `record-branch: null` stops the roadmap build, while `waiting-page: null` counts as unset. Decision: session-tiers.
- reap-log-done-by-margin: janitor/agent_end_reap.py LOG_DONE_BY 58 leaves 2 s, but the hook's 60 s timer starts before `started` (shell, interpreter start, stdin read); 55 was proposed. Decision: c95.
- log-lock-retries-every-oserror: _log_lock retries on every OSError, so a non-contention error (ENOLCK, EOPNOTSUPP) waits to the deadline; a contention-only retry was proposed but its Windows errno pair (13, 36) for msvcrt.locking is unverified. Decision: c95.
- command-file-backticked: a `command` with both shells excuses "Run the command in `plans/c152.md`", because the file name sits in backticks (lane command-per-os). Decision: act-in-place.
- command-run-verb-script-path: a run verb earlier in the sentence makes a script path such as `./hooks/guard.py` read as a shell line, as in "Run the tests and open `./hooks/guard.py`" (lane command-per-os). Decision: act-in-place.
- test-git-ps1-twin: the PowerShell suites (install.test.ps1 and the install-* .ps1 tests) commit in fixtures with no lint/_test_git twin. They run on Windows CI, not on the cloud signer. Step: test-runs-skip-signing.
- test-git-bad-count-reset: lint/_test_git.py/.sh/.mjs reset a non-numeric GIT_CONFIG_COUNT to 0 and drop the preset keys silently. Step: test-runs-skip-signing.
- page-title-scan-edges: `splice.title_span` takes the last markup `<title>` outside an svg, so a later markup title wins, and a self-closing or unclosed `<svg` before it gives no title (carry keeps the template's; stale reads behind). page.html has one markup title, so no real page reaches either (lane page-name-queue). Decision: none.
- c204-clamp-untested: in `fitFrame` the scroll width clamp `pw = min(ceil(bw × sc), room)` has no test that goes red without it. Float error makes ceil pass the room by 1px only at rooms such as 202, 203, 214 and 215, which no tested width reaches (lane c204-scroll-width, review round 2). Decision: none.
- standing-grant-local-unenforced: merge/merge.py validates `merge.standingGrant` on every run but enforces it only in a cloud session. A local session's `merge <pr> --confirm` merges with the switch false. There the tool cannot tell the owner from a session. The owner must stay able to merge the PR that turns the switch back on. Decision: standing-merge-grant (the merge grant and the merge tool)
- merge-put-bypass: no guard refuses a direct `gh api -X PUT .../pulls/<n>/merge`, and the cloud proxy passes it. That skips the merge tool's role check and its `merge.standingGrant` switch. A debt after an incident. Decision: standing-merge-grant (the merge grant and the merge tool)
- standing-grant-read-once: merge/merge.py reads `merge.standingGrant` once, before the lock. A switch set to false during that run's lock wait or checks wait does not stop the run. Decision: standing-merge-grant (the merge grant and the merge tool)
- cloud-setup-timeout-orphan: when `cloud-setup.sh` hits its 240 s cap, the kill may not reach install.sh's inner `timeout 40 git`. That git can run up to 40 s on, and install.sh's lock may stay, so the next install within 10 minutes fails. Found by code reading, never run (lane setup-script-install, review round 1). Decision: agent-model-cap.
- cloud-setup-origin-copy: `origin_is_repo` in cloud-setup.sh is a looser copy of install.sh's `origin_matches_repo`, because install.sh runs on load and cannot be sourced. It differs only on contrived URLs, and no test pins the two together (lane setup-script-install). Decision: agent-model-cap.
- cloud-setup-zombie-step: before its group kill, cloud-setup.sh first sends TERM to each child of the install, then waits 1 s. The step is there for test c2 only, because this sandbox's pid 1 never clears dead children, so a dead child still looks alive. The better fix is for c2 to count a dead child (state Z) as gone, and then drop the step (lane setup-script-install, review round 2). Decision: agent-model-cap.
- holder-slash-unpinned: nothing pins "no / in HOLDER" (janitor/test_session_end_sweep.py); a later "/" breaks Windows only, under MSYS path rewriting, while Linux stays green. Decision: c95.
- sweep-no-pipes-kwargs-only: SweepGetsNoPipes pins the DEVNULL kwargs; no case has a grandchild holding a pipe, so a hang by another route passes unseen. A Windows-only grandchild case would make it red-capable. Decision: c95.
- heartbeat-starved-reads-stopped: a live holder starved 1 s or more reads as stopped, a false green only when the kill also failed. Decision: c95.
- kill-holder-rekills-dead-pids: kill_holder and the atexit stop_holders kill each pid again, dead or not; a recycled pid inside that seconds-wide window could be killed. Decision: c95.
- scratchpad-reap-reads-ps: ScratchpadReap reads liveness with ps, not the heartbeat; the reap is POSIX-only and needs ps to find orphans. Decision: c95.
- reap-start-row-budget-unbound: janitor/agent_end_reap.py's start row give_up_at (HOOK_BUDGET, 45 s) never binds, because LOG_WAIT_SECONDS (30 s) ends the wait first; the hook-bound case tests a clamp production never reaches. The 60 s bound holds through the done row. Decision: c95.
- hook-bound-case-1s-slack: janitor/test_sweep.py's hook-bound case allows 4.0 s on an expected 3.0 s; a stall over 1 s in main() on a loaded runner reads red. Decision: c95.
- lock1sh-first-holder-unproven: hooks/test_install_src.sh lock1sh does not prove install A took the lock first; under load B can go first and the case passes uncontested. Decision: c95.
- win-lk-lock-regression-uncovered: on Windows the holder holds 5 s, under LK_LOCK's own 10 s give-up, so only the POSIX stand-in can catch an LK_LOCK regression. Decision: c95.
- sendmessage-state-skips-at-cap: hooks/test_sendmessage_state.py still skips (NOT-RUN) when a slow runner reaches its harness cap; the step's goal says never skipped. Decision: c95.
- ci-job-no-pointer-unknown: a copied install with no pointer checkout reads a remote code-check step unknown, not FAIL; exit 2 still blocks a pass (bin/ci-job code_check).
- ci-job-interrupt-leaves-verdict: Ctrl-C during a step leaves bin/verdict and the step's shell running; the step's children outlived it on main too (bin/ci-job heartbeat relay).
- ci-job-relay-locale-decode: the heartbeat relay reads verdict's UTF-8 output in the locale's text mode, so a non-cp1252 byte on Windows can raise mid-step (bin/ci-job).
- ci-job-local-action-other-path: LOCAL_ACTION matches only `$/actions/` and `./actions/`, so a local action at another path reads as remote and borrows a copy; same on main (bin/ci-job).
- test-ci-job-pipx-probe-windows: the pipx probe's 60 s timeout on Windows can wait on a pipx child that holds the pipe (lint/test_ci_job.py).
- test-ci-job-archive-head: the in-repo code-check case runs `git archive HEAD`, so a local run tests only committed bin/ci-job edits (lint/test_ci_job.py).
- map-path-hard-coded: skills/plain-english-map and its map-check write and read "docs/map" as a literal, not the layout kind `map` through lint/layout.py. Decision: one-layout-every-repo.
- map-check-double-install: when the mods flag is on, the checks job runs `npm ci` twice on one lockfile, in .github/scripts/map-check.sh and in .github/scripts/mod-tests.sh; `npm ci` wipes node_modules, so the second never skips. Install time in CI is unmeasured. Decision: map-facts-are-checked.
- map-check-install-no-step-cap: the Map check step runs a network install with no step timeout; the 52 min job cap covers it, and the code-check `npm ci` has the same gap. Decision: ci-job-caps.
- draft-step-any-session: outside the cloud no merge-role check runs, so any session may run `merge <pr> --confirm` on a draft PR. That pushes a page-only commit to the draft branch, and no merge follows (review round 1 of PR #895). Decision: records-index-at-merge.
- draft-hint-claim-no-kinds: on a repo whose stamp config has no kinds, a draft head with a hand-written Record-claim trailer gets the preview hint "--confirm on the draft pushes it", but the draft step refuses that head (merge/merge.py, preview and draft_step; review round 3 of PR #895, probed). Decision: records-index-at-merge.
- draft-step-stands-wording: the draft step prints "The green run stands." on a draft, where CI skipped the run. Wording only (review round 3 of PR #895). Decision: records-index-at-merge.
- role-model-layout-import: role_model_table imports lint/layout.py; with no lint/ beside hooks/ the import fails, the guard logs guard-crash and fails open on that Agent spawn. Installs ship lint/. The comment at hooks/mutate_guard.py ~1992 says "exits silently"; it logs guard-crash. Decision: agent-model-cap.
- frozen-redirect-and-word: `echo x >&settings.json` from the config dir writes the file `settings.json` and the frozen-path rule allows it; same on main (hooks/guard_frozen.py).
- frozen-copy-read-amp-redirect: `cp /tmp/x <frozen> &> /dev/null` is allowed because `_copy_reads` reads the bare `&` of `&>` as an operand, so the frozen DEST counts as a source; same on main (hooks/guard_frozen.py).
- test-verdict-temp-half: lint/test_verdict.py uses the run-folder temp standard only in `run()` and the pipe case; its other `TemporaryDirectory()` calls keep the default (review round 1 of PR #927).
- stale-stamped-tree-called-live-pin: session_start.sh calls an old `<clone>-live-<hex>` tree "the live pin" after the link has moved on, though hooks/guard_frozen.py leaves it writable; the cd advice still holds (hooks/session_start.sh)
- pin-line-silent-on-failed-listing: when the worktree listing fails inside a pin, no pin line prints and the unknown line names only the agent count (hooks/session_start.sh)
- pin-name-fifth-copy: install.ps1 also builds `<clone>-live`, which the copy list in install.sh's comment omits (install.sh, install.ps1)
- bg-timeout-no-beat-file: A foreground Bash call that its timeout moves to the background skips `--hook`, so it gets no beat file. Only `--stop` lists it, and nothing strands it. Decision: stall-watch
- merge-progress-stdout-only: merge/merge.py prints its progress line to stdout only, so `merge ... | grep` hides it from the stall watch; no incident (review round 1 of PR #932). Decision: stall-watch
- bare-verdict-double-beat: in a bare `verdict x` background job each heartbeat shows twice in the output (stderr via LOG, and the beat relay); cost one extra line per heartbeat; kept because verdict keeps its stderr line on purpose. Decision: stall-watch
- process-start-lc-all-unpinned: no test covers `LC_ALL=C` on the `ps -o lstart=` call; the fake ps ignores locale, so a mutant that drops it survives; no mutate_guard mutant covers the `env=` line or `calendar.timegm` (hooks/git_reads.py _process_start_ms)
- ps-lstart-tz-macos-unmeasured: unmeasured: does real macOS BSD `ps` honour `TZ=UTC0` for lstart? macOS CI runners run in UTC, so they cannot show a non-UTC read; procps was measured and it does (hooks/git_reads.py _process_start_ms)
- frozen-main-mirror-literal-home: `_in_main_mirror` builds its root from the literal HOME only, so under a symlinked HOME the real-path spelling of ~/claude-settings-main is not frozen. Cloud HOME is not a symlink (hooks/guard_frozen.py). Decision: merge-reinstalls-from-main (the pin follows local main)
- install-cloud-budget-untested: no test drives install.sh's 50 s cloud_git budget or the OWN_CHECKOUT backup note, so deleting either stays green (install.sh). Decision: merge-reinstalls-from-main (the pin follows local main)
- frozen-grep-install-refused: the frozen-path guard counts an unquoted install before a mirror path as a write, so `grep -n install ~/claude-settings-main/install.sh` is refused. The pin and the config folder already behave this way (hooks/guard_frozen.py). Decision: merge-reinstalls-from-main (the pin follows local main)
- mirror-name-copies-unchecked: MAIN_MIRROR_NAME in guard_frozen.py copies the name in install.sh's MAIN_MIRROR, and no check ties the two. LIVE_PIN_SUFFIX has the same older gap with merge/install_from_main.sh (hooks/guard_frozen.py, install.sh). Rule: building-allow-list-is-the-constant (an allow list points at the constant the code emits)
- home-clone-mirror-unfrozen: `$HOME/claude-settings`, made by clone_mirror when no checkout exists, stays unfrozen. Freezing it by name would block the owner's own clone, so only a marker check would work (install.sh). Decision: merge-reinstalls-from-main (the pin follows local main)
- lane-fetch-before-mirror: a clean lane branch ahead of main pays one origin fetch, up to the 40 s cap, before the mirror clone. On a slow link this can leave the mirror clone about 10 s of the 50 s budget (unmeasured). An `ls-remote` tip compare would avoid the fetch (install.sh ~459-462). Decision: merge-reinstalls-from-main (the pin follows local main)
- branch-fetch-keep-comment: the comment at install.sh ~443, "When `$REF` cannot be fetched, the checkout stays the source as before", holds for a detached checkout only. On a branch, a failed fetch keeps the checkout only when HEAD equals the older origin/main record (install.sh ~463-465). Decision: merge-reinstalls-from-main (the pin follows local main)
- noremote-mutant-fixture: in hooks/test_install_src.sh caseM10 noremote, the fixture needs INSTALL_SH to equal the checkout's install.sh. A mutant passed through INSTALL_SH_UNDER_TEST fails as FIXTURE, so a mutant proof must swap the file into the tree. Decision: merge-reinstalls-from-main (the pin follows local main)
- ln-over-directory-no-record: refusal ln-over-directory has no guard record, so the read-the-record duty cannot be met for it; Claude rarely hits it (review round 1 of PR #939) (hooks/guard.py). Decision: guard-that-cries-wolf-is-spent
- live-lock-subject-row-unpinned: the live-lock test checks that the subject keeps the live lock. It does not check that its row reads "locked:", so no row pins the re-read in `_lock_unlockable` (janitor/test_sweep.py; review round 1 of PR #941). Decision: when-a-worktree-may-be-removed
- frozen-path-string-mention: frozen-path refused a Bash python heredoc that wrote only the session handoff file, because the script text named the install mirror path inside a string (Nov 1 07:32 CST; the Edit tool wrote the same change). One case (hooks/guard_frozen.py). Decision: frozen-path-resolves-shell-vars
- impeccable-guard-shell-forms: guard rule impeccable-review-only lets through `sh <launcher> context`, `bash <launcher> live-server`, `detect --json & live-server` and `detect --target $(impeccable live-server)`; Claude rarely writes these forms (review round 1 of lane impeccable-4.8.0) (hooks/guard.py, hooks/guard_core.py). Decision: design-skills
- widens-mark-quote-refused: the chat check refuses a Recommended option that quotes the widening words to reject them, as main did with the old words; Claude rarely writes this (review round 1 of lane widens-card-plain-words) (hooks/question_form.py). Decision: uniform-over-variants
- reply-judge-import-fail-untested: reply_judge.py drops the widening words from its prompt when its import of question_form fails; no test covers that branch, and a probe showed no crash (review round 1 of lane widens-card-plain-words). Decision: uniform-over-variants
- feedback-home-resolves-cwd: the feedback check resolves an inbox home against the cwd repo, so a session whose cwd was banchi saw five claude-settings homes reported as untracked (Nov 1 11:35 CST). Fix idea: resolve against the session repo roots, or let a home name its repo (hooks/feedback_guard.py). Decision: owner-feedback-inbox
- cc-312-plugin-no-autoinstall: at claude-code v2.1.312, v2.1.314 and v2.1.316 the claude-code-plugins marketplace lists frontend-design as a git-subdir of claude-plugins-official at ref main (review round 1 of lane fd-float-cc-newest read marketplace.json at these tags). A headless fresh start in a scratch CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR did not auto-install it ("External plugin frontend-design@claude-code-plugins not in cache, skipping"): 0 of 5 starts at v2.1.316 and 0 of 3 at v2.1.312 (v2.1.313 to v2.1.315 not started). It installed on 1 of 2 starts at v2.1.311 (Nov 1, review of lane fd-float-cc-newest). `claude plugin install` works at v2.1.316. The ref stays held at v2.1.311 (settings.json). Renovate will propose v2.1.312 and later: skip those PRs until a fresh start installs frontend-design. Retire when a fresh start on a newer tag installs frontend-design. Decision: design-skills
- kill-stub-subreaper-foreign: a stranded job whose stub a subreaper adopted (PPID above 1, such as `systemd --user`) reads foreign, so a main-session kill of it stays refused; unmeasured (hooks/guard_kill.py foreign_pid_kill). Decision: a-kill-by-pid-needs-an-owned-pid.
- layout-repeat-unnormalised-path: `lint/layout.py` `kinds()` compares a repeated kind's path with `strip("/")` only, so an extras path such as `"docs//map"` gets the both-paths message instead of the delete remedy. It is still refused. Decision: one-layout-every-repo.
- layout-repeat-test-order: `lint/test_layout.py`'s repeat tests check that each path is in the message, not which path is the layout's, so a swapped message still passes. Decision: one-layout-every-repo.
- explore-audit-marker-word-boundary: the Explore audit marker match in hooks/guard.py (EXPLORE_AUDIT_MARKER) uses `\b` before `Audit:`, so `xAudit:` or `_Audit:` ahead of it is refused. The record says any text ahead counts. The refusal names the marker, so one retry fixes it. Decision: agent-model-cap
- test-guard-new-constant-comment: the comment above EXPLORE_MARKER in hooks/test_guard.py (near line 20008) says the guard constant "is new", which goes stale once that constant merges. Test files need a builder. Decision: agent-model-cap
- page-judge-title-only: an items update that carries neither options nor context, such as a title-only write, is not model-judged, so a bare id in a title passes (same source). Decision: question-form
- page-judge-partial-update: an items update with `context` and no `kind` is judged as a decision even when the stored item is a task. A context-only update may cite ids glossed only in the unseen item, so it may be flagged under the no-unexplained-ids item. The owner override lifts it (review round 3 of lane page-question-judge) (hooks/question_form.py judged_item_writes, hooks/call_judge.py PAGE_PROMPT). Decision: question-form
- page-judge-dead-fragment-fields: _judged_fragment still accepts `options` and `options.N` subfields, which the ArtifactData str_replace schema cannot send (one top-level string key); dead code, harmless (review round 1 of lane page-judge-task-steps). Decision: question-form
- page-judge-mixed-batch: a decision item in a batch with a task item should keep its Goal, Problem and pick checks under PAGE_PROMPT's task exemption. It is unmeasured, because the real-model probe ran single items only (review round 3 of lane page-judge-task-steps). Decision: question-form
- page-judge-task-ids: task steps are now judged on unexplained ids, so a step that names a branch, sha or command may get a false alarm. It is unmeasured, and the owner override lifts it (review round 3 of lane page-judge-task-steps). Decision: question-form
- still-scene-multidraw-ext: rule ux-still-scene-draws-nothing wraps `multiDraw*` on the context prototypes, but those methods live on the `WEBGL_multi_draw` extension object, so a resting three.js BatchedMesh can pass. Fix: also wrap the object `getExtension` returns (docs/design/principles.md). Decision: ux-house-rules
- lost-scene-webgpu-wording: rule ux-lost-scene-recovers says the scene returns after restore, but WebGPU `device.destroy()` is final. The pass for WebGPU is the words only (docs/design/principles.md, checklist.md). Decision: ux-house-rules
- full-height-lvh-grep: rule ux-full-height-fits greps `[0-9]vh`, which skips `lvh`, a unit that acts like `vh` (docs/design/principles.md, checklist.md). Decision: ux-house-rules
- page-design-band-comment: test comments call the 390x56 band "near 1" in scale, but it measures 0.75 (skills/waiting-page/tests/test_check_designs.mjs). Decision: page-item-design
- page-design-stamp-path: the published stamp holds a path, not a copy, so an edit to that file after the publish changes what the next item write is checked against (hooks/guard.py, skills/waiting-page/stale_pages.py). Decision: page-item-design
- page-design-closed-update: an update with no status to a closed item is still checked, though the page never draws closed items (hooks/question_form.py). Decision: page-item-design
- ps1-copy-skill-prune-no-backup: in copy mode, install.ps1 prunes files inside a copied skill in place, while install.sh backs the copy up first (install.ps1 Install-Dir). Decision: copy-mode-overwrites-no-backup
- ps1-copy-skill-prune-no-backup: in copy mode, install.ps1 prunes files inside a copied skill in place, while install.sh backs the copy up first. The copy-mode refresh and the copy-to-link swap also delete an owner's file added inside a shipped skill folder (install.ps1 Install-Dir). Decision: copy-mode-overwrites-no-backup
- install-sh-unchecked-skill-mv: install.sh moves an old skill backup with an unchecked `mv` (install.sh move_old_skill_baks). Decision: install-manifest-prunes
- install-tarball-no-prune: an install from a source with no .git counts every file in the extracted tree as shipped, so a file main removed stays in place (install.sh tarball fallback). Decision: install-manifest-prunes
- install-first-run-rm-no-retry: on the first run with no manifest, a failed delete of a listed link into the clone is not held back. The manifest is then written, and the link is never retried (install.sh retire_listed). Decision: install-manifest-prunes
- install-ps1-nonascii-names: the install.ps1 post-merge hook reads git without -z and the installer decodes -z output with the console encoding, so a non-ASCII shipped name could be skipped. No such name ships now. Decision: install-manifest-prunes
- install-manifest-test-gaps: no test runs install.ps1's manifest code outside CI, none feeds git a failure mid-install, and none uses odd file names. Decision: install-manifest-prunes
- option-letter-keys-label-word-tag: a label that opens with a word tag other than Option, such as "Approach B:" or "Plan B:", passes LABEL_TAG. It is kept out because "Plan B" is plain English (hooks/question_form.py LABEL_TAG; decision option-keys-are-letters)
- report-guard-head-omits-pr: BLOCK_REASON_HEAD says "commit, push, or merge" and leaves out an opened PR, a revert, an update-branch and a file delete, which the guard also checks (lint/report_guard.py). Found in PR #966 round 3.
- report-guard-gh-value-flags: the no-write flag scan skips no value flags, so a create whose title is `-w` is missed and `-fw` reads as landing; reuse PR_CREATE_VALUE_FLAGS (lint/report_guard.py). Decision: guard-that-cries-wolf-is-spent
- report-guard-glued-repo-flag: a glued `-Ro/r` reads repo empty in merge_call_of, so the root form is judged against the cwd repo; no test pins it (hooks/guard_merge.py). Decision: guard-that-cries-wolf-is-spent
- pw-os-release-source-fail: pin.sh sources os-release under `set -eu` with stderr hidden, so a file with shell logic or an unset reference ends the step with no message. Real os-release files hold none (actions/playwright-setup/pin.sh). Decision: one-browser-setup-no-apt
- pw-os-release-env-name: pin.sh's test hook `OS_RELEASE` is a generic env name. A job that sets it for another reason gets `os=Linux` and a warning (actions/playwright-setup/pin.sh). Decision: one-browser-setup-no-apt
- pw-os-test-gaps: test_action.py has no case for an os-release with no ID, and no check that a normal Ubuntu run prints no warning (actions/playwright-setup/test_action.py). Decision: one-browser-setup-no-apt
- pw-26-04-cause-unread: the decision cites `libicudata.so.74` on 26.04 from the joyous lane's report, not a log this lane read (docs/decisions/one-browser-setup-no-apt.md). Decision: one-browser-setup-no-apt
- tracking-turn-mixed-row: the rule_mechanisms row for output-tracking-turn-one-line is kind check for its push case, and its tracking-only case stays judgment in the row's reason; one row holds one kind (lint/rule_mechanisms.json, lint/rule_audit.py JUDGMENT_EXPECTED).
- tracking-turn-git-push-test: that row cites test_67 (push_files) only; Bash `git push` is proven by test_61 and test_64, which the row does not cite (lint/test_report_guard.py).
- page-stale-empty-live: a live page that is itself untouched (placeholder title, empty blocks) makes `--write` build a page `page-stale` refuses, because the guard cannot read the live page. The exit is the title: the refusal tells the session to set the page `<title>` to "<repo> Cockpit" (owner ruling, 2026-11-02 11:12 CST) and republish (hooks/guard.py PAGE_EMPTY_REASON). Decision: page-stale-publish
- roadmap-quoted-push-passes: a push whose words are all quoted (`git push 'origin' 'roadmap'`) passes roadmap-one-writer, because PUSH_QUOTED joins them (hooks/guard_push_delete.py). Main's `force-push` rule has the same gap. Decision: session-tiers.
- own-session-cse-id-form: the own-id match in `_own_session_read` takes the `session_<x>` form only, so a `set_session_tags` call naming the `cse_<x>` form leaves the proof current (hooks/guard_merge.py). Decision: session-tiers.
- guard-comments-stale-wording: the hooks/guard.py comments at the rule catalogue (~line 85, trust-card) and at clause 6a2 (cost) still give the old wording. Fix them in the next lane that edits hooks/guard.py. Decision: session-tiers.
- origin-odd-forms-bogus-link: odd origins give a bogus slug and link: `../sibling`, Azure `_git/repo`, `github.com/o/n/pulls`. No test_build case pins a link for such an origin; its only link case uses a github.com origin (skills/roadmap/test_build.py). Decision: agent-model-cap.
- layout-third-origin-parser: lint/layout.py keeps a third, GitHub-only origin parser (`GITHUB` and `repo_name`). It could call `git_reads.origin_slug`. Decision: agent-model-cap.
- extras-path-literal-unpinned: the extras path literal in hooks/git_reads.py (`LAYOUT_EXTRAS`) repeats lint/layout.json "extras". Pin it in lint/check_layout.py as RECORD_LAYOUT_FILE is pinned. No case covers a one-segment origin (`https://host/r`). Decision: agent-model-cap.
- roll-out-lane-starting-tag: a roll-out lane carries the starting repo's tag beside its target's. The starting roadmap counts it live if its cs-step id matches one of its own steps, and step ids repeat across repos. Unverified. Decision: session-tiers.
- lane-wrong-cs-step-unjudged: a lane tagged with the wrong cs-step is not caught by lane-repo-tag, and a set_session_tags call after the start is not judged either. It may be a cause of the 2026-11-01 18:08 CST false flags (incident in agent-model-cap); unmeasured. Decision: session-tiers.
- page-parked-off-look-untested: no test covers the parked lines' off look (struck label, --faint edge, forced-colors GrayText) or the "from parked <ref>" bar text, so a CSS edit could make an off act look live (skills/waiting-page/page.html, skills/waiting-page/tests/page.test.mjs). Step: page-parked-actions.
- page-parked-badge-race: keepRouted shows the "Unparked" or "Dropped" badge only when the meta/page snapshot arrives before the answers delete; otherwise the line leaves at once. Harmless (skills/waiting-page/page.html). Step: page-parked-actions.
- page-why-noid-copy-wording: WHY_NOID copy could read "The writer has not set this line up. Reply in the thread." (skills/waiting-page/page.html). Step: page-parked-actions.
- recovered-sidechain-boundary: the Recovered guard's boundary scan does not skip `isSidechain` records, so an agent's compaction, if inlined in the main transcript, would demand the line once (lint/report_guard.py recovered_problem). Decision: precompact-handoff
- recovered-rewrite-crash: in the rewrite path, a crash in recovered_problem reaches the outer except and drops the Unstarted and model reasons too (lint/report_guard.py rewrite_problems). Decision: precompact-handoff
- recovered-missing-report-tail: the missing-report block does not name the Recovered line, so a compacted session gets a second block on the rewrite (lint/report_guard.py BLOCK_REASON_TAIL). Decision: precompact-handoff
- recovered-prose-match: a `Recovered:` line in a mid-turn prose reply, or a report skipped after another Stop hook's block, satisfies or delays the demand (lint/report_guard.py CARRY_LINES). Decision: precompact-handoff
- carry-start-separators: `CARRY_START` does not count `Handoff gave:` after ",", "and", a dash or a pipe. The demand stays pending (lint/report_guard.py). Decision: precompact-handoff
- carry-none-forms: `(none)`, `[none]`, "no losses" and "n/a" are judged as Recovered items that need a source (lint/report_guard.py). Decision: precompact-handoff
- carry-bare-wrap: under a bare `Recovered:`, a wrapped line or sub-bullet becomes an extra item with no source (lint/report_guard.py). Decision: precompact-handoff
- carry-label-lookahead: the Recovered and Handoff gave exclusion in `LABEL_RE` has no word boundary, so `**Recovered work:**` is not read as a label. It passes report shape, and a bold carry line under Done joins the last Done item (lint/report_guard.py). Decision: precompact-handoff
- carry-pre-deploy: a session compacted before the guard lands gets one demand at its first report (lint/report_guard.py). Decision: precompact-handoff
- page-ruling-recite: the roadmap upkeep row "page rulings not on their step" lists a closed item again when a rewrite of its step note drops the `item <n>` cite (skills/roadmap/build.py, unfiled).
- page-ruling-free-home: that row matches only a bare step id or `step <id>` as an item's home, so a free-text home such as "c204 (zoomed picture)" is a silent miss (skills/roadmap/build.py, unfiled).
- page-ruling-doc-gaps: build.py's module docstring omits two unknown cases that SKILL.md lists (not a JSON object; no threads or pulls list), and no test covers them (skills/roadmap/build.py, test_build.py).
- dated-claim-year-digits: the dated-claims upkeep row reads any four digits after the day as a year. So "Checked Nov 1 1830 CDT" lists a fresh claim (skills/roadmap/build.py, CLAIM).
- dated-claim-dot-stops: that row stops at any ".", so "Checked hooks/guard.py on main Oct 20", "Oct. 28" and "Sept 28" are misses (skills/roadmap/build.py, CLAIM).
- dated-claim-edges: that row counts days in UTC, not Central. An explicit future year masks older claims. "_Checked_" is a miss. A future plan date after the word rolls to last year and lists (skills/roadmap/build.py, dated_claims).
- archive-no-record-any-repo: rule lane-closes-after-records denies any archive_session with no get_session record of the target, lane or not, in every repo; the steer costs one turn (hooks/guard_merge.py archive_hit). Decision: session-tiers
- archive-tag-literals: archive_hit repeats the `cs-step:` and `cs-repo:` literals from skills/roadmap/build.py; a rename there fails closed with "no step or repo tag" (hooks/guard_merge.py). Decision: session-tiers
- archive-rebased-lane-commit: rule lane-closes-after-records still refuses an archive when the lane's draft head was rebased into the integration branch, because the new hash is not the head. A repo that merges by squash or rebase never lands a lane head, so the release never fires there. A session on the owner's computer closes the draft (hooks/guard_merge.py archive_hit). Decision: session-tiers
- repeat-mark-inbox-ref-space: the tail-mark regex reads `\S+`, but an inbox ref holds a space (`inbox:<sid8>@<date> <hh:mm>`), so after a lost turn state file a second pass marks the item again (hooks/feedback_guard.py MARKED). Fix: `\S+(?: \d\d:\d\d)?`. Decision: owner-feedback-inbox
- repeat-inbox-source-shared-try: own and other-inbox blocks share one try, so an `os.listdir` error drops both sources (hooks/feedback_guard.py other_inbox_candidates). Decision: owner-feedback-inbox
- repeat-mark-lone-cr: mark_items reads bytes and parse reads text, so a lone CR in an owner message shifts item lines and the mark is reported "not marked" (hooks/feedback_guard.py mark_items). Decision: owner-feedback-inbox
- repeat-inbox-ref-same-minute: two blocks in one minute share an inbox ref, so the last label wins (hooks/feedback_guard.py). Decision: owner-feedback-inbox
- repeat-mark-append-race: the inbox rewrite on a hit can lose a capture append that lands in the same instant; there is no lock (hooks/feedback_guard.py mark_items). Decision: owner-feedback-inbox
- marker-flags-unmutated: the `(?im)` flags on the BRIEF_MARKERS rows in hooks/shared_names.py have no mutant, because mutate_guard TARGETS has no shared_names entry. Dropping the round row's flag would miss a Round on line two. Decision: agent-model-cap
- lifted-row-refusal-skips-load-brake: in hooks/guard.py, a lifted above-row refusal (step 9b3, the role model row) exits refuse() at once, so the load brake (9c) is skipped. Only under an owner override. The 9c comment names this hazard for the Opus refusal only (found in lane model-above-row-refused). Decision: agent-model-cap (agent model cap)
- role-key-not-sole-normaliser: role_key's docstring in hooks/guard.py calls it the one normaliser in this file, but role_has_model_pin folds role case on its own (found in lane model-above-row-refused). Decision: agent-model-cap (agent model cap)
- opus-override-confirm-says-ask: hooks/local_time.py (~line 101) says the Opus override lets spawns "go through without the ask". The override now lifts a refusal (owner item 127 pick a, Nov 1 17:25 CST) (found in lane model-above-row-refused). Decision: agent-model-cap (agent model cap)
- model-cap-mutants-missing: hooks/mutate_guard.py has no mutant for the never_lifted no-model split, the Explore Audit: Opus grant, Explore leaving OPUS_STANDING_ROLES, or the literal-brief remedy lines (`BRIEF_LITERAL_REMEDY`, hooks/guard.py ~2206 role row and ~3416 Opus refusal). The tests in hooks/test_guard.py cover them. The mutant label at line ~862 says "denial" (glossary term: refuse) (found in lane model-above-row-refused). Decision: agent-model-cap (agent model cap)
- explore-no-model-env-default: an Explore spawn with no model is not judged by `role_model_check` in hooks/guard.py (~line 2162) when `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL` is set. Per the Claude Code docs ("Choose a model", checked on 2.1.316, 2026-11-02), the agent file's `model` frontmatter beats `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL`, and `agents/explore.md` pins haiku, so the spawn runs haiku. Only `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL_FORCE` lifts the pin; the settings-write check asks before that is set. No guard change. Owner waiting-page item 137, pick a (Nov 1 18:38 CST), covers the case. Decision: agent-model-cap (agent model cap)
- workflow-unread-brief-false-alarms: hooks/guard.py refuses a Workflow `agent()` call whose brief is a variable, even when the variable holds the grant: an Explore with `Audit:`, a reviewer with `Round 2 of 3` or `Round 3 of 3`, a builder with `Round 3 of 3`. The override or a literal brief lifts it (coordinator ruling, Nov 1 22:54 CST). A real incident can promote reading the leading literal of a concatenation. The literal-brief remedy also shows on refusals no marker can pass: a test-author or record-writer row refusal, and a general-purpose or reviewer Opus refusal. Each costs one wasted retry. Decision: agent-model-cap (agent model cap)
- page-stale-untested-paths: no test pins `artifact_id` returning "" for a bare artifact url, or the `noted` line when the stamp write fails (hooks/guard.py page_stale_hit). Decision: page-stale-publish
- page-stale-url-forms: the `/artifact/{id}` and `/code/artifact/{uuid}` links may carry different ids for one page; then the by-id compare matches neither across forms. Unmeasured. Decision: page-stale-publish
- other-checkout-mkdir-touch: `touch` and `mkdir` are not in `MUTATING_COMMAND`, so a direct `mkdir <other checkout>/x` with no `cd` passes the write scan, and so does `mkdir -p <other>/d && cd <other>/d` (the `cd` target is made in the same command, so it does not exist when the guard reads it); the list is shared with frozen-path (hooks/guard.py `SHELL_WRITE_HINT`). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop
- builders-reset-soft-refused: `reset --soft` to a cut point or to HEAD~N, then a commit, is refused; the squash and `cherry-pick -n` gap covers two of these forms (hooks/guard.py own_changes). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests)
- builders-own-test-commit-forgotten: an own test commit is forgotten after any rebase (hooks/guard.py own_changes). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests)
- builders-reflog-no-author: the reflog cannot name an author, so a shared worktree or the main checkout refuses another role's test commit, and commit and push skip stop's main-checkout check (hooks/guard.py own_changes). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests)
- builders-amend-ff-pull-refused: amending a fast-forward-pulled commit is refused (hooks/guard.py own_changes). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests)
- builders-untracked-test-folder-refused: an untracked file under a test folder is refused; other repos are unmeasured (hooks/guard.py own_changes). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests)
- builders-cost-line-untested: no test proves the Cost paragraph (hooks/guard.py own_changes). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests)
- builders-cherry-pick-unlogged: ENTRY_RESOLVED does not match the subject `commit (cherry-pick):`, because `\b` after `)` never matches before `:`. So a cherry-pick the builder resolved by hand leaves no log line, though the decision says such picks are logged only. Fix idea: `(?::|\b)` (hooks/guard.py ENTRY_RESOLVED). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests)
- builders-rebase-start-unread: ENTRY_REBASE does not read a `pull -r ... (start)` subject as a rebase start, so no KEPT log line is written for that rebase. The write-back refusal does not depend on it (hooks/guard.py ENTRY_REBASE). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests)
- builders-amend-replayed-test: amending a product edit into a replayed test-author commit is refused, because the amended commit's diff holds the test path (hooks/guard.py own_changes). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests)
- lease-fails-open: simulator-boot-lease allows an MCP `launch` when `simctl list` cannot be read, as the cap and reuse rules do on main. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- lease-name-two-runtimes: an MCP `launch` by a name that is booted on one runtime and shut down on another (or a name differing only in case) is refused, though the tool may pick the booted one. Unmeasured. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- lease-action-case: the guard judges only action `launch`; whether the simulator MCP takes `Launch` or a missing action as launch is unread. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- lease-reason-carry-weak: the lease cases check that the reason carries "lease", which the rule-name prefix always supplies; only "sim-test" pins the reason text (hooks/test_guard.py). Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- lease-session-raw-payload: simulator-boot-lease reads the payload's `session_id` directly, not through session_id_of (hooks/guard_shared_tree.py), the home the other call sites use. It fails closed: a missing id refuses (hooks/guard.py sim_lease_holds). Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- sim-name-casefold-xcodebuild: the casefold name match also applies to `xcodebuild -destination name=`; whether xcodebuild matches names without case is unmeasured. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- sim-fixture-folder-silent: test_guard.py `folder_path` does not check that its fake xcrun folder exists, so a misnamed fixture reads as an unreadable device list and the guard allows. Decision: none
- mcp-launch-before-lock: an MCP `launch` that boots just before a `sim-test` run takes the lock skips the lock rule; both then run on the machine. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- mutate-guard-owner-unclear: hooks/guard.py (AUTHOR_MUTATION_TESTS comment) says builders keep editing hooks/mutate_guard.py and also calls it test code for a test-author. Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests.
- mutant-lint-copy-cost: each guard mutant copy now holds lint/ (97 files, 2.6 MB, about 9 ms); copies live until the run ends, so peak temp rises from about 0.6 GB to about 1.8 GB (estimate). Decision: none
- mutant-watch-tree-lint: the decision-watch and ruling mutant trees in hooks/mutate_guard.py may lack ../lint as the guard copy did; no verdict path to `_layout_module` found, not measured in full. Decision: none
- route-unread-wrappers: simulator-boot-route and the cap do not read `xcrun xcodebuild` or `arch -arm64 xcodebuild`, and they judge `ssh mac "xcodebuild ..."` against local devices. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- detached-launch-glued-amp: detached-launch passes a `&` glued to a closing quote (`xcodebuild ... "id=U"&`). Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- route-remedy-glued-action: simulator-boot-route reads `(cd ios && xcodebuild ... test)` and `test-without-building` as no test action, so the remedy names the generic destination; the verdict is still deny. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- route-glued-fd-redirect: a redirect glued to the destination quote (`'name=iPhone 15'2>&1`) reads as another name, so the boot is not judged. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- sim-test-record-silent: sim-test drops the record call's output and failure, so a failed record leaves its device unrecorded and the sweep keeps it. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- route-build-unmeasured: simulator-boot-route refuses `build`, `clean` and `-showBuildSettings` naming a shut-down simulator; whether those boot one is unmeasured (needs a Mac). Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- sim-test-untested-stops: no red test pins the 10 s KILL fallback, the crash window expiring, or the cleanup stop of the child. The HUP trap is pinned by outcome only, since bash 5 runs the EXIT trap on a fatal signal (lint/test_sim_test.sh). Decision: sim-test-stops-a-crash-loop.
- sim-test-child-signals: a backgrounded xcodebuild ignores INT and QUIT (no job control), so sim-test stops only xcodebuild itself, with TERM then KILL; its children may outlive it. Unmeasured. Decision: sim-test-stops-a-crash-loop.
- sim-test-late-reports: late reports of an earlier crash loop on the same UDID can stop a healthy run. Unmeasured. Decision: sim-test-stops-a-crash-loop.
- sim-test-kill-skips-cleanup: KILL on sim-test skips its cleanup, so the lock folder and a simulator it booted stay; the next run takes over a dead lock. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- sim-test-rename-back-gap: `take_over` puts a renamed folder back with a second rename; a run that takes the lock between the two renames is not covered (bin/sim-test). Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- sim-test-ctrl-c-in-boot: Ctrl-C during `simctl boot` leaves the device booted, since `booted_by_me` is set after the boot returns (bin/sim-test). Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- sim-test-lock-per-config-dir: the lock follows CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR, so two config folders mean two locks. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- bypass-closer-glued: simulator-boot-bypass passes a shell closer glued to the last word: `(cd x && open -a Simulator)`, `open -a Simulator&`, `open -a Simulator>/dev/null`, and `$(xcrun simctl boot)`. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- bootstatus-flag-boots: `xcrun simctl bootstatus X -b` boots a shut-down device and is not refused. The ruling names neither a refusal nor an exemption for it. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- bypass-rare-false-alarms: simulator-boot-bypass refuses three rare, harmless forms: `open -R .../Simulator.app`, `open -a Safari --args -b com.apple.iphonesimulator`, and a heredoc that writes a script holding `simctl boot`. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- bypass-flag-form-passes: simulator-boot-bypass passes `simctl --set P boot` and a backtick-wrapped boot. Decision: sim-test-is-the-one-boot-path.
- none-direction-bare-step: a bare "step" stays where a repo may have no roadmap; GLOSSARY.md makes step a roadmap step. Say "a roadmap step, or a todo record where no roadmap" (skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md, hooks/call_judge.py NONE_DIRECTION_RULE, docs/decisions/questions-go-to-owner.md, rework-leaves-page.md). Decision: questions-go-to-owner
- none-direction-doc-drift: the skill, the rule text and the record say "new options plus a lastAnswer note", but `is_reask` also needs no picked choice; add "with no picked choice" (skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md, hooks/call_judge.py, docs/decisions/question-reviewer-agent-hook.md). Decision: question-reviewer-agent-hook
- feel-step-set-done-untried: triage can set a feel step to done with no device trial, and the step then leaves every upkeep row (skills/roadmap/build.py upkeep). Decision: feel-fix-waits-device-check.
- feel-rename-half-similar: a rename under 50% similar, or with diff.renames off, reads as a delete: safe fail, wrong message (lint/check_record_slugs.py).
- feel-edit-swap-message: an edit that drops `**Feel.**` and adds a valid device trial fails with a misleading message (lint/check_record_slugs.py).
- feel-trial-time-space: a device-trial time with a space instead of `T` fails, though step JSON takes it (lint/check_record_slugs.py).
- feel-lint-says-device-check: the lint message says "device check" where prose says "device trial" (lint/check_record_slugs.py).
- feel-probe-skips-debt-check: the compliance probe in other repos does not run the debt check (lint/compliance_probes.py).
- feel-trial-trailing-text: a device-trial line with text after the time (a trailing period, a device name) reads as no trial, and the FAIL text prints no accepted form (lint/check_record_slugs.py).
- feel-debt-abandon-blocked: an abandoned feel debt (screen removed, owner drops it) cannot be deleted or have its Feel line dropped; only a false trial line gets past (lint/check_record_slugs.py).
- feel-trial-older-than-fix: the step row never compares a trial's `at` with the landing time, so a later feel PR on a step with an old trial passes (skills/roadmap/build.py).
- lane-lint-digit-sha-refused: an all-digit short sha is read as a PR number by `merge --lane-lint` and refused, never a false pass (merge/merge.py lane_lint). Decision: lanes-run-no-full-ci-job.
- lane-lint-hex-branch-read-as-sha: a local-only branch named like hex (cafe123) is read as a sha by `merge --lane-lint` (merge/merge.py). Decision: lanes-run-no-full-ci-job.
- lane-lint-sigterm-leaks-worktree: a SIGTERM between `git worktree add` and the try in lane_lint can leak a locked worktree (merge/merge.py lane_lint). Decision: lanes-run-no-full-ci-job.
- lane-lint-stale-origin-ref: after a branch is deleted on origin, a stale origin/<name> makes `merge --lane-lint` lint the old head (merge/merge.py). Decision: lanes-run-no-full-ci-job.
- answered-check-case: the ref, hash and path patterns match case, so a capitalised source reads as no source (lint/report_guard.py).
- answered-check-question-line: a line that ends in "?" is exempt whole, so a claim and a question on one line pass (lint/report_guard.py).
- answered-check-inline-fence: a line that starts with an inline triple-backtick span opens a fence; "#2" counts as a source; "no-op" counts as a negator (lint/report_guard.py).
- answered-check-silent-error: main() swallows an answered-check error with no stderr line (lint/report_guard.py).
- ruling-cite-plain-mode-missing-record: ruling_cite plain mode: a reason like "Item 8: decisions/zzz" names a record that does not exist; the refusal stands (hooks/question_form.py ruling_cite). Decision: question-form
- report-guard-quote-prefix-indented-code: QUOTE_PREFIX strips 3 spaces, so INDENTED_CODE (4 spaces) never matches; 4-space code with `?` costs one extra model call (lint/report_guard.py QUOTE_PREFIX, INDENTED_CODE). Decision: question-form
- report-guard-ask-text-cuts-front: ASK_TEXT_MAX cuts from the front, so an Input Needed item above the last 6000 characters is not shown to the model (lint/report_guard.py ASK_TEXT_MAX). Decision: question-form
- fail-open-three-paths: rewrite_relay fails open on a transcript error (lint/report_guard.py rewrite_relay). call_judge's outer except fails open on a past_rulings bug (hooks/call_judge.py). question_form's readline has no timeout. A harness kill at 35 s or 70 s passes silently (hooks/question_form.py readline). Decision: question-form
- no-direction-rule-no-candidate-test: NONE_DIRECTION_RULE says the reason names no target. Item 8 says the reason names the listed id. No test covers a re-ask that has candidates. The re-ask cases use PAGE_REPO, which lists none (hooks/test_call_judge.py PAGE_REPO). Decision: question-form
- call-judge-unreadable-source-untested: No case reaches the unreadable-source branch of past_ruling_context (hooks/call_judge.py). Decision: question-form
- rewrite-stamp-binds-to-sentence: the ruling stamp binds to the sentence, not the clause. "The <stamp A> ruling changed Y, and x was rewritten for the <stamp B> ruling" reads stamp A (lint/report_guard.py _ruling_stamp). Decision: rewrite-cite-predates-ruling
- rewrite-stamp-utc-clock-date: a clock-only UTC or GMT stamp takes the Central date, so the ruling can read a day early, a miss only (lint/report_guard.py _stamp_time). Decision: rewrite-cite-predates-ruling
- rewrite-stamp-recall-forms: "ruling (<stamp>)", "ruling, <stamp>" and "ruling <stamp>" are not read, so the claim passes (lint/report_guard.py _ruling_stamp). Decision: rewrite-cite-predates-ruling
- rewrite-unfetched-lane-refused: a true report about a lane branch that is not fetched is refused until the branch is fetched; cost one turn. Decision: rewrite-cite-predates-ruling
- pair-title-quotes-short-id: a pull title names every step id it quotes, such as "Revert r5 and c91". So a todo r5 goes red. False-alarm risk; it becomes a debt on a real incident (skills/roadmap/build.py).
- new-feature-mutant-no-return-step: P3 has the test-author make a mutant after the build, but no line sends it back, and none says it goes on a scratch copy outside the repo. The guard refuses a test-author write to product files. Decision: test-practices.
- reviewer-scratch-copy-outside-repo: the reviewer's P10 mutant on a scratch copy sits beside "never write a file through the shell"; the scratch copy must be outside the repo. Decision: test-practices.
- check-ruling-kind-judge-conflict: check_ruling_kind and the prose judge give conflicting verdicts on a second Kind line. The judge required a Kind line under a new ruling section; the lint fails on two. The judge also refused folding the section under the ruling with one Kind line kept (2026-11-01 21:40 CST). Decision: test-practices, PR 1028.
- cc-facts-word-in-string: cc_facts reads an env var name from `word.NAME` inside a string literal as a code read (hooks/precompact_handoff.py).
- cc-facts-unbounded-decl: `_cc_assigned` finds a declaration at any distance, so a lost literal can resolve to an unrelated same-named number and miss a change (hooks/precompact_handoff.py). A bound of about 50 KB before and 2 KB after would close it.
- cc-line-cut-tail: `_cc_line` cuts a 240-char line, so a one-fact change with a long list value loses the "Read:" pointer (hooks/precompact_handoff.py).
- window-env-forms-unread: `window_limit` takes the env window only as plain digits or with a k or m suffix. A form Claude Code reads, such as `1e6`, falls back to the settings or default window (500k). The hook then nudges later than Claude Code compacts when the real window is below 500k. It nudges earlier when the window is above (hooks/precompact_handoff.py). Decision: precompact-handoff
- last-nudge-gap-no-summary: with no early summary in Claude Code and no percent override, the last nudge can sit ~130k tokens before Claude Code's hard trigger (500k, f 0.2). Owner ruling 2026-11-01 19:27 CST is in precompact-handoff item 2. Decision: precompact-handoff
- grant-unfenced-paste: a pasted, unfenced message with a grant word and an act or scope word, such as "approved ... all checks", fires the grant check; a `[note] -> not-a-grant` item clears it (hooks/feedback_guard.py).
- answer-own-options: a reply such as "2 b" that answers the session's own options, in a repo with a waiting page, needs a send to the page writer (hooks/feedback_guard.py).
- grant-no-word-miss: a grant with no grant word, such as "you can merge anything green" or "ok to merge", is not caught (hooks/feedback_guard.py).
- answer-in-prose-miss: a chat answer to a page item with other text after it, such as "143 b, ok" or "143 b!", is not caught (hooks/feedback_guard.py).
- grant-home-not-read: a grant block passes with any tracked ruling home, and the guard does not read the record. An unclosed inline fence hides the rest of the message from the grant scan (hooks/feedback_guard.py).
- grant-root-home-any: in any block with a grant word and an act word, `settings.json` and `.claude/CLAUDE.md` pass as ruling homes, even when the grant match is false (hooks/feedback_guard.py).
- closed-token-midline: a bare `[closed]` token in mid-line, after the home, stops the standing, page and compaction open-id checks for that item (hooks/feedback_guard.py).
- grant-scope-misses: "all repositories", "all-repos", "all the repos" and "every repository" are not scope words. A negated "you do not have approval to push" fires the check (hooks/feedback_guard.py).
- question-placeholder-trailing-period: a Goal, Problem or Based on line that is a placeholder followed by a period passes, because the check is a fullmatch of one `<...>` (hooks/question_form.py is_placeholder). Decision: question-form.
- port-str-replace-no-symbol: a `context` str_replace on a port item skips the file:symbol rule, because the stored port flag is unseen there (hooks/question_form.py form_str_replace_hit). Decision: port-keeps-behaviour.
- departs-bare-angle-name: "Departs from: main — adds <Modal>" is refused as a placeholder, though `<Modal>` can be a real component name; "adds a <Modal>" passes (hooks/question_form.py).
- unread-early-line-east-blind: the early +05:00 line in the unread test can fail only on a clock at or west of UTC+3; CI on UTC covers it (hooks/test_decision_watch.py, mods/decision-watch/decision-watch.test.mjs). Step: guard-log-time-offset.
- guard-log-between-untested: `--between` in main() has no test, only window(); a `Z` bound fails before Python 3.11, so a pace read passes `+00:00` (lint/guard_log_report.py). Step: guard-log-time-offset.
- decision-watch-long-comment: a comment line near hooks/decision_watch.py:875 runs to 154 characters. Cosmetic. Step: guard-log-time-offset.
- undo-presence-skip: a line the merge-base holds, which both parents keep and the merge drops, reads as kept, in place or across a rename, by the `in_b` skip in lost_in_parent (lint/check_silent_undo.py). Decision: silent-undo-check-design
- undo-readd-old-name: main renames a file and adds a new file at the old name. Git pairs no rename, so lost_in_parent reads the base at the new name and flags a line the branch edited (lint/check_silent_undo.py). False alarm, same on main. Decision: silent-undo-check-design
- report-guard-model-window: the report guard's model-name check flagged a correct "reviewer haiku" twice on 2026-11-01, in a lane where the round-1 reviewer ran haiku and later rounds ran sonnet. It matched only the newest reviewer spawn. spawn_models keeps a set per role, so the transcript window it reads likely leaves out the older spawn; cause not traced (lint/report_guard.py spawn_models). Decision: none
- handoff-unread: --post-compact in hooks/reread_check.py sets the flag with no agent_id check. Unmeasured whether PostCompact fires inside an agent; if it does, the main session is refused until it reads its handoff. Decision: precompact-handoff
- handoff-unread: a session with no handoff at compaction writes one, then its next guarded call is refused until it reads that file. One Read. Decision: precompact-handoff
- page-locked-card-rebuild: a card built locked with its fold closed rebuilds once more on the next render, and a push in that render moves the caret in a note being typed (skills/waiting-page/page.html buildCard sets state.unfold after the signature is stored). Step: page-stale-asks-sink (the waiting page's stale-asks sink).
- page-unstood-draft-locks: an unpicked stood pick leaves draft `{unstood:true}`, so the fold stays locked and reads "Stays open: your answer is ready" though Send has nothing for the item; too strict, hides nothing. Step: page-stale-asks-sink (the waiting page's stale-asks sink).
- page-seen-divider-words: the seen divider's words were "Asked before, not answered" in the mockup (docs/design/stale-asks-sink.html); the page says "Asked before" (skills/waiting-page/page.html). Step: page-stale-asks-sink (the waiting page's stale-asks sink).
- lane-create-session-refused: a test-author in lane merge-lock-record-drift called create_session by mistake (its brief forbids it); auto mode refused it, correctly. The report guard then asks the owner to allow the tool, a false alarm when the refusal was right (lint/report_guard.py). Decision: none.
- shallow-probe-fails-allows: if `rev-parse --is-shallow-repository` fails (git older than 2.15, or a 10 s timeout), `branch_is_ancestor` gives None, and the guard allows `branch -D` over an only copy with a subject-unread note; main denies it. Only an environment fault reaches this, not a Claude act (`hooks/git_reads.py` `is_shallow`). Step: ste-changed-shallow-clone (shallow-clone ancestry reads unknown).
- shallow-only-copy-wording: in a shallow clone, the branch-delete refusal and the sweep's only-copy reason say "not an ancestor" or "only copy" when ancestry is unproven. The refusal fails closed, and the archive remedy loses nothing. Step: ste-changed-shallow-clone (shallow-clone ancestry reads unknown).
- ste-changed-none-says-shallow: `bin/ste-changed` names "shallow clone" as the cause for every unknown ancestry, even a 10 s timeout. Step: ste-changed-shallow-clone (shallow-clone ancestry reads unknown).
- sweep-shallow-skips-cherry: in a shallow clone, the worktree merged read in `janitor/sweep.py` does not see a tree merged by patch, so the sweep keeps it until `git fetch --unshallow` runs. The error is on the safe side. Step: ste-changed-shallow-clone (shallow-clone ancestry reads unknown).
- shallow-bash-sites: in a shallow clone, four sites print false lines: `githooks/main-ref/reference-transaction` (exit 1 reads as not on origin), `install.sh` (logs "a commit REF does not hold"), the session-start hook in `settings.json` ("did not fast-forward"), and `hooks/session_start.sh` (the "N behind" count can be too high). Each fails closed or is advisory only. Step: ste-changed-shallow-clone (shallow-clone ancestry reads unknown).
- is-shallow-no-mutant: `is_shallow` has no mutant, because the git_reads shallow case loads `git_reads.py` from its own folder, not from the tree under test. Step: ste-changed-shallow-clone (shallow-clone ancestry reads unknown).
- ask-send-position-untested: no test pins where Send ask sits or that Copy ask stays hidden after Undo. The over-limit tests accept either a disabled Send ask or an enabled one with a notice (skills/waiting-page/tests/page.test.mjs). Step: page-ask-box (the waiting-page Ask box).
- ask-term-no-glossary-row: GLOSSARY.md has no row for "ask" (the owner's free text on the waiting page), and the guard rows use "ask" for a permission ask. Step: page-ask-box (the waiting-page Ask box).
- ci-alarm-greener-conclusions: alarm.sh reds only failure, timed_out and startup_failure (actions/ci-alarm/alarm.sh). merge.py base_state also reds action_required, stale and any other conclusion but success, skipped, neutral and cancelled (merge/merge.py), so the alarm reads greener than the merge brake. Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main.
- ci-alarm-run-window: alarm.sh reads 100 runs (`--limit 100`, actions/ci-alarm/alarm.sh) and merge.py base_state reads 50 (`per_page: 50`, merge/merge.py). On a busy repo an old red schedule run can fall off the page and go unjudged. Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main.
- ci-alarm-same-second-tie: merge/merge.py base_state judges "newer" by list position, actions/ci-alarm/alarm.sh by strict createdAt. On a same-second tie the merge tool can clear a red run and the alarm opens an issue, so the alarm is the stricter side. Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main.
- ci-alarm-unknown-is-quiet: a failed alarm read or issue write prints `unknown` and a `::warning::` only, and exits 0 (actions/ci-alarm/alarm.sh, actions/ci-alarm/issue.sh), so a mis-permissioned or failing alarm opens no issue and no red job. Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main.
- ci-cancelled-red-run-cleared: a cancelled run with a red job (one job failed, then the run was cancelled) is red. A newer success run that passed every job name can clear it across groups, as it clears a failed run. actions/ci-alarm/alarm.sh never judges cancelled runs, so only the merge tool sees it (merge/merge.py base_state). Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main.
- ci-clear-compares-job-names: the cross-group clear in merge/merge.py base_state and actions/ci-alarm/alarm.sh compares job names and conclusions, not steps. A same-named job that skips a step in one event could clear the other event's failed run. Not reachable in this repo's checks.yml; other repos unmeasured. Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main.
- verdict-skip-other-runners: TAP `# skip`, go `--- SKIP:`, mocha `pending` and cargo `ignored` read PASS in `bin/verdict`; no runner in the repos uses them. Plan row S8.
- verdict-skip-stray-text: any exit-0 output that reads `N skipped` or `skipped=N`, such as a sync log, shows SKIP. Display only; the exit code is unchanged. Plan row S8.
- caution-line-missing: session start prints no caution line, not even the unknown line, when the claude-settings checkout ($d) is unset or has no lint/layout.py (hooks/session_start.sh).
- review-cost-gh-only: `--reviewed`'s cost counts gh calls only. It also runs `git fetch origin main` and local reads, and judges every PR on main's required checks. The pinned-list five-call count has no test. Decision: pr-review-record.
- design-round-alone-done: `--round 1:design-reviewer:...:PASS` alone reads done with no code reviewer. That is a lane claim. Decision: pr-review-record.
- report-guard-url-string-compare: lint/report_guard.py (about :591) compares the page-write URL as a string. It should call layout.is_waiting_page_url (lint/layout.py), the one home of "same artifact". A /code/artifact/<id> form or a URL with a query string blocks wrongly. Decision: questions-go-to-owner (questions go to the owner).
- send-error-still-counts: a send whose result is an error still counts (lint/_transcript.py send_targets), and the cloud form of fromSession for ack matching (lint/_transcript.py peer_sender) is unmeasured. Decision: questions-go-to-owner (questions go to the owner).
- waiting-page-chat-first-heading: skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md keeps the heading "Chat first" (about :178) above same-turn text. Decision: questions-go-to-owner (questions go to the owner).
- cite-reader-module-alias: `from <pkg> import merge as m`, then `m.name`, is not read. A helper read that way still counts as a helper, and an outside call is not scanned (lint/test_rule_audit.py _names_read_outside). Step: refusal-cite-reach (every refusal cites its rule).
- cite-reader-rebound-catch: rebinding the caught name inside a Stop handler (`e = OSError(..)`, then `fail(str(e))`) still reads as cited (lint/test_rule_audit.py _caught_as_stop). Step: refusal-cite-reach (every refusal cites its rule).
- cite-reader-tuple-except: `except (Stop, X) as e` reads as "not resolved", which is a false alarm. A one-line fix (lint/test_rule_audit.py _caught_as_stop). Step: refusal-cite-reach (every refusal cites its rule).
- cite-reader-stop-variable: `err = Stop("x"); raise err` is not read, and the `return False, "..."` tuples in wait_checks are not read, so their cite text is unchecked (lint/test_rule_audit.py; merge/merge.py wait_checks). Step: refusal-cite-reach (every refusal cites its rule).
- merge-network-stops-uncited: the network-error stops in merge/merge.py (about lines 666, 1937, 2315) stay exempt from the cite check. Decision standing-merge-grant (the standing merge grant) covers them: the merge tool "retries a network error 3 times". Step: refusal-cite-reach (every refusal cites its rule).
- merge-ignorechecks-no-record: the `merge.ignoreChecks` stop in required_names (merge/merge.py, about line 801) is exempt, because no decision states that a required check is never ignored. That rule is in actions/stamp/README.md only. Step: refusal-cite-reach (every refusal cites its rule).
- rejected-git-reads-absent: `_read_steps` in hooks/question_form.py maps git's "not a git repository" to an absent roadmap, on both the normal path and the early-exit path. The pre-check proves only that a `.git` entry exists, so a broken worktree pointer or a GIT_CEILING_DIRECTORIES setting skips the step-note search with only a stderr unknown line. This is rare, and Claude does not set these in normal work. Lane ruling, 2026-11-01 23:25 CST: keep absent, so a broken worktree does not refuse every question. Decision: question-form
- git-stderr-locale: the "not a git repository" match is English-only. A translated git message reads as unreadable, the safe side. Fix if it ever fires: set LC_ALL=C in the git child's environment. Decision: question-form
- shell-named-file-header: `_INTERPRETER_FIRST` reads a file or folder named like a shell (`cat > start.sh <<'EOF'`, `docs/sh/x.md`) as a shell header, so the body stays in view. Where: hooks/guard_core.py. Decision: env-file-stays-unread (no session reads or writes an env file's contents; a quoted heredoc body is text).
- odd-backtick-unquoted-body: an odd backtick in an unquoted heredoc body makes every layer read the rest of the body as a command. Where: hooks/guard_core.py strip_heredoc_bodies. Decision: env-file-stays-unread (no session reads or writes an env file's contents; a quoted heredoc body is text).
- written-file-run-forms-unread: `make -f f`, `at -f f`, `mv f g; bash g`, `bash ${f}` are not read as runs of a written file. A body holding a nested interpreter heredoc, which main refused by accident, now passes in these forms. Where: hooks/guard_core.py. Decision: guard-guards-mistakes-only (a guard stops Claude's mistakes, not an attacker's forms).
- repo-item-judged-as-repeat: a `[repo]` item is feedback about another repo, but the repeat check (hooks/feedback_guard.py `new_items`, `PROMPT`) judges it against this repo's roadmap, backlog and inboxes. So a "may repeat roadmap:x" flag can be a wrong-repo false alarm. The flag is advisory, and no case covers it. Fix idea: skip `[repo]` items in `new_items`. Decision: owner-feedback-inbox.
- pin-lint-named-file-silent: lint/check_agent_models.py ~121 says an unreadable record is reported. That report runs only with no argument (~141). A run on named files passes a mismatched pin silently. CI runs the full form (.github/workflows/checks.yml ~407). Decision: agent-model-cap (each role's model and effort).
- pin-lint-key-case: the pin reader (lint/check_agent_models.py ~66-78) reads `model:` case-sensitively. haiku_model (~49-54) does not. So `Model: sonnet` on reviewer passes. A quoted `name: "reviewer"` is linted, but hooks/guard.py role_has_model_pin compares the raw value. Decision: agent-model-cap (each role's model and effort).
- pin-lint-test-gaps: no unknown-family pin case (for example, `fable`) in lint/test_check_agent_models.py. No block-scalar `model:` case in hooks/test_guard.py role-model family G. The lint test has one (test_pin_is_read_past_comments_quotes_and_indented_text). frontmatter_keys now skips indented lines (hooks/guard.py ~2045). Decision: agent-model-cap (each role's model and effort).
- other-checkout-agent-flag: `has_agent` in `judge` reads only `agent_id` (hooks/guard.py, the `other_checkout_hit` call), while the agent flag passed to `judge_shell` (hooks/guard.py, ~3630) reads `agent_id` or `agent_type`. No measured payload differs, since agents carry both (hooks/guard.py:785). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop.
- cloud-disk-full: with the brake off in a cloud session (`CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE=true`), a cloud lane can use up its disk allowance. Writes then fail with "no space left on device". Decision: machine-load-brake (the machine-load brake).
- override-trailing-mark-on-word: `override off.`, `override 15m.`, `override ,15m` and `override -off` grant a one-act override, not off or timed. The whole-prompt check matches only a bare word. The owner may type a trailing period after `off` and keep an override meant to end (hooks/override.py). Same on main. Decision: owner-override
- base-copies-delete-refused: a staged or unstaged delete of a file whose content sits in pushed HEAD keeps `git worktree remove` refused, though nothing is lost (hooks/git_reads.py dirty_paths_are_pushed_copies). Decision: when-a-worktree-may-be-removed.
- base-copies-old-ancestor-refused: An index matches a pushed ancestor outside HEAD, its merge-base and HEAD's last 20 reflog entries. That match keeps the remove refused. The candidate set is bounded by design, with no history walk (hooks/git_reads.py). Decision: when-a-worktree-may-be-removed.
- ready-heal-second-run: a ready head may already hold its page. If the base gains a record during the wait, the page conflicts. prove() then heals and pushes a merge commit after green, so CI runs twice. The batch brake keeps the base still during a batch run (review round 1 of PR #1035, read from code). Decision: records-index-at-merge
- ready-resumed-claim-page-push: a resumed claim head with a stale page still pushes a page-only commit to a ready PR, since the draft step refuses a claim head (merge/merge.py confirm; PR #1035). Decision: records-index-at-merge
- claim-remedy-wording-narrow: the claim-head remedy says the base "claims nothing for" the claim; it also fires when the base has kinds but the claim made no numbers. The remedy is still right (review round 2 of PR #1035). Decision: records-index-at-merge
- ruling-kind-judge-live-unmeasured: the Edit judge's refusal of an added second ruling section is pinned as prompt text only, with the model stubbed. Whether the live model refuses it, or also refuses a legitimate added subsection, is unmeasured. Decision: rulings-declare-kind
- ruling-kind-first-section-stub: an Edit that adds the first ruling section, with no Kind line, to a record with no Kind line passes the judge. The lint catches it at pull request time. Decision: rulings-declare-kind
- ruling-kind-lint-no-remedy: the lint's "found N" message for two Kind lines does not name the remedy, a second ruling's own record. Only the judge and the record name it. Decision: rulings-declare-kind
- auto-mode-refuses-page-grant: auto mode refused `mcp__claude-code-remote__send_message` from the coordinator (Nov 1 23:05 CST) as self-modification, on a brief for a lane that adds an auto-mode allow line. The owner had granted it by a Queue page save (item 149 b), which auto mode does not count as his words. A page grant that widens auto mode needs his words typed in chat. Decision: questions-go-to-owner
- red-run-rebase-judges-old-commits: a lane rebased onto main after the check landed has each of its commits judged, so a commit from before the check needs a trailer after the rebase. Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits
- red-run-sha-not-verified: the old-code sha in a Red-run trailer is never checked to exist, so a made-up sha passes (lint/check_red_run.py). Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits
- red-run-existing-behaviour-claims-none: a test of existing behaviour fails on no old code, so it can only claim `Red-run: none: <reason>` (lint/check_red_run.py). Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits
- test-temp-sweep-unproven: no test proves the sweep in lint/_test_temp.py, which removes run folders older than a day; the red-run suite sweep case was dropped. Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits
- red-run-merge-revert-judged: the revert skip misses a merge revert. Git writes `This reverts commit <sha>, reversing`, and REVERT (lint/check_red_run.py:35) needs a `.` or line end after the sha, so the revert is judged and needs a trailer. Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits (Red-run trailer rule)
- red-run-base-unknown-quiet: `--base origin/main` in a repo whose default branch is not main gives UNKNOWN with no git reason. commits() runs `rev-parse --quiet` (lint/check_red_run.py:63), so git() gets empty stderr (:48). Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits (Red-run trailer rule)
- red-run-fixture-data-needs-none: a commit that adds only fixture data under `test_support`, `testdata` or `__snapshots__` is a test path (TEST_DIRS, hooks/guard.py:1383, read by is_test_rel), so it needs `Red-run: none: <reason>`. Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits (Red-run trailer rule)
- merge-superseded-cancel-only: a cancel row of a superseded cancelled run drops with no newest-run row of that name. Then head_read reads green where main read red. Needs one workflow with different job sets on one head. Fix: none — no incident, and the fix would stop dropping skip and pending rows with no newest-run match, which risks new false holds (merge/merge.py Host.superseded).
- merge-superseded-two-run-reads: a head with two or more linked runs reads `gh run list` twice per pass (Host.superseded, then judge_runs); the check-runs read and the runs read are not atomic. Cost only (merge/merge.py Host.checks).
- merge-cancelled-newest-twin: a cancelled newest run beside an older pass of the same name reads green, by main's twin rule (Host.classify, Host.judge_runs). Unchanged by merge-ignores-superseded-runs.
- silent-write-ref-gaps: guard rule silent-write reads seven git subcommands, a `src:dst` fetch, `gh pr merge` and `make merge`; a silenced `git branch -D` or `git update-ref -d` passes. Widen only after an incident (rule building-guard-needs-incident). Plan: refusal-vs-rule.
- silent-write-comment-stale: the comment above SILENT_WRITE_SUBCOMMANDS in hooks/guard.py quotes a rule text a cull cut and says six subcommands where the constant holds seven. Fix in a comment-only lane.
- load-brake-skips-create-session: guard rule load-brake does not read `create_session`. A cloud session runs in its own container and adds no load here. Test that claim if a local environment id is ever used. Decision: machine-load-brake.
- pwd-delete-trailing-quote: `rm -rf "$(pwd)"/*` followed by a comment that holds a lone quote passes, though the same delete of `./*` is refused; RM_PARENT_RAW has no `$(pwd)` form (hooks/guard_push_delete.py).
- checkout-f-cluster: `git checkout -fb x` passes while `git switch -fc x` is read as a force; checkout reads -f exactly and switch reads clusters (hooks/guard_shared_tree.py).
- switch-clean-cluster-false-alarm: `git switch -Cf x` asks or denies, and `git clean -fdn` or `-nfd` in a lost primary is refused, though a dry run deletes nothing (hooks/guard_shared_tree.py).
- merge-liftable-worktree-gone: the worktree-gone branch of fail() in merge/merge.py raises a plain Stop, so a liftable stop that reaches it prints its flag and also "No override lifts this refusal". It needs the tree removed after the claim push plus an unreadable-history stop. Step: refusal-stricter-fixes (refusals narrowed to their rule).
