- merge-silent-undo-no-repair: on 2026-11-02 at 00:53 CST the auto-mode classifier refused `Bash` for a `git push --force-with-lease` that would replace a lane's own merge commit (Git Destructive). A later commit cannot clear check_silent_undo, which reads the merge commit itself. The lane moved to a new branch and PR (#1068 replaces #1021). That is the cost of a silent-undo failure on a merge commit.
- press-label-verb-only: the press-link label excusal reads the verb only, so label "Approve on GitHub" beside "The session approves PR #5." passes. Decision: act-in-place
- shell-line-no-arg-chain: command-in-item passes a chain of commands with no arguments (`whoami; hostname`, `pwd && whoami`, `date | sort`). Decision: act-in-place
- shell-line-capital-prose: command-in-item lowercases the command word, so capitalised prose with `;` (`Option A; Option B`) is refused, and the decision does not say so. Decision: act-in-place
- time-pinned-nested-def-missed: the time-pinned finder keeps Python taint inside one function, so a nested def that asserts on a name tainted in module or outer scope is not listed (lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset
- time-pinned-mock-now-alarm: the time-pinned finder lists `mock.assert_called_once_with(now)` with a clock-tainted `now` as elapsed-assert, though it checks an injected clock, not speed (lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset
- time-pinned-destructure-decl: JS and Swift destructured declarations never taint a name. `const { ms } = planted(); assert.ok(ms < 10000)` is not listed (lint/find_time_pinned.py; real case: q_max harness/tests/run-tests.test.mjs, about lines 592-596). Decision: test-strategy-reset.
- time-pinned-bound-duration-arg: a duration bound to a name and then passed to a helper is not listed: `const dt = Date.now() - t0; expect(toMs(dt))`. Only the inline spelling is read (lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset.
- time-pinned-helper-clock-diff: a Python helper at module level, or a method helper such as `self.elapsed()`, that returns a clock difference does not taint a test's assert (lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset.
- time-pinned-literal-hides-ms: an object literal inside an asserted call hides a duration: `expect(report({ ms: Date.now() - t0 }))` is not listed. The finder does not read a clock past a `{` in a call's arguments (lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset.
- time-pinned-swift-since-helper: a Swift `func ms(since:)` called with a bare start time is not listed. A Swift `func` taints nothing, and a clock passed to a call is only an input (degen FreeSpaceCountSafetyTests.swift lines 188 and 213; lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset.
- time-pinned-offset-clock-input: an injected clock with an offset is listed as elapsed-assert: `edit_idle(tree, time.monotonic() - 1)` inside `assertIsNone` (claude-settings janitor/test_sweep.py:5178). The finder reads a `-` inside a call's arguments as a measured duration, though the test sets a clock, not a speed (lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset.
- time-pinned-bracket-scan-slow: each hit can start a bracket scan across much of the file, so a multi-MB file with many hits is slow: a mutated 4.4 MB file took 12 s. Real files are unaffected (lint/find_time_pinned.py). Decision: test-strategy-reset.
- pkill-inverse-parent: `pkill -v -P $$` and `pkill --inverse -P $$` pass the kill guard, though they kill every process that is not the shell's child. Decision: a-kill-by-pid-needs-an-owned-pid
- lsof-kill-in-quoted-string: `lsof -t | xargs -I{} sh -c "kill {}"`, and `xargs` into `python3 -c`, `perl -e`, `node -e` or `busybox kill`, pass, because no plain kill word follows. Main refused them. Decision: a-kill-by-pid-needs-an-owned-pid
- nohup-prefix-setsid: `nohup env A=1 setsid cmd`, `nohup nice setsid cmd` and `nohup sudo setsid cmd` pass, because the detached check reads past `nohup` only. Step: refusal-stricter-fixes (refusals narrowed to their rule).
- impeccable-verbs-off-table: after `;`, `doctor`, `hooks on`, `teach`, `pin`, `unpin`, a capitalised verb and `then,` pass, because only Commands table rows count. Decision: design-skills
- builder-test-path-symlink: the builder test-path check uses normpath, not realpath, so a symlink from outside a repo into its test folder passes. Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests
- lsof-kill-glued-paren: `(lsof -ti :3000 | xargs kill)` passes, because the kill check reads `kill)` as one word. Main refused it. The same gap holds for pgrep on main. Decision: a-kill-by-pid-needs-an-owned-pid
- spec-prose-oracle: a builder write to a prose fixture under spec/ (`spec/golden/out.md`, `spec/fixtures/expected.txt`) passes, though such a file is a test oracle. Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests
- author-raw-cwd: the test-author and designer branch of role_write_hit reads a `/c/` cwd raw, so on Windows an author write outside its scope passes. Same on main. Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests
- builder-diff-check-fail-open: On the batch-64b local run (2026-11-02, batch runner 7), hooks/test_guard.py failed 48 builder-refuse cases, all "got allow". Lint, test_merge and test_install_src ran in parallel from the same worktree. The failure did not reproduce alone, or on main commit 07f855dc with the same heads. Suspect: diff_violation in hooks/guard.py returns unread, and the commit is allowed, when its git read fails (fail open). Unmeasured. Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests (builders cannot edit tests).
- auto-mode-refuses-run-cancel: auto mode refused `Bash` (Nov 1 22:57 CST) for `gh api -X POST .../actions/runs/41377928751/cancel` on q_max, as "Interfere With Workloads". The run was a browser tier hung toward its 180-minute cap. MCP `cancel_workflow_run` on a session's own run passed with no refusal (Nov 6); a cancel of another session's run via MCP is unmeasured, and stays that session's act (shared-trees-no-machine-wide-kill). Decision: auto-mode-owner-acts (route run-cancel-mcp-cloud)
- bounded-git-kill-leaves-git: the bound's kill stops the subshell, not git under it, so a killed fetch runs on after the hook exits; on Windows CI it held FETCH_HEAD past cleanup (main run 41418810686, caseG1). Harm seen: none beyond a busy temp file (hooks/session_start.sh bounded_git).
- qmax-select-exits-1: q_max `select` (`harness/tests-for.mjs`) exited 1 in 24 scored PRs, so every changed suite ran there. A q_max commit that lacks `harness/tests-for.mjs` now reads the HEAD copy. Cause of the exit 1 unread (`_select_suites`, hooks/mutate_shared.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites.
- banchi-cli-identify-red-clean: banchi `t7/cli_identify.py` was not green on the clean source (assert) in PRs 898, 923, 926, 930 and 956. Cause unread. Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites.
- defines-checks-misses-augassign: `defines_checks` reads only a top-level `CHECKS =` or annotated `CHECKS:`, so `CHECKS +=` and other forms are missed (hooks/mutate_shared.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites.
- t7-box-map-no-run: `harness/tests/t7_box_map.py` matches the tN regex but has no `run()`, so it reads crash (hooks/mutate_guard.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites.
- t7-group-cap-margin: a T7 group takes 94 s against the 120 s suite cap (`--suite-timeout` default, hooks/mutate_guard.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites.
- playwright-closed-title-crash: `_PW_NOT_ASSERT` reads "has been closed" in a test title as a crash, which undercounts kills (hooks/mutate_shared.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites.
- pr-budget-restarts-per-part: `--pr-budget` restarts for each banchi profile part, since `replay_pr` counts from its own start (hooks/mutate_shared.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites.
- budget-s-flake-margin: `BUDGET_S = 5.0` leaves about 1 s above the first mutant's start, so a slow machine can flake (hooks/test_mutate_replay.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites.
- mutate-js-linecount-per-site: `lineOf` in hooks/mutate_js.mjs splits the source from its start for each site, so the cost is O(n) per site. Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites.
- mutate-js-multiline-site-first-line: a multi-line site is keyed by its first line (`lineOf(node)`, hooks/mutate_js.mjs). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites.
- renamed-file-all-lines-changed: `changed_lines` runs with `--no-renames`, so a renamed file reads as all lines added (hooks/mutate_shared.py). Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites.
- lane-force-push-refused: the auto-mode classifier refused a lane's `git push --force-with-lease` to its own draft branch, to add Red-run trailers, twice on 2026-11-02: lane worktree-base-copies-remove at 00:30 CST (replacement PR #1064) and lane tci-6 at 06:05 CST (replacement PR #1087). Refusing stays as on main (lint/report_guard.py forced an allow ask). Decision: owner-override.
- stale-cancel-all-skipped: a cancelled main run whose jobs were all skipped clears when a newer covering run holds those job names, whatever their conclusions (`cleared`, merge/merge.py). Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main.
- stale-cancel-missing-head-sha: the cancelled-run clear treats a newer run with no `head_sha` as on another head; GitHub always sends it (`cleared`, merge/merge.py). Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main.
- rewrite-shared-try-drops-all: lint/report_guard.py rewrite_problems runs answered_problem, recovered_problem, the models check and the unstarted check in one try. One raise drops all four reasons with no log. The landed and unlanded paths isolate each call. Unlikely in normal work: it needs a malformed transcript record.
- rewrite-no-marker-skips-judge: lint/report_guard.py, the stop_hook_active rewrite path. A rewrite after a refusal that only the shape check made carries no marker, so the answered and Recovered checks do not judge it again. lint/test_report_guard.py pins this. It may be stale; the owner has not ruled. Unlikely in normal work: it needs a shape-only refusal. Decision: none.
- relay-comment-stale-in-main: lint/report_guard.py, the comment above `reasons += relay` in main(). It names only the pick and Departs checks. The relay also carries past-ruling, question-keys and answered. Unlikely in normal work: a comment only, no run changes. Decision: none.
- cross-pr-merge-review-partial: the cross-PR merge resolution for PR #1016 and PR #1023 (answered-needs-source) was reviewed only for dropped or doubled checks (reviewer haiku, 2026-11-02). It was not a full lane review. No single file (merge resolution). Unlikely in normal work: the review covered dropped and doubled checks.
- interrupt-tool-refused: a test-author agent called interrupt_session by mistake, and the auto-mode classifier refused it, with no effect. Step: refusal-stricter-fixes (refusals narrowed to their rule).
- page-button-this-item: "this item" in the shared page-button list means "Click this item on GitHub." needs no link, though main refused it. Decision: act-in-place
- clause-start-noun-verb: a clause after a new break that starts with the noun "merge", "hit" or "approve" ("…, but merge stays manual.") is refused, though main allowed it. Decision: act-in-place
- close-paren-no-break: ")" is no clause break, so "(The session reruns CI) Mark the PR ready." with no link passes. Decision: act-in-place
- classifier-refused-read-probe: on 2026-11-02 the auto-mode classifier refused a reviewer's read-only Bash probe of merge/merge.py. The reviewer read the code instead. The coordinator ruled no new allow.
- other-clone-unread-writers: into a separate clone, as into another checkout, these pass the write scan: `touch`, `mkdir`, `rsync -a src/ <dir>/`, `curl -o`, `tar -C`, `make -C`, `env -C`, `bash -c 'cd X && touch f'`, `cd $HOME/...` (a `$` name is not read), and `git -C <dir>` with `add`, `rm`, `branch -D`, `tag` or `push`, or with `--git-dir=` or `--work-tree=` (hooks/guard.py `SHELL_WRITE_HINT`, `_cd_targets`). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop
- other-clone-project-dir-unmeasured: the stranded-cwd part of `other-checkout` reads the home clone from `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR`; that hooks receive it is read from the Claude Code hooks docs, not measured live. Unset, the part never fires (hooks/guard.py `_clone_scope`). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop
- other-clone-stranded-false-alarm: from a cwd stranded in a separate clone, `cp a <home>/`, `mv`, `sed -i 's/a/b/' <home file>` and `chmod +x <home file>` are refused even when the target is home. The write scan reads sources and non-path words as paths in the stranded clone (hooks/guard.py `elsewhere`). `cd <home> && <cmd>` works. Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop
- other-clone-silent-catch: `_clone_scope` catches every exception as "not stranded" with no log line, so a bug there turns the stranded part off silently. `_origin_key` reads `github.com/o/r` and `gitlab.com/o/r`, or two relative-path origins, as one repo (hooks/guard.py). Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop
- retired-advisor-reader: the retired terminal advisor still names a log reader (hooks/reply_judge.py:31, docs/decisions/reply-judge-samples-never-refuses.md:8). The log has no live reader.
- restates-na-by-copied-anchors: a child repo whose rule files hold at least 90% (`lint/compliance_probes.py: NA_SHARE = 0.9`) of the live claude-settings anchors reads as claude-settings, so restates gives n/a there. Reachable only by copying the global rules whole. Decision: child-cites-not-copies (a child repo names a claude-settings record and never copies it).
- restates-kind-line-unit-dropped: a unit whose first line is a `Kind:`, `Plan:` or `Home:` line is dropped whole (`plain_words`, lint/compliance_probes.py), so a copy on the next line with no blank line between passes. Rare in a CLAUDE.md. Decision: child-cites-not-copies (a child repo names a claude-settings record and never copies it).
- page-stale-title-space: `stale_pages.carries_data` renames the live title before it collapses white space, so an untouched page titled "Queue " (with a trailing space, a newline or NBSP) and empty blocks passes `page-stale`. A copy of page.html has the exact title, so normal work does not hit it. Decision: page-stale-publish
- page-stale-old-main: until a checkout fetches this merge, its origin/main template is still titled "Queue", and an untouched "Queue" page passes `page-stale`. It ends at the fetch. Decision: page-stale-publish
- mockup-link-target-text-ok: a link whose target file holds the link's own target-path text reads as ok on origin (origin check, `mockup_on_origin`). Unlikely in normal work: Claude does not write such a file by mistake. Decision: mockup-home (mockups live in the design folder).
- layout-test-docstring-home-none: lint/test_layout.py:1264 docstring says `mockup_homes(root, home=None)`, but `mockup_homes` takes `root` only (lint/layout.py:520). Decision: mockup-home (mockups live in the design folder).
- mockup-origin-deleted-branch-ref: an origin ref of a deleted branch that was never pruned still holds the file, so the mockup reads ok after its branch is gone. The lane ruling "any origin ref" allows this. Decision: mockup-home (mockups live in the design folder).
- build-docstring-says-six: skills/roadmap/build.py:91 docstring still says about six git processes per build; the mockup check adds up to four. Decision: mockup-home (mockups live in the design folder).
- designer-other-repo: a screen for another repo has no designer path from this repo (blind_brief reads the spawn repo's device list; designer-scope checks only the folder prefix).
- refusal-plural-not-cleared: "refusals were right" (a plural, or one phrase for two tools) clears no row; only "refusal was right" or "refusal is right" directly after each tool name does, so the reply must say it once per tool (lint/report_guard.py `says_refusal_right`). Decision: refusals-reach-the-owner
- refusal-hedge-word-broad: the hedge words include plain "or" and "if", so "`Bash`: refusal was right (rm -rf or similar)" keeps the row; the block reason does not name the hedge rule (lint/report_guard.py `says_refusal_right`). Decision: refusals-reach-the-owner
- report-guard-gh-wrapped-unread: the report guard does not read `gh pr merge` or `gh pr create` inside `$(...)`, `bash -c` or `eval`; the outer command word is not gh (lint/report_guard.py). Decision: none.
- ci-step-roadmap-builder: the CI step "Roadmap builder fixture suite" (.github/workflows/checks.yml) names build.py a builder. Renaming it changes a check name the merge tool and branch rules may read. Decision: none
- dropped-start-same-sha-silent: after the draft-and-ready remedy, a second dropped start on the same head sha posts nothing, since the marker is per sha (actions/ci-alarm/dropped.sh). Decision: opt-in-ci-templates-composite
- dropped-start-fork-approval: a first-time fork PR waiting for approval has no run, so the alarm may call it dropped (actions/ci-alarm/dropped.sh). Decision: opt-in-ci-templates-composite
- dropped-start-check-name-copy: dropped.sh holds its own copy of DRAFT_SKIP_CHECK; test_action.sh pins the two equal (actions/ci-alarm/dropped.sh). Decision: opt-in-ci-templates-composite
- zizmor-env-unset-untested: `ZIZMOR_PATHS` in RUNNER_UNSET (bin/ci-job) has no red case. An exported value reaches only a zizmor step with no `with: paths`, and checks.yml always sets it. Decision: none
- zizmor-paths-doc-drift: the `paths` input text in actions/zizmor/action.yml says "separated by spaces"; run.sh splits on spaces or newlines. Decision: none
- zizmor-test-comment-files: actions/ste-lint/test_action.py:11-12 and :653-654 say zizmor pins are compared on run.sh only; bin/ste-changed compares run.sh and action.yml. Decision: action-pins-follow-sessions
- multiline-value-edges: in `block_scalar` (lint/workflow_steps.py), a body line indented less than the first is cut mid-text, leading blank lines are dropped, an all-blank `|+` body reads "", and `|2` stays as text. GitHub rejects or rarely meets each form. Decision: none
- bash-refused-in-ci-wall-time: the Stop-hook refusal guard reported that the auto-mode classifier refused `Bash` in lane session ci-wall-time (2026-11-03, about 17:40 CST). The refusal is unseen. The lane's own Bash calls all succeeded, so the refused call was likely an agent's. Possibly a false alarm of the refusal guard. No owner question: decision auto-mode-owner-acts rules out a whole-tool allow.
- agent-send-message-refused: an agent's mistaken `mcp__claude-code-remote__send_message` call was refused by the auto-mode classifier in lane ci-wall-time (2026-11-03). The refusal was correct. The refusal guard still demanded an owner ask: possible false alarm of that guard.
- ci-hygiene-step16-env-override: ci-hygiene step 16 says "with no env override", but the Windows and macOS guard shards pin a `GUARD_SHARD` env. The wording should say no env that overrides the scope.
- checks-macos-b-idle-runner: checks-macos-b starts an idle macOS runner on docs-only PRs. Its only `needs` is `scope`, and the scope flag gates its CI steps, not the job. A job-level `if` would skip a needed job and the rollup fails a skipped job (lint/test_windows_split.py forbids a scoped job-level `if`). Fix: none — owner decides
- draft-guard-only-loose: lint/test_windows_split.py `draft_guard_only` (:522-536) accepts `<flag> || !draft` and `!draft && event_name != 'schedule'`, though its docstring says it reads no scope flag. Its five truth-table rows have no draft PR with the flag true, and no schedule event. Adding `("pull_request", "true", True, False)` closes the first form. Unlikely in normal work: either form in a job-level `if:` is a deliberate choice. Decision: none.
- draft-house-form-untested: lint/test_windows_split.py, the job-level `if:` subTest near :835, tests only the bare `${{ !github.event.pull_request.draft }}` form as allowed. The house form `github.event_name != 'pull_request' || !github.event.pull_request.draft` (.github/workflows/checks.yml:42) is not tested as allowed there. Unlikely in normal work: a test gap only, no run changes. Decision: none.
- bare-step-for-ci-step: comments use a bare `step` where they mean a CI step: checks.yml:505 (`Last step`), :507 (`The same step ends`), :749 (`moved steps`), :919 (`their steps`); lint/test_windows_split.py:34 (`step parser`), :69 (`its step's`), :872 (`a full step's`). Unlikely in normal work: comments only, no run changes. Decision: none.
- designer-unpushed-worktree-lost: a container reclaim loses a designer worktree that was never pushed, and its mockups with it. No hook pushes for the designer (agents/designer.md). Unmeasured, no incident. Fix: none — no incident, agents/designer.md already orders commit and push before stopping, and a new SubagentStop refusal needs an incident (building-guard-needs-incident). Decision: waiting-data-in-repo (git is each artifact's home).
- export-scan-vendored-js: the export's `password` shape refuses vendored JS libs in a page or supporting file (jquery, axios.min.js), so the whole artifact's export stops. A debt after a real incident (skills/waiting-page/export_page.py).
- export-scan-near-misses: `password: <your password>` (a space), `OLDPWD=/x`, `pkg@1.0.0-beta.1` and `icon@1.5x.png` still refuse; `jane@2x.png.example.com` passes; UTF-16 files are not scanned (skills/waiting-page/export_page.py).
- option-key-gap-relative-path-unjoined: `design_writes` (hooks/question_form.py, read at hooks/guard.py page_design_hit) and `judged_item_writes` (read at hooks/call_judge.py) call `_write_data` without the payload cwd, so a relative `file_path` reads as None there and the picture check and the model review skip it (decision option-keys-are-letters)
- option-key-gap-str-replace-other-page: a `str_replace` to another page's item is not judged, since it carries no `options`. The cwd's own page judges its new text (hooks/question_form.py keyed_options_hit; decision option-keys-are-letters)
- option-key-gap-closed-reopened: a closed item with a key none, reopened by a later update, passes both writes, since each is judged alone (hooks/question_form.py _open_decision; decision option-keys-are-letters)
- option-key-gap-kind-case: an item with `kind` "Decision" (capital) and a key none passes, since the kind is compared as written (hooks/question_form.py _open_decision; decision option-keys-are-letters)
- option-key-gap-kind-unpinned-off-page: the gap suite pins a closed item off the cwd page, not a task or note item. A mutant that drops the kind check in keyed_options_hit survives (hooks/test_question_form_option_gap.py). Decision: option-keys-are-letters
- auto-mode-refuses-private-push: the auto-mode classifier refused a `git push` of q_max and degen audit questions to a claude-settings handoff branch (2026-11-03 15:55 CST, reason "Out-of-Place Publication"). The owner had just ruled not to over-guard privacy for these items (15:54 CST). The coordinator sent them to triage by message instead. Decision: auto-mode-owner-acts
- wait-pid-fixed-pid-flake: the guard test case "wait-pid: a kill -0 loop stays refused" uses the fixed pid 12345. When a live process the session did not start holds that pid, rule machine-wide-kill fires before the waiter rule, and the case fails (batch 3 local run, 2026-11-03 19:58 CST, 1 of 5677; tier-boundaries saw it too). Fix: pick a pid known to be free, or assert either refusal (hooks/test_guard.py, decision a-kill-by-pid-needs-an-owned-pid).
- milestone-branch-run-wording: decision swift-tests-at-milestones says a milestone run "tests main". Since degen #426, a dispatch on a branch tests that branch, and degen uses one such run on a batch branch as its native-check record (degen rule tests-once-per-batch). The record does not say whether a branch run counts as milestone proof (lane degen-milestone-ref, 2026-11-03 20:11 CST).
- seed-branch-delete-refused: the auto-mode classifier refused triage's `git push origin --delete seed/banchi-roadmap` as destructive git, though the branch and banchi's `roadmap` were the same commit (2026-11-03 20:55 CST). Each seed hand-off to the one roadmap writer leaves a stray branch. The standing grant for merged-branch deletes covers claude-settings only (decision roadmap-one-writer). At 21:20 CST the classifier also refused triage's send_message that handed this delete to the coordinator. That refusal was right: it blocked a hand-off of a refused act (decision refusals-reach-the-owner).
- review-cite-open-only: `REVIEW_CITE` in merge/merge.py says only an open pull request takes a review record. The code enforces it in `main`, but decision pr-review-record does not say it. Wording only. Decision: pr-review-record
- green-cite-misplaced: two refusals in merge/merge.py, "deadline passed, GitHub still shows the head" and "branch head moved", print `GREEN_CITE`, a rule that governs neither. Wording only. Decision: standing-merge-grant (the merge grant and the merge tool)
- scope-playwright-test-action-cost: an edit to actions/playwright-setup/test_action.py starts checks-guard and checks-shoot, though neither reads it; the `checks` job runs it. Cost: one guard job per edit. A `test_action.py` arm setting only `code` would drop it (.github/scripts/ci-scope.sh).
- scope-shoot-md-no-carve-out: a .md under .github/shoot/ (none exists) would start checks-shoot and checks-guard; only actions/playwright-setup has the .md carve-out (.github/scripts/ci-scope.sh).
- scope-playwright-comment-md: ci-scope.sh line 3 and lint/test_ci_scope.py lines 29-30 say the playwright-setup arm sets checks_guard without naming its .md exception; ci-scope.sh line 57 states it.
- execpath-fallback-script: when CLAUDE_CODE_EXECPATH gives no readable file, `cc_version_check` (hooks/precompact_handoff.py) reads `claude` on PATH. If that is a wrapper script, not the binary, every fact reads "unknown" and the check prints a "changed compaction facts" line, not an unknown line. A fix would take the fallback file only when its version fact reads. Locally the variable is normally set, so the fallback seldom runs. Likelihood unmeasured. Decision: precompact-handoff, item 12.
- execpath-fallback-key-split: the variable keys its cache entry on its own path, which may be a link, and the fallback keys on the real file. When the variable is set and later unset, the same binary is read once more (about 3 s) and the line may print once more. Decision: precompact-handoff, item 12.
- execpath-fallback-windows-cwd: on Windows, `shutil.which` searches the current directory before the injected PATH, so a `claude.exe` in the hook's working directory would be read. Not run on Windows. Decision: precompact-handoff, item 12.
- execpath-fallback-untested-mutants: 3 mutants pass the cc_version_check tests: removing `realpath`, looking up before the no-session early return, and looking up when the variable's file reads. Decision: precompact-handoff, item 12.
- text-only-decision-wrapping-wide: `docs/decisions/design-agents.md` says the design reviewer catches "wrapping" unqualified, while `agents/design-reviewer.md`, the file the reviewer reads, narrows it to a label, button or heading that newly wraps. Decision: design-agents
- text-only-overflow-css-fix: in a text-only lane, an overflow fix routes to the builder (skill design-review step 5), and only the "edits more than copy" bullet, read at fix time, sends a CSS fix to a designer. Decision: design-agents
- lockfail1-catch-text: lockfail1's catch says "could not hold settings.json open" but also catches a throw from the install run; it still counts a FAIL, so only the text misleads (install.test.ps1). Decision: copy-mode-overwrites-no-backup
- lint-jobs-copy-suite-jobs: `LINT_JOBS` copies `CHECKS_SUITE_JOBS`, so a third split job added to the constant leaves that job's no-scope-condition check unread (lint/test_windows_split.py LINT_JOBS; rule `building-allow-list-is-the-constant`: an allow list points at the constant, never a copy).
- page-ready-line-removed-key: a stored draft whose option key the writer later removes reads "Ready to send: , your choice." (Send stays off); show nothing when the draft is not ready (skills/waiting-page/page.html). Decision: questions-go-to-owner
- auto-mode-refuses-lane-force-push: the auto-mode classifier refused a test-author's `Bash` force-with-lease push of its own rebased lane branch (2026-11-03 20:07 CST, lane value-cites). The lane pushed to a new branch name instead, so nothing was lost. A rebase of a pushed lane branch goes to a new branch name, never a force push. Decision: stale-safe-force-push
- decision-watch-slack-unpinned: no case pins the one-second slack in `collect_unread` (hooks/decision_watch.py), the allowance for a guard.log line written in the same second as the human message; dropping it stays green (review round 1, lane decision-watch-dst).
- lane-one-topic-slug-dots: rule lane-one-topic reads step and topic slugs as letters, digits, `_` and `-`; a dotted slug such as `v1.2` reads as no topic and is refused (hooks/guard_merge.py brief_hit). Decision: session-tiers
- lane-one-topic-new-step-reread: adding a cs-step tag by set_session_tags before a brief makes the brief refused once as stale; one get_session clears it (hooks/guard_merge.py brief_hit). Decision: session-tiers
- step-home-any-ref: a `step:<slug>` home passes when any branch holds roadmap/data/steps/<slug>.json, not only the record branch, so a retired step on an old branch still counts (`Homes._held`, hooks/feedback_guard.py). Closed notes resolve the same way. Decision: owner-feedback-inbox
- step-dir-spelled-twice: the step folder is spelled in `STEP_PATH` (hooks/feedback_guard.py) and in override.py's step folder constant. Decision: owner-feedback-inbox
- silent-undo-two-call-sites: lint/check_silent_undo.py is called twice, by the "Silent undo check" run line in checks.yml and by actions/silent-undo/check.sh. The run line stays until bin/ci-job --lint can run a local uses: action, which it reports as unknown. ruling-kind has the same shape: lint/check_ruling_kind.py is called by checks.yml and by actions/ruling-kind/check.sh. Decision: none.
- silent-undo-push-skips-green: with an empty BASE_REF (a push event), actions/silent-undo/check.sh exits 0 with a skip, so the step is green without a check. A caller must gate the step on pull_request, as checks.yml does. Fix: none — action.yml and check.sh already say a run with no pull request base is skipped, and checks.yml gates its call on pull_request. Decision: none.
- precommit-merge-old-offender: during a conflict-resolving merge, `githooks/pre-commit/pre-commit` diffs the index against HEAD. So it refuses a symlink or env file the other branch already committed. Its printed `git rm --cached` would drop that branch's file. Hits only a repo that already tracks an offender. Fix if it fires: when MERGE_HEAD exists, keep only paths that also differ from MERGE_HEAD. Decision: opt-in-ci-templates-composite.
- no-verify-unguarded: `git commit --no-verify` (or `-n`) skips `githooks/pre-commit/pre-commit`, and no guard refuses it. No session is known to have used it, so no guard is built (rule building-guard-needs-incident). Decision: opt-in-ci-templates-composite.
- all-jobs-passed-child-key-indent-steps: a child workflow whose `steps:` list sits at the key's indent (valid YAML) reads as 0 steps (`step_blocks`, lint/workflow_steps.py), so `child_problems` (lint/all_jobs_passed_rules.py) says the action step is missing. Red only, never a pass.
- all-jobs-passed-flow-needs-bracket-comment: a multi-line flow `needs: [` with `]` inside a comment on an earlier line cuts the list short (`needs_of`, lint/all_jobs_passed_rules.py) and reports missing jobs. Red only.
- all-jobs-passed-draft-eq-false: an if written `draft == false` or `draft != true` reads as unreadable (`eval_if`, lint/test_windows_split.py), a loud false alarm. A fix moves the cs scope verdicts too.
- all-jobs-passed-job-continue-on-error: a job-level `continue-on-error` on all-jobs-passed or on a needed job is unread by both rule sets, as on main. Claude is unlikely to write it.
- all-jobs-passed-cs-crlf: the cs rules read a CRLF checks.yml as 0 steps. checks.yml is LF; `child_problems` normalizes CRLF.
- builder-glob-past-refusal: in lane all-jobs-passed-wiring a builder met a refusal on a command that named `.github/` and `git` together, and ran its read-only check with the path written `lint/../.gith*/workflows/checks.yml`. guard.log holds no row for it, so the refusal came from outside the repo's guard (the harness's own check), not a named hook here. Briefs now say: stop and report a refusal, never route around it.
- all-jobs-passed-run-both-untested: no case in actions/all-jobs-passed/test_action.sh shows that run.sh still runs wiring.sh after a failed check.sh. A run.sh that stops early still fails the job; only the wiring message is lost until the next run.
- all-jobs-passed-action-path-inline: actions/all-jobs-passed/action.yml runs `bash "${{ github.action_path }}/run.sh"`, the same form main used for check.sh. github.action_path is not caller input. Whether zizmor flags it is unmeasured here; CI runs zizmor on actions/.
- refile-target-unfetched: in `stale_pages.py` batch mode, a not-found page names the claude-settings waiting page as its re-file target even when that page was not fetched in this run (no file, no marker). That page's own line reads `unknown: no fetched file`, so the run still exits 1. Decision: none (skill waiting-page, "Update a repo's pages").
- hub-word-in-code: `hub_clone()` and the tests in `skills/waiting-page/` call the claude-settings checkout "hub", a name not in GLOSSARY.md. It appears in code and tests only, never in output or prose. Decision: none.
- answered-fold-false-lock: a stale local draft restored for an item answered elsewhere locks its Answered-section fold with "Stays open: your answer is ready", though Send skips saved items. Fix: `foldLocked = (it) => !isSaved(it) && (...)` (skills/waiting-page/page.html foldLocked). Decision: answered-sink notes, pick 4.
- answered-feed-receipts-test-only: `feed.receipts` has no product reader; one test waits on it. Re-point that test to wait on `state.receipts[<id>]` and drop the field (skills/waiting-page/page.html feed).
- answered-refold-unprobed: answered-sink notes pick 4 says a sunk card folds again after Change and Send; the code reads it stays open until reload. No test covers it; unknown, not clear.
- lane-force-push-refused: the auto-mode classifier refused `git push --force-with-lease --force-if-includes` to a lane's own draft branch (page-answered-sink, 2026-11-03 18:0x CST) after an amend for a missing Red-run trailer, so the lane opened a replacement PR (#1137 for #1129). Fix: allow that form for a session's own unmerged lane branch, or teach lanes to add the trailer before the first push.
- page-filed-cost-misses-read-back-rev-parse: the cost line of the page-filed check (docs/decisions/owner-feedback-inbox.md) counts one `rev-parse` only for a Stop with no read-back. A read-back also runs one (`Homes.reads_waiting_page`, hooks/feedback_guard.py), so the cost reads one process start low. Decision: owner-feedback-inbox
- page-filed-blocks-is-a-glossary-not-name: the page-filed check text (hooks/feedback_guard.py docstring, docs/decisions/owner-feedback-inbox.md) says the guard "blocks" and "blocks nothing", and the glossary lists `block (this sense)` as a Not-name of `refuse`. Reword to "refuses" when the text is next edited. Decision: owner-feedback-inbox
- verdict-argv-value-match: `test_argv` (bin/verdict) matches any argv token, values included. `xcodebuild -scheme test build` reads as a test run, and `swift test --filter list` reads as no run. Rare names.
- verdict-swift-version: `swift test --version` is not in SWIFT_INFO (bin/verdict), so it reads FAIL "no test count". Rare in normal work.
- verdict-marker-prefixed: a test success marker after `\r`, a timestamp or a `sed` prefix is not at a line start, so `verdict` does not mark it. Same family as debt verdict-rewritten-xcode-output.
- verdict-sgr-in-count: EXEC (bin/verdict) reads raw text, so a colour code inside a count line with a marker reads FAIL. Forced colour only.
- playwright-pass-with-no-tests: `npx playwright test --pass-with-no-tests` exits 0 with no count and reads PASS in `verdict`. Opt-in flag only.
- tier-line-lane-tests-loose: `SessionStartMergeRoleTests` (hooks/test_session_start.py) does not pin "never merges" or the literal `cs-build:lane` tag on the hand-off sentence, so a reworded line that drops either stays green. The line itself is right today. Decision: session-tiers (who started a session decides its merge path)
- tool-list-comment-only-key: `unread_list` (hooks/tool_lists_test.py) reads a comment-only key such as `tools: # list below` as a value, not as an unread list, so the tool-list tests judge that agent with no raise. No agent file has that form. Decision: lanes-start-in-tool-worktrees (lanes start only in the tool's own worktrees).
- can-write-partial-deny: `can_write` (hooks/test_agent_brief.py) reads any write tool in `disallowedTools`, even a scoped `Write(docs/**)` or `Edit` alone, as read-only, though the agent can still write elsewhere. Every agent file denies Edit, Write and NotebookEdit together. Decision: lanes-start-in-tool-worktrees (lanes start only in the tool's own worktrees).
- refuse-print-indirect-output: the refusal cite scan (`_reaches_output`, lint/test_rule_audit.py) misses some refuse prints, so an uncited one passes. It misses a helper that prints its dict argument, `sys.stdout.write`, and `decision` passed as a parameter. Every hook print today is a direct `print(json.dumps(...))`. Step: stop-block-cites
- classifier-refuses-lease-push: in a cloud lane, the auto-mode classifier refused `git push --force-with-lease --force-if-includes` to the lane's own fresh branch as "Git Destructive" (lane child-claude-md-dedupe, 2026-11-03 18:36 CST). That is the exact form the force-push guard's remedy names. Again in lane call-judge-widening, 2026-11-03 23:15 CST: the lane moved to a fresh branch and a new draft PR to fix one commit message. Again in lane red-main-flag-who-types, 2026-11-04 01:40 CST: `Bash` refused an amend and a lease push to the lane's own branch, so two empty trailer commits took its place. Cost: a bad commit subject stays on the branch, or the lane rebuilds on a new branch. Decision: none yet.
- lane-no-own-record-owner-facing: a lane that has not read its own get_session record, or has no remote id, is judged owner-facing, so its send to @parent blocks with the writer route (lint/report_guard.py question_problem). Decision: questions-go-to-owner
- map-fix-trailing-space-dup: map-fix (skills/plain-english-map/scripts/map-fix.mjs) adds an id after trailing spaces on a decisions line, the line then reads as bad, and a second `--write` adds a duplicate line. Main's map pages have no such line, and map-fix writes none. Decision: none yet.
- python-fstring-nested-quote-cite: `blankPython` (scripts/lib/source-text.mjs) reads a nested quote in an f-string such as `f"{d["#"]}"` as a string end, so a later string can read as a cite. A stated limit in that file. Decision: none yet.
- hash-comment-langs-unread: source-text.mjs reads yml, rb and toml files with the JS scanner, so MAP10 finds no cite in their `#` comments. No host maps those extensions today. Decision: none yet.
- map10-scanner-cases-unpinned: decisions.test.mjs has no case for some scanner traps. They are an apostrophe in a Python or shell comment, a shell heredoc, and f, raw or byte strings. It also has none for an id inside a longer token (XD7) or a record in a subfolder. Probes pass. Decision: none yet.
- map-fix-ismain-reason: map-fix.mjs repeats the `isMain` guard of map-check.mjs, and the reason sits only in its commit message, with no note on the ENOENT case. Decision: none yet.
- map10-bad-id-line: MAP10's message for an id that names no record cites the entry's first decisions line, not the line of that id. Decision: none yet.
- cloud-setup-runs-start-hooks: the cloud setup script runs `install.sh --cloud` with no `CS_HOOK_INPUT`, so the startup entries run at snapshot build, and `daily_note.py` stamps that day. Decision: cloud-start-hooks-run-fresh
- cloud-resume-only-hook-stale: a SessionStart entry that fires on resume but not on startup stays in the cloud settings.json and runs the stale snapshot copy. None exists. Decision: cloud-start-hooks-run-fresh
- cloud-start-entries-unread-branch: no case reaches install.sh's "could not read the startup entries" WARNING; the settings jq fails first on the same bad file. Decision: cloud-start-hooks-run-fresh
- reason-flag-rebase-no-record: on a repo whose merge config sets method `rebase`, a reason-flag lift is printed but not kept, because a rebase merge has no merge commit to hold `commit_message` (merge/merge.py `lift_message`). No repo sets rebase today. Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main
- fake-gh-amp-in-field-value: the fake gh in merge/test_merge.py refuses `&` only in a whole argument, not inside a `-f key=value` value, so a `-f q=a&b` that cmd.exe would split on Windows passes on Linux. Decision: none yet.
- ask-stack-keepfail-over-holding: with storage refused, a Send or Undo in flight shows the keepFail hint ("Send it, or copy it") while Send is off, and Copy ask shows for that moment (skills/waiting-page/page.html renderAskForm). Decision: none yet.
- ask-stack-spent-label-narrower: the spent label "Undo send" is 24 px narrower than the live "Undo send (N)". A long not-told line could wrap again when the count ends. Measured at 540, 600 and 1280 wide: the line kept its 44 px height. Decision: none yet.
- ask-stack-undo-window-from-at: Send stamps `at` before its writes, and the 8 s Undo window counts from `at`, so a slow store shortens Undo for a batch. Main does the same for one ask. Decision: none yet.
- ask-stack-reload-mid-undo-dup: a reload after an undoing row is saved to the list, but before its delete lands, leaves the ask both in the store and in the list. A second Send then writes it twice. The design keeps every ask over a reload and accepts a duplicate. Decision: none yet.
- ask-undo-focus-ios-unmeasured: Undo moves focus to the ask box only when the Undo button had focus. A tap in iOS Safari may not focus the button, so focus may still drop to the page there. This was measured only in headless Chromium. Decision: none yet.
- ask-undo-window-real-clock: the in-window Undo hint test (skills/waiting-page/tests/page.test.mjs) asserts `undoLeft > 0` on the real clock with an 8 s window. A slow runner turns it red, never green. Decision: none yet.
- ask-undone-focus-unconditional: after Undo ends, the page focuses the ask box even when the owner typed into another field during a held delete. The same happens on main. Decision: none yet.
- scan-key-unanchored: the waiting-page export's credential-name shape matches inside any word holding secret, token, key or cookie, so `monkey: Banana12345` or `hotkey: Ctrl1Shift2` refuses (review round 2, lane export-scan-addresses). Fix: a word start or camelCase hump before the key word. Decision: waiting-data-in-repo
- scan-key-quadratic: the same shape's unbounded name prefix is quadratic on one long alphanumeric run: 8,000 characters take 1.3 s. Fix: bound the prefix. Decision: waiting-data-in-repo
- scan-address-capital-phrase: a number then Title Case words ending in a street word, such as `Step 4 Main Drive` or `Due 5 PM Lane 2 is open`, refuses as an address. Decision: waiting-data-in-repo
- map-show-symlink-file: map-show (skills/plain-english-map/scripts/map-show.mjs) resolves an absolute path with realpath, so it follows a symlinked code file to its target. The absolute arg then prints the target's map line, and the relative arg prints the link's own line. A tracked symlinked code file is rare. Decision: none yet.
- map-show-path-step-unpinned: no test pins the order in which map-show adds back a path's missing parts, and none sets an 8.3 name or drive case on Linux. Windows CI case 22 is the only proof of that class. Decision: none yet.
- map-show-maplines-filter-copy: `mapLines` in map-check.mjs repeats the `mapCodePaths` filter instead of being its base. Test case 19 pins the two to agree. Decision: none yet.
- own-session-null-text-block: hooks/own_session.py joins list blocks with `b.get("text", "")`, so a block with `"text": null` raises an uncaught TypeError (report_guard crashes, guard_merge fails closed). Real text blocks carry a str. Found in review round 2 of lane guard-reads-wrapped-record, 2026-11-03 21:57 CST. Decision: none yet.
- rerun-in-flight-blocks-clear: a re-run in flight is the newest run of its group. So `cleared()` in `Host.base_state` (merge/merge.py) no longer sees the completed green run under it. An older red run of another group then stays red until the re-run ends. Stricter than before, never greener. Rare: it needs a red run and a re-run in flight in two groups of one file. Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main
- rerun-in-flight-mixed-unknown: the unknown detail can hold a cancelled run and a re-run in flight. Then `red_base` says to wait for the re-run, but the cancelled run stays UNKNOWN after the re-run ends green. Wording only; the merge still refuses. Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main
- opus-plain-ask: `<child-session-event>`, `<standing_owner_message>`, `<channel>`, `<message>` and `<event>` frames are not cut before the plain Opus ask judge (`hooks/shared_names.py` `NOTICE_FRAMES`). Their text is rarely an Opus ask.
- opus-plain-ask: `ASK_LENGTH` in `hooks/override.py` reads `1:30h` as 30h, and "an hour" as the 2h default. These are the owner's own words, so no hole.
- opus-plain-ask: `_owner_text` in `hooks/override.py` reads the record-level `origin` only. A `queued_command` attachment carries `attachment.origin`. Every one seen starts with `<task-notification>`, so no live path.
- opus-plain-ask: a compaction summary and "[Request interrupted by user]" count as owner turns in `_owner_text`. They reach the seed check and the scope read's context.
- map-stale-drops-untracked-cite: map-stale (skills/plain-english-map/scripts/map-stale.mjs) skips a map line whose cited file is untracked or renamed away, and still prints "nothing stale". MAP1 or the not-tracked check fails the same line, so the map check catches it first. Decision: none yet.
- map-stale-own-page-list: map-stale lists map pages with its own readdir, beside `mapFilePaths` in map-check.mjs. Same result today. Decision: none yet.
- map-stale-cost-posix-only: the git call count test (ST9) skips on Windows, so the cost of 4 git calls is proven on Linux and macOS only. Decision: none yet.
- armed-copy-branch-behind-main: `install.sh --check` reads any HEAD hook blob that differs from main as "this branch edits the hook", so a branch cut before main's hook update passes a stale armed copy with a NOTE. A missing copy still refuses. A `git merge-base main HEAD` baseline would close it. Step: hook-installer-armed-copy
- armed-copy-untested-unknowns: no test drives the `git config` failure, `git worktree list` failure or `--git-common-dir` failure branches of `install.sh --check`; a change that dropped them turns nothing red. Step: hook-installer-armed-copy
- armed-copy-dangling-link: a dangling symlink at the armed path reads unknown and the commit lands; the check compares content, not the executable bit. Step: hook-installer-armed-copy
- pr-files-api-first-page: the GitHub PR files API returns 30 files per page. Batch runner 9 read only page 1 of banchi #1042, missed a deletion, and reported a script as left behind (2026-11-03 22:44 CST). A batch runner that reads a PR's files pages through them or reads git. Decision: none yet.
- prose-judge-decisions-folder-fixture: prose_judge refuses any file in a folder named `decisions` that has no Kind line, test fixtures included. Lane map-skill-banchi-gains moved its fixture to `docs/records/` through the config key (2026-11-03 23:53 CST). Decision: none yet.
- builder-diff-merge-main-refused: builder-diff (a builder's commit or push may not hold a test path) refused a builder's commit of a merge of main that brought test paths, on lane prose-governance-workflow (2026-11-03). The orchestrator committed the merge itself, and the resolved files came over through the scratchpad. The orchestrator-never-builds rule (roles-orchestrator-never-builds, global CLAUDE.md) and this guard pull against each other. Cause unmeasured: the suite allows a clean merge of main's test change (merge-of-main cases in hooks/test_guard.py). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests.
- worktree-home-child-clone: an agent's git in a child-repo clone was refused, on lane prose-governance-workflow (2026-11-03). The agent was spawned from a claude-settings cwd, so it got a claude-settings worktree. The lane names worktree-home (an agent stays in its own recorded worktree) as the refuser, but the refusal text was not read. This machine's guard log has no worktree-home line, and the harness worktree-isolation check, whose text is not in hooks/, is the other candidate. The lane used the working route: cd the shell into the child repo, then spawn with isolation worktree. Decision: an-agent-outside-its-home-tree-must-stop.
- classifier-refuses-branch-create: the auto-mode classifier (no hook id: a classifier verdict, not a rule in hooks/) refused Bash branch creation in the degen and q_max clones, and one send_message to a peer session, on lane prose-governance-workflow (2026-11-03). The refused calls were not retried. The coordinator moved that work to other lanes. Decision: auto-mode-owner-acts.
- opus-plain-ask: in a cloud session, an owner's first Opus ask that is refused as a seed is refused again when he repeats it, because `_own_record` skips the earlier copy. Any other owner turn first lets it through.
- opus-plain-ask: a seed record with more than one text block has joined text that differs from the prompt, so `_own_record` misses it and the seed counts as an owner turn.
- opus-plain-ask: the check-in fixtures in `hooks/test_local_time.py` put the record in the transcript, the unmeasured shape, and none sets `isMeta`.
- sim-test-sigkill-leaves-sim-booted: SIGKILL skips the EXIT trap of sim-test, so its simulator (a Duo or the 18 Pro) stays booted while its lease is reaped. A later run of the same role reuses it; a run of the other role refuses (one phone kind at a time), until the simulator is stopped. Decision: sim-lease-tool.
- classifier-refuses-lease-push, again: in lane mutate-guard-cap (2026-11-04 01:35 CST) the auto-mode classifier refused `git push --force-with-lease` to the lane's own branch, which the red-run check's own remedy names. The lane pushed the same trees to a new branch, and PR 1210 became PR 1211. Decision: none yet.
- classifier-refuses-lease-push, third: in lane restore-roadmap-fork-test (2026-11-04 08:30 CST) the auto-mode classifier refused `git push --force-with-lease=<branch>:<sha>` to the lane's own branch, to fix a commit message. Decision stale-safe-force-push allows that push. The lane pushed to a new branch, and PR 1233 was replaced. Decision: refusals-reach-the-owner
- cloud-watchdog-143-reads-cut: without `timeout`, install.sh maps an entry that exits 143 or 137 by itself to 124, so the log says "was cut" for an entry that was not. The log text alone is wrong; the next entry still runs. Decision: cloud-start-hooks-run-fresh
- classifier-refuses-bash-src-build: in lane cc-facts-unknown-cloud (2026-11-04 01:25 CST), the auto-mode classifier refused a reviewer's Bash build of bash 3.2.57 from source, meant to prove install.sh's watchdog on macOS bash. CI shell-macos is the proof instead. Decision: refusals-reach-the-owner
- archive-guard-agent: `lane-closes-after-records` refuses an archive_session that an agent of the session makes, because it reads only the parent session's transcript for the get_session record. The coordinator archives lanes itself. Decision: session-tiers.
- archive-guard-child-repo-read: `lane-closes-after-records` reads the lane repo's open PRs with gh, so a cloud coordinator must add_repo each child repo before it can archive that repo's lanes. Without it, the archive is refused as "could not be read". Decision: session-tiers.
- classifier-refuses-reviewer-probe: in lane push-guard-asks (2026-11-04 00:30 CST) the auto-mode classifier refused a reviewer's Bash probe that was rewritten to hide the word git. The refusal was right; the reviewer stopped that route and probed with stubs. Decision: none yet.
- force-branches-asks: a forced `--branches` push off the default branch now asks instead of denying, because `--branches` reads as `--all` (lane push-guard-asks, review round 3). Claude rarely writes it. Decision: plain-push-asks
- artifactdata-list-others-docs: `ArtifactData list` with `out_dir` saves docs that others wrote, and refuses none; it labels them collaborator-written data. Measured 2026-11-04 02:40 CST on the waiting page's `answers`, 18 docs. Decision: waiting-data-in-repo.
- export-scan-telegram-width: the `telegram-bot-token` shape takes 8 to 10 digits before the colon, after degen's rule; an id of 7 or 11 digits passes. Real id widths unmeasured. Decision: waiting-data-in-repo.
- export-scan-encoded-tokens: a Telegram token with its colon URL-encoded (`%3A`) or HTML-escaped (`&#58;`) passes the scan, and so does `@<digits>:<tail>` in page text, since the version-pin blank eats `@<digits>`. Decision: waiting-data-in-repo.
- export-scan-red-checks-verdict: `assert_refused` in `test_export_page.py` checks the refused verdict, not the kind, so a case can stay red through the wrong shape. Each case hits only its own shape today. Decision: waiting-data-in-repo.
- classifier-refuses-rule-record-writer: in a cloud lane, the auto-mode classifier refused an `Agent` spawn (record-writer) that edits CLAUDE.md and agents/reviewer.md on a coordinator brief, as "Self-Modification" (lane review-fix-check, 2026-11-03 22:30 CST). A rule edit briefed by a peer session needs the owner's grant in the lane chat. Decision: refusals-reach-the-owner.
- lane-history-reword-refused: 2026-11-04, the lane-session-brake lane could not add a `Red-run: none` trailer to its own comment-only commit, because auto mode refused `git filter-branch` on the lane branch as destructive git. The route that works: a new branch from main, with the lane's commits cherry-picked and the trailer added to the copy. Decision: refusals-reach-the-owner (refusals reach the owner).
- stall-stop-summary-race: the Stop check keys on the newest `stop_hook_summary` time, which the harness writes after the hooks end, so a stall that crosses in that gap (about 100 ms) is never listed. Decision: stall-watch.
- stall-listing-lost-on-block: when a sibling hook blocks the same Stop, the stall listing may never reach the model, and the dedupe does not list the job again. Unmeasured. A wrapped job's STALL report reaches the model anyway. Decision: stall-watch.
- launchd-sweep-log-growth: the daily job's stdout log, `<config>/janitor/launchd-sweep.log`, has no cap, and its growth per run is unmeasured. Decision: the-janitor-is-one-machine-wide-sweep.
- agent-end-note-sees-200-rows: the daily note's agent-end check reads only the last 200 rows of the payload log. A 7-day window can hold more runs than that on a busy machine. A run whose rows were cut is not counted. Decision: guard-log-read-weekly.
- agent-end-lock-wait-hides-kill: a started row lost to the payload log's 30 s lock wait (`LOG_WAIT_SECONDS`) hides a killed run from the daily note. Decision: agent-end-reap-stops-what-a-finished-agent-left.
- agent-end-lost-done-reads-killed: a lost done row (debt `agent-end-log-loses-row`) makes a finished run read as killed in the daily note, and the note's line names that cause. Rate unmeasured. Decision: guard-log-read-weekly.
- run-log-key-case-on-mac: `janitor/sweep.py` `run_log_key` says it matches a differently cased path, but `normcase` folds case only on Windows. On macOS, a root typed in another case reads `none` in the preview. Decision: the-janitor-is-one-machine-wide-sweep.
- run-log-key-untested: no test pins the trim's use of `run_log_key`. The reader merges spellings, so no wrong line shows today. Decision: the-janitor-is-one-machine-wide-sweep.
- run-log-lock-thread-only: `_RUN_LOG_LOCK` guards a thread race that `_pmap` does not make on a `--confirm` run, since the root pool exists only for previews. The real race, two processes writing at once, is unguarded. Decision: the-janitor-is-one-machine-wide-sweep.
- blind-count-marks: in lane lane-word-rename (review round 2, reviewer sonnet, 2026-11-04 09:22 CST), `BLIND_COUNT` in hooks/blind_brief.py does not strip leading markdown marks, though `KIND_LINE` does. So `- Designer kind: informed` with `- Blind designers: 2` is refused for a missing count line. Cost: one retry. The refusal names the plain form. Decision: blind-briefs-use-a-template.
- kind-line-pins: in lane lane-word-rename (review round 2, reviewer sonnet, 2026-11-04 09:22 CST), no test pins `\s*:` in `KIND_LINE` or `>` in `LEADING_MARKS` in hooks/blind_brief.py, and no hook-level case spawns a decorated informed brief. Decision: blind-briefs-use-a-template.
- notes-blind-lanes-cite: in lane lane-word-rename (review round 2, reviewer sonnet, 2026-11-04 09:22 CST), older lines in this file, such as blind-brief-count-unchecked and blind-lane-on-redraw, name the header `Blind lanes:`. The header is now `Blind designers:`. The old lines stay as history. Decision: blind-briefs-use-a-template.
- lane-call-wording: in lane lane-word-rename (review round 2, reviewer sonnet, 2026-11-04 09:22 CST), comments in hooks/test_guard.py still say "lane call" beside "start call" for START_TOOLS calls. Decision: none yet.
- export-all-step-test-pins-wording: the test of the "Before you switch Claude accounts" step 2 in `test_export_page.py` reads `out_dir` after "set to" or "=", and the "make ... by hand" sentence; a reword such as "Create the folder by hand" goes red, with the cause line shown. Decision: waiting-data-in-repo.
- cloud-released-lock-stuck: in a cloud session, a released merge lock ref (refs/heads/merge-lock/main, q_max, 2026-11-04 03:44 CST) blocked every later merge with "another run took the merge lock first". The cloud lock (GitLock) re-takes a released lock with a leased push and no delete. But it read every failed push as a rival run and dropped the push output. So the cause is unmeasured. A lease push of an orphan commit to a session branch lands through the proxy (probe, 2026-11-04 03:58 CST). The auto-mode classifier refused both clears, `gh api -X DELETE .../git/refs/heads/merge-lock/main` and `merge --unlock main`. Fixed in part by PR merge-lock-retake: a failed push with the ref unchanged now stops and prints the push output. Read that output on the next refusal. Decision: merge-lock.
- lock-retake-reread-hides-push: when the re-read after a failed lock push fails, its error replaces the push output, so the refusal text is lost (merge/merge.py GitLock.acquire). Fix: append the push output. Decision: merge-lock.
- lock-retake-net-reads-refused: a failed lock push that never reached the remote (a network drop) with the ref unchanged prints "the remote refused". Fix: NetStop when is_net says so (merge/merge.py GitLock.acquire). Decision: merge-lock.
- lock-retake-stale-reread: a stale ls-remote after a rival's take reads as Stop, not Held. A caching proxy could cause it; unmeasured (merge/merge.py GitLock.acquire). Decision: merge-lock.
- lane-send-message-refused: lane merge-lock-retake sent a peer session a hand force-push recipe for refs/heads/merge-lock/main; auto mode refused send_message, correctly. The resend without the recipe landed. The report guard then asks the owner to allow the tool, a false alarm when the refusal was right (lint/report_guard.py). Same family as lane-create-session-refused. Decision: refusals-reach-the-owner.
- merge-body-unlink-untested: no test fails if `Host.api` stops deleting its `--input` temp file (merge/merge.py `Host.api`). Decision: none yet.
- gh-cmd-shim-claim-split: the `Host.api` docstring says gh on Windows runs through a `.cmd` wrapper, but the GhLockTest skip says real gh.exe is native. If the wrapper is real, `GhLock.api` still sends the lock's two-line message as `-f`. Decision: none yet.
- alarm-renamed-workflow-unknown: after a workflow file rename, actions/ci-alarm/alarm.sh reads the workflow at its current path, gets a 404 and prints unknown, never red; merge/merge.py reads the run's own path. Decision: no-merge-onto-red-main.
- docs-only-design-fail-open: a `docs-only` review record that has rounds and a last design-reviewer FAIL or PARTIAL now reads open, where main read done. A docs-only change rarely gets a design review (lane merge-design-fail-open, review round 3). Decision: pr-review-record
- review-state-docstring-width: the `review_state` docstring in merge/merge.py has a 233-character line. Width lint does not flag it today (lane merge-design-fail-open, review round 3). Decision: pr-review-record
- auto-mode-force-push-lane: the auto-mode classifier refused `git push --force-with-lease` on a lane's own branch (2026-11-04 09:17 CST), though check_red_run's printed remedy names that push. The lane moved to a new branch and PR instead (#1240 replaces #1237). Decision: refusals-reach-the-owner.
- session-end-flag-text: test comments (test_install_launchd.py:141, test_install_schtasks.py:143, test_session_end_sweep.py:729-743, test_sweep.py:1722 and 1903) and the `--session-end` help say the flag scopes the run to one repository or narrate history. The flag only skips the loose-process report; the root argument sets the scope. Round-3 review, PR #1240. Decision: the-janitor-is-one-machine-wide-sweep.
- materialize-all-builds-every-fixture: materialize_all builds ~30 fixtures (~6 s) whenever any log check is picked; 253 of 543 log checks read ROOT; a per-check audit could skip builds (hooks/test_guard.py). Decision: none yet.
- frozen-path-refuses-verdict: frozen-path refused `verdict` 4 times in lane guard-fixture-cost (guard.log 09:38-10:31 CST); possible false alarm, unread. Decision: guard-that-cries-wolf-is-spent.
- credential-letters-end-anchor: the letters-only branch needs the value to end at a line end, `,`, `;` or `}`, after an optional closing quote. `f(api_key="<letters>")` passes. A value followed by a comment, a URL or `&` passes (`API_KEY=<letters> # prod`), and so does one with 3 lower-case letters then a capital. Main refused none of these. Decision: waiting-data-in-repo.
- credential-letters-brace-untested: no RED case in `test_export_page.py` ends a letters-only credential value right before `}`. So a `credential-name` shape without `}` in its end class stays green. A hump bound of 4 instead of 3 stays green too. Decision: waiting-data-in-repo.
- ci-job-lint-skips-make: `ci-job --lint` does not run `make` steps (degen runs 1 of 11). Its summary line says so. Running them waits on a degen cost measure.
- shared-tree-scratch-repo-false-alarm: the shared-tree guard (hooks/guard_shared_tree.py) refused `git reset --hard` and `git reset --merge` in a throwaway repo that this session created under its scratchpad. The refusal came after a `cd` into that repo, in one Bash call. The repo is no shared tree. Seen 2026-11-04 07:59 CST, lane guard-reset-merge-keep. Decision: agent-worktree-discard-is-noted.
- shared-tree-script-file-unread: the same reset commands, written into a script file and run as `bash <file>`, passed the shared-tree guard (hooks/guard_shared_tree.py) unread. Seen 2026-11-04 07:59 CST, lane guard-reset-merge-keep. Decision: agent-worktree-discard-is-noted.
- replay-select-root-dot: a select script written `./x` at the repo root passes the "/" gate, so `_head_copy` extracts the whole HEAD tree for it. The rows stay right; only the extract is large. No profile names one. Decision: mutation-report-scores-suites.
- opus-plain-ask-one-role-exclusion: a one-role exclusion with an unlisted word, such as "leave reviewers out", grants the excluded role, as on main (`_ask_scope` in hooks/override.py). Decision: owner-override (an owner override lets a refused or asked act through).
- opus-plain-ask-scope-kill: a hook kill in the scope read leaves the every-role file with no `opus-grant` line and no owner-visible line (hooks/override.py `grant_opus_ask`). `opus_ask` measured 2.1 to 2.2 s per call, 3 runs, 2026-11-04 (hooks/call_judge.py). Decision: owner-override (an owner override lets a refused or asked act through).
- reply-undo-hidden-by-fold: a fold during a reply's 8 s Undo count hides Undo until the card unfolds. The reply stays sent and shows under the fold (skills/waiting-page/page.html askItem). Decision: none yet.
- reply-send-clears-ask-line: a reply send clears the Ask box's result line, so an earlier "Undo failed" line goes. Nothing is lost (skills/waiting-page/page.html sendAsk). Decision: none yet.
- reply-owner-falls-to-writer: askOwner falls back to the writer's name, so a wake for an answer with no owner says "answered by <writer>". Only bad data hits it (skills/waiting-page/page.html askOwner). Decision: none yet.
- reply-midbox-edit-keeps-sent: an edit inside the reply box during its save keeps the whole box as the draft, sent words too, so a re-send repeats them. No words are lost (skills/waiting-page/page.html sendAsk). Decision: none yet.
- reply-close-drops-late-keys: keys typed after the reply box closes on a save are dropped. The words typed before the close are kept (skills/waiting-page/page.html sendAsk). Decision: none yet.
- batch-runner-classifier-refusals: on 2026-11-04 05:10 CST the auto-mode classifier refused two acts of the claude-settings batch runner. It refused `Bash` for `merge 1224 --confirm --base-red-ok "<reason>"`, which decision no-merge-onto-red-main lets Claude type for a fix batch. It refused `mcp__claude-code-remote__delete_trigger` on its own check-in. Both went to the owner (decision refusals-reach-the-owner). On 2026-11-04 15:28 CST it refused `Bash` again, for `merge 1220 --confirm --base-red-ok "<reason>"`, after the owner granted that exact act in chat to the coordinator. It went to the owner the same way.
- classifier-refuses-relayed-ruling: the auto-mode classifier refused a `SendMessage` to a running record-writer that passed on an owner page ruling relayed by triage (lane review-fix-check, 2026-11-05 07:53 CST). The owner then typed the ruling in the lane. Triage filed the gap as step owner-word-relay on the owner's page. Decision: refusals-reach-the-owner.
- alts-accessible-name: the `.alts` buttons (None of these, Mark done, Park) are named by their label only. `docs/design/a11y.md` wants the act and its target, such as "Park No. 27". The card's `aria-labelledby` names the item (skills/waiting-page/page.html, the card renderer). Decision: none yet.
- bar-stay-open-plural: the Send bar reads "1 stay open." for one item, where it should read "1 stays open". Three tests in skills/waiting-page/tests/page.test.mjs pin the current text (skills/waiting-page/page.html renderBar). Decision: none yet.
- park-turn-start-read: the writer's turn-start read of `answers/` (the fallback when no wake comes) runs only while `meta/page.parked` is not empty or the writer owns an open item. The first Park press, with neither true, waits for a wake (skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md, "Read the answers"). Decision: none yet.
- daily-note-unknown-long-root: `hooks/test_daily_note.py` `test_a_payload_log_that_cannot_be_read_is_reported_as_unknown` asserts "unknown". Past a 116-character temp root, the line swaps to its short head and drops "its state is unknown". macOS and Windows temp paths stay shorter. Decision: guard-log-read-weekly.
- wait-pid-timing-flake: a wait-pid timing check (lint/test_wait_pid.py) in the lint suite failed once, then passed alone on rerun. Seen 2026-11-04, lane guard-reset-merge-keep. Decision: none yet.
- prose-judge-decisions-fixture: possible false alarm. prose_judge (hooks/prose_judge.py) refuses any file in a folder named `decisions` that has no Kind line, test fixtures included. Seen 2026-11-05, lane map-skill-banchi-gains. Decision: none yet.
- secret-scan-dash-prefix: the shared secret scan (skills/waiting-page/export_page.py) passes a token that follows a `-` directly, in the sk-, Stripe, Docker, Google and JWT forms. Seen 2026-11-05, lane degen-export-to-shared round 3 (degen PR 434). Decision: waiting-data-in-repo.
- secret-scan-google-prefix: the google-key pattern in the same scan needs the `AIzaSy` prefix, so only a made-up key escapes it. Seen 2026-11-05, lane degen-export-to-shared round 3 (degen PR 434). Decision: waiting-data-in-repo.
- classifier-refuses-allow-brief: 2026-11-05 07:47 CST the auto-mode classifier refused the coordinator's send_message brief asking a lane to add an autoMode.allow entry (owner item 257 c), as Self-Modification; the owner pastes such entries himself. Decision: refusals-reach-the-owner.
- red-run-remedy-route-limits: the red-run check's cherry-pick route (lint/check_red_run.py main) stops on a range that holds a merge ("is a merge but no -m option"); `git cherry-pick --abort` recovers, and nothing is lost. Several flagged commits need the route once each. `--amend -m` drops the Co-Authored-By and Claude-Session lines. The route test asserts the word `cherry-pick` only, and its force regexes miss combined short flags such as `-uf`. Review round 2 of lane lease-push-c-and-d, 2026-11-05. Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits.
- wait-copy-uses-stood-pick: Copy answers on an item answered by its wait that also has `stands` copies the stood pick, not `lastAnswer.choice` (skills/waiting-page/page.html answerLine). Needs a `lastAnswer` that differs from the pick. Decision: none yet.
- wait-badge-outside-answered-untested: no case pins "Answered, Sent" on an item answered by its wait outside the Answered section, such as one grouped with an open mate (skills/waiting-page/page.html buildCard). Decision: none yet.
- wait-ended-reads-answered: an item whose wait ended reads as answered until the writer's next wake runs `stale_pages.py --items` (docs/design/page-item-waits-notes.md section 5). Decision: page-stale-publish.
- wait-gh-timeout-untested: the per-call gh time cap in `stale_pages.py --items` has no test (skills/waiting-page/stale_pages.py ask_gh). Decision: page-stale-publish.
- page-grant-routing-pick: SKILL.md "A decision answer is clear only when page_grant.py would print counted" is unclear for the routing session, which has no relayed pick; run without `--pick` the checker says not counted, so the item is re-asked. It also needs a `--new-fetch` folder, which the routing steps never make. Fails safe (skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md "Read the answers"). Decision: relayed-grant-read-from-page.
- page-grant-usage-error-keeps-folder: a page_grant.py usage error, such as a missing `--item`, exits 2 before the stamp is used up, so the folder stays checkable. No test covers it (skills/waiting-page/page_grant.py main). Decision: relayed-grant-read-from-page.
- relay-kept-answer-counted: a kept answer still reads `counted` after the receiver ran the act, until the owning session closes the item. "Never re-run" is prose only. The per-turn `get_session` read of the receiver has no test (skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md "Route and close"). Decision: relayed-grant-read-from-page.
- relay-lane-blank-untested: no test covers `at: ""` or a whitespace-only `lane`; `wait_lines` prints a lane line for `lane: "  "`, which page.html's `laneOf` trims to none (skills/waiting-page/stale_pages.py). Decision: relayed-grant-read-from-page.
- relay-idle-receiver-no-status: a receiver that went idle (status_bucket completed) with no done report is neither `ended` nor `unknown`, so no dead-receiver rule fires; the page's lane line still shows the wait (skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md "Route and close"). Decision: relayed-grant-read-from-page.
- relay-park-receiver-untold: on a Park, a receiver lookup that finds no session or cannot run (status `unknown`) still closes the item and drops `lane` with no tell. The miss lives only in the writer's report, so a live receiver waits with no word until its `page_grant.py` check fails closed (skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md park branch). Decision: relayed-grant-read-from-page.
- park-tell-resent-on-retry: a retry after a failed pinned close write sends the writer's withdrawn tell again. The `ended` status is not named for the tell (skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md park branch). Decision: relayed-grant-read-from-page.
- park-second-tell-test-narrow: test_skill_park_relay.py case 3 flags an owning-session second tell only when "park" sits in the same or the previous un-negated sentence, so "The owning session sends a second tell." at SKILL.md's park branch stays green (skills/waiting-page/test_skill_park_relay.py). Decision: relayed-grant-read-from-page.
- relay-chat-after-kept-answer: a chat answer typed after a kept page answer closes the item and skips the delete, so `answers/<id>` is left behind. It fails safe: a closed item is never counted (skills/waiting-page/SKILL.md "Route and close"). Decision: relayed-grant-read-from-page.
- mappaths-prefix-no-slash: a `mapPaths` prefix with no trailing `/`, such as `src`, also covers `src-data/`, so MAP1 and MAP7 read more than meant (skills/plain-english-map/scripts/map-check.mjs inMapPaths). Seen 2026-11-05, lane map7-scope-mappaths. Decision: map-facts-are-checked.
- mutation-report-action-untested: `actions/mutation-report/action.yml` has no `test_action.sh` of its own, as `actions/ci-alarm/` has; its two run blocks were run by hand only (lane mutation-ci-report, 2026-11-05). Decision: mutation-ci-report-and-replay.
- changed-log-case-misses-git: the `--changed` log case in `hooks/test_mutate_ci.py` compares the tree and status only, so a no-op `_drop_checkout` keeps it green; the replay suite covers the shared helper. Decision: mutation-ci-report-and-replay.
- budget-is-per-part: `replay_profile` gives each part of a parts profile (banchi) its own budget, so its `--changed` run can last 2 times the budget; claude_settings has one part. Decision: mutation-ci-report-and-replay.
- tests-for-pools-scopes: `_loads_module` pools assignments from every scope, so a name bound to `<stem>.py` in one function and passed to a loader in another maps. So `--tests-for hooks/guard_merge.py` prints `hooks/test_guard.py` (lines 18658 and 4583), which does run guard_merge through guard.py. No fixture pins the pooling. Decision: mutation-ci-report-and-replay.
- underscore-test-is-product: the claude_settings `code_not` skips `test_*.py` and `_test_*.py` only, so a `*_test.py` file such as `hooks/case_runner_test.py` reads as product and gets mutants (hooks/mutate_guard.py REPLAY_PROFILES). Kept as main's behaviour, lane mutation-scope-skills, 2026-11-05. Decision: mutation-ci-report-and-replay.
- stale-pages-maps-guard-suite: `--tests-for skills/waiting-page/stale_pages.py` also prints `hooks/test_guard.py`, so each stale_pages mutant also runs the slow guard suite. Seen 2026-11-05, lane mutation-scope-skills. Decision: mutation-ci-report-and-replay.
- tested-by-label-near-miss: `  - Tested-by:` (a hyphen) is read as no line, so the claim goes unchecked; `Decisions:` has the same exact-label rule (skills/plain-english-map/scripts/map-check.mjs subLineEntries). Seen 2026-11-05, lane map-tested-by-line. Decision: map-tested-by-line.
- tested-by-data-string: a path string that is only data, such as `DATA = "src/x.py"`, passes MAP11 as a load. Decision: map-tested-by-line.
- tested-by-subheading: a `### ` heading inside `## Code files` ends the section for MAP11, so a tested-by line under it fails as misplaced (skills/plain-english-map/scripts/map-check.mjs subLineEntries). Decision: map-tested-by-line.
- own-model-unquoted-forms: the agent-body rule in `lint/check_agent_models.py` lets through an unbackticked header such as "first line is FAIL, round 2 of 3, sonnet." and "runs on the frontmatter model, haiku"; the incident form (backticked, "your ... model") is caught (review round 1 of lane reviewer-spawn-model). It also lets through "your frontmatter model, e.g. haiku.", since the sentence ends at "e.g." (review round 2). Decision: agent-model-cap.
- model-slot-phrase-loose: the same rule counts "the model" in any PASS/FAIL sentence as a model slot, and agent-model-cap names only `<model>`. It misses a sentence end before a closing quote. It accepts "powered by the" and "model named" split across a heading and a paragraph. It flags "If your brief names a model above Sonnet, stop.", since any "your" before "model" counts (review round 2) (review round 1 and fix check of lane reviewer-spawn-model). Decision: agent-model-cap.
- known-defects-deps-argv: `--known-defects` never fills `{deps}` in a defect's argv (hooks/mutate_shared.py _known_defect), so a part with `"link": None` (banchi py, degen) installs into a folder no catcher can name and reads UNKNOWN. Seen 2026-11-05, lane known-defects-install. Decision: mutation-ci-report-and-replay.
- known-defects-install-false-red: a failed install or link in `--known-defects` turns every defect UNKNOWN and the job red, even when no catcher needs a package (hooks/mutate_shared.py run_known_defects). The action takes a profile name only, so a caller cannot opt out; banchi, ios-adblock and joyous are exposed (review round 3 of lane known-defects-install). Decision: mutation-ci-report-and-replay.
- workflow-drops-per-file: `workflow_drops_tests` compares one file's test paths before and after. So the guard refuses three acts as a dropped test: a delete of a workflow that runs tests, a rename of it, and a move of a test step to another workflow file (hooks/guard.py `workflow_drops_tests`, called by `workflow_builder_refused`). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests.
- workflow-fragment-write-passes: a write-tool Edit fragment with no `run:` line, such as `name:` plus `continue-on-error: true`, passes at the write tool, because `workflow_disables_step` reads only `run:` lines of the fragment. Commit, push and stop refuse the same change on the full file (hooks/guard.py `workflow_disables_step`, `workflow_pairs`). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests.
- workflow-addopts-env-unread: `PYTEST_ADDOPTS: --ignore=...` under a step's `env:` is not read, since the guard reads `run:` lines only (hooks/guard.py `workflow_run_commands`). Main does not read it either. Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests.
- test-author-run-forms-refused: a test-author cannot add `pytest tests/`, `python -m pytest`, `npm test`, or a step with an `env:` key. `workflow_run_is_test` accepts only an interpreter and a test path. `workflow_test_step` accepts only the keys in `WORKFLOW_STEP_KEYS`. A builder may add them (hooks/guard.py `workflow_run_is_test`, `workflow_test_step`). Decision: builders-cannot-edit-tests.
- verdict-log-path-space: VERDICT_LOG_RE reads `log=(\S+)`, so a verdict log path with a space is not matched and that run's log is not read (lint/report_guard.py VERDICT_LOG_RE).
- shared-tag-pin-partial: SharedTagNames pins REPO_TAG, STEP_TAG and repo_tag_names by identity only, so a local copy of TOPIC_TAG, BUILD_TAG or lane_head_refs stays green (skills/roadmap/test_build.py SharedTagNames).
- roadmap-page-date-untested: skills/roadmap/tests/page.test.mjs has no case for index.html's date and time labels (`when`, `timeLabel`, lines 452-455).
- test-build-pin-comment-stale: skills/roadmap/test_build.py line 60 says "the page the old script used"; that comment is stale.
