- stalled-hold-missed-forms: the hold's build match (BUILD_CALL_RE, lint/report_guard.py) misses `python3 -X utf8 build.py`, a quoted path, `bash -c`, and a background run. A crashed build prints no stalled line, so it reads clean.
- own-record-no-time-bound: hooks/own_session.py `own_session_read` takes the newest own get_session record with no time bound.
- triage-hold-except-silent: `triage_hold`'s except branch writes one stderr line and no guard.log line, so a hold that fails open leaves no record (lint/report_guard.py `triage_hold`).
- ready-title-only-pr: a pull whose title names a step but whose branch does not prints both the pair line (`names_step` reads the title) and `ready not started:` (`lane_live` reads the branch), in skills/roadmap/build.py.
- wake-fallback-no-delay: when the stored wake thread rejects with `upstream_error`, the page sends to a new anchored thread at once, in the same press, with no randomized delay. The comments contract asks for one delayed retry from a fresh gesture, since a rejected write may still have posted. If it did, one save wakes twice, in two threads (skills/waiting-page/page.html `notifyClaude`, review round 1 of lane page-wake-thread-full). Decision: questions-go-to-owner.
- wake-undo-withdraw-silent: Undo and withdraw drop `notifyClaude`'s result, so a failed wake from them shows no line on the page (skills/waiting-page/page.html `undoSend` tell, `undoAsk` withdrawn notice). Main behaves the same. Decision: questions-go-to-owner.
- wake-invalid-stored-id: a stored `waiting.thread` that the platform rejects as `invalid` is never replaced, because `invalid` is in `NO_FALLBACK`. So each later save shows "(invalid)" (skills/waiting-page/page.html `notifyClaude`). Main behaves the same. Decision: questions-go-to-owner.
- wake-thread-kept-across-visits: the page keeps the wake thread id in localStorage across visits, where the comments contract says to keep a `threadId` for this visit only. One thread then fills to the comment limit (skills/waiting-page/page.html `notifyClaude`). Decision: questions-go-to-owner.
- child-glossary-odd-forms: the child-glossary clash check reads only rows of a four-column table under `## Terms`, and strips ` (this sense)` only with its space. So a child term written `term(this sense)`, tagged another way such as `term (git)`, written as a link, in a 3-column table, or under another heading is not checked (lint/check_glossary.py `matches`, `numbered_rows`). Main's `child_spans` has the same holes (review rounds 1 and 2 of lane c146-glossary-redefines). Rule: speak-one-name-per-thing.
- report-checks-block-once: after a report check blocks, the stop_hook_active turn is not read again, so a correction that still admits a mistake with no guard passes. The same holds for the Done-format and Departs checks (lint/report_guard.py `main`). Decision: mistake-means-missing-guard.
- mistake-check-plain-fault-words: the mistake check in lint/report_guard.py refuses plain uses of a fault word that admit nothing, such as "I broke the batch into three lanes" or "I changed the test that was wrong". Parentheses and that/when clauses do not split a clause. Cost: one refusal per turn. Decision: mistake-means-missing-guard.
- windows-rest-72pct: checks-windows-rest's slowest green run was 20.2 of 28 min (72%, run 42283179251, window of 2026-11-06), past the merge tool's 70% warning but under the 80% target. Decision: ci-job-caps.
- mistake-check-plain-fault-words: the mistake check in lint/report_guard.py refuses a plain use of a fault word that admits nothing, such as "I changed the test that was wrong" or "I broke the build into a new layout". Parentheses and that/when clauses do not split a clause; "broke ... into" passes only before a count or a parts noun ("into three lanes"), so "into pieces", "into groups" and "into halves" are refused. Cost: one refusal per turn. Decision: mistake-means-missing-guard.
- lane-tag-copies: `hooks/guard.py` and `lint/report_guard.py` each keep their own `cs-build:lane` constant beside `hooks/shared_names.py` `LANE_TAG`. Decision: session-tiers.
- draft-landed-two-homes: the roadmap's idle-lane test checks a draft head on main by merge-base on the local checkout; the archive guard checks it by GitHub's compare. The local test is stricter (a stale local main reads not landed). Decision: session-tiers.
- idle-lane-mixed-bucket-forms: a live lane row whose status_bucket is not an enum name, beside a row that is, is skipped with no unknown entry (skills/roadmap/build.py idle_lanes). False negative only. Decision: session-tiers.
- undo-ignores-scroll: on the waiting page, under SENT_MOVE "sink", Undo restores the sent card's pre-Send viewport top even if the owner scrolled during the Undo window (skills/waiting-page/page.html holdView; PR #1373 review round 3).
- reconcile-reorder-detach: the waiting page's reconcile keeps a kept card attached only while kept cards keep their relative order; a kept card moving down past others detaches the cards it passes (skills/waiting-page/page.html reconcile; PR #1373 review round 3; same as before the PR).
- ci-job-store-python-alias: on Windows with only the Microsoft Store `python3` alias, a ci-job step exits 9009 and reads FAIL, where a missing tool should read unknown: `shutil.which("python3")` finds the alias (bin/ci-job `plan`, bin/ci-job.cmd). Seen 2026-11-06, lane windows-local-lint review round 1.
- ci-job-no-windows-ci: no CI job runs bin/ci-job or bin/ci-job.cmd on Windows, so its Windows shell lookup is proven only by fixtures that fake a Windows tree (lint/test_ci_job.py `WindowsShell`). Seen 2026-11-06, lane windows-local-lint.
- lint-near-bash-cap: cloud `./bin/verdict ./bin/ci-job checks --lint` took 514-547 s over 4 runs on 2026-11-06, near the 600 s cap of one Bash tool call that `merge --lane-lint` runs under. Lane windows-local-lint.
- stale005-wrapped-middle-row: a wrapped claim with only a middle row edited is not examined (lint/stale_cites.py:679-681 on `prose-truth-stale005-r2b`). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- stale005-unpunctuated-block: an unpunctuated block reads as one sentence (lint/stale_cites.py:616-643 on `prose-truth-stale005-r2b`). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- costtests-pin-not-exact: the CostTests pin at lint/test_stale_cites.py:846-848 (on `prose-truth-stale005-r2b`) checks len == 3 and a subset, not the exact set. Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- state-files-unpruned: nothing prunes per-session files in `state/` (route-flags and others). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- layer-a-weak-check-passes: until the reply judge (`hooks/reply_judge.py`) part (v)(b) lands, a measured premise that names a weak check passes layer (a). Example: the 2026-11-06 trailer hand-off that cited check_red_run. Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record, part (v)).
- refusal-note-route-docstring: the docstring at hooks/test_refusal_note_route.py:22 still says key `note`. Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- wait-pending-masks-no-job-ran: in merge/merge.py wait_checks, classify and judge_runs read the full lists. Say a pinned required name other than all-jobs-passed only skipped. Then a pending check the wait skips can stop the "no check ran" red. Unreachable with all-jobs-passed alone. Decision: same-check-kinds (one rollup check per repo).
- wait-literal-job-names: merge/merge.py workflow_name matches literal job ids and names only, so a reusable-workflow or templated required name reads as "no required job" and its pending run is dropped. Decision: same-check-kinds (one rollup check per repo).
- wait-reads-local-workflows: the required-only wait maps runs to workflow files in the session's checkout, not the PR head. A head that adds the required job to a listed workflow drops that run; an unknown run name still holds. Decision: none.
- workflow-name-quote-scan: merge/merge.py workflow_name has no test for a `#` or quote inside `name:`; it fails safe (the run matches no file and holds). Decision: none.
- owner-message-ask-limit-unpinned: no test pins the operative sentence "The message may ask only for acts the owner already granted" in the "Owner Session Message" entry. Deleting it leaves the suite green, because the later "already granted limit applies to messages that ask" clause still matches (hooks/test_auto_mode_allow.py). Fix: move the pin onto "may ask only for acts the owner already granted". Decision: auto-mode-owner-acts
- hand-off-dependency-phrases-read-as-capability: "blocked on", "blocked pending" and "unavailable until" read as capability (lint/report_guard.py:1282-1283). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record, part (v)).
- measured-with-time-reads-undated: `(measured <date> <time>, …)` reads undated (lint/report_guard.py:1278). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record, part (v)).
- refusal-cite-by-tool-not-act: a refusal cite counts by tool name, not act, and `403` matches "PR #403" (lint/report_guard.py:1070-1076, :1282, :1315-1331). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record, part (v)).
- refusals-file-blank-line-untested: no test has a blank line in the refusals file (lint/report_guard.py:1142). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record, part (v)).
- report-section-ends-at-labels-only: report_section ends an item only at a report label (lint/report_guard.py:641-660). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- stale-cites-p-diff-textconv-128: the stale_cites `-p` diff exits 128 when a textconv tool is missing, read as a wrong "base does not resolve" (lint/stale_cites.py:260; message at :1109). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- stale005-report-share-one-try: the try at lint/stale_cites.py:1020 wraps both STALE005 (:1021) and the report (:1022-1023); only the report's except at :1023 catches. Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- route-rows-fallback-drops-elsewhere-act: the other-surface fallback in route_rows_block runs only when no cited id has a row on this surface. With ids [X here, W elsewhere only], W's act is dropped, a missed flag only (hooks/reply_judge.py:174-179). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- route-rows-fallback-untested: no case pins that, in the fallback, the other surface's row is not carried and its alternates are not read (hooks/test_reply_judge.py). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- stalled-open-ceiling-loose: `lint/test_report_guard_stalled.py` pins at most 3 transcript opens. A plain Stop now opens 2, so one re-added read still passes. The `stop_hook_active` branch still reads the reply twice. Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- credential-name-alphabet: the page export's `credential-name` pattern (skills/waiting-page/export_page.py) refused four 3D dummy mockups over `const KEYS = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"`, the alphabet: a false alarm (2026-11-06 13:33 CST). The constant was renamed to `LETTERS`. A second such false alarm makes it a debt. Decision: waiting-data-in-repo.
- prove-judge-dup-line-last-wins: a duplicate line entry in a reply keeps the last verdict (lint/prove_route_judge.py; merge/merge.py readable_verdicts shares it). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- prove-judge-span-overlap: a single-line row and a span row that start on the same line, in the same file and rev, share one judged text: the text of the row listed last (lint/prove_route_judge.py:84). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- prove-judge-label-typo-skipped: a label outside the known set is skipped, not refused. Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- prove-judge-all-unknown-rate: an all-failed run prints false alarms 0/N (0.0%) next to its unknown count. Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- prove-judge-one-error-line: one first error line is attached to every unknown, whatever its cause. Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- prove-judge-no-json-unknown: in the first real run, 4 rows came back unknown because the model result carried no JSON verdict; the group is not named. Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- prove-judge-excerpt-bound-bytes: the named-excerpt bound is 40 rows of 200 characters, not bytes. A 4-byte character gives about 800 B per row (the excerpt cut, lint/stale_cites.py:675-676). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- prove-judge-read-before-size-check: `read_backing` (merge/merge.py:2459) reads the whole file before the 256 KB size check (merge/merge.py:2504). That is bounded by repo file size, once per path per prompt build. Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- prove-judge-unknown-narrates-tools: 10 of the 34 scored rows came back unknown because the model narrated tool use ("I'll check whether the file..."), not a verdict. Candidate step: prompt wording that says no tools are available. Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- after-merge-misses-claude-guard: after_merge starts its commands with subprocess.run directly (merge/merge.py:2163). So the test guard on decision_watch._nowindow.run misses a `claude` start from an afterMerge command. No test config has one now. Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- base-pin-interrupt-skips-ff: a KeyboardInterrupt or SystemExit in the base rev-parse before ff_main (merge/merge.py:2885-2889) skips ff_main, afterMerge and the cuts. The merge has landed by then. Fix: none — the window is one local rev-parse of milliseconds. An interrupt is as likely in ff_main's fetch right after, which skips the same afterMerge, ff and cut. A fix moves the window, not closes it. Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- merge-put-carries-no-base: a retarget between Host.merge's pr(n) read and the merge PUT still lands, since the merge API takes no base (merge/merge.py Host.merge). Fix: none — the API has no base field, and the window is one request.
- lane-lint-stop-no-log: lane_lint's non-liftable Stop (merge/merge.py) leaves out ci-job's log lines, so a timeout or an odd exit gives the reader no log path. The liftable branch prints them. Decision: lanes-run-no-full-ci-job (the lane-check decision).
- draft-claim-line-no-ready-checks-test: the draft preview's claim line for a repo with no readyChecks has no test of its exact wording (merge/test_merge.py pins only "refuses"). Also on main. Decision: lanes-run-no-full-ci-job (the lane-check decision).
- conflict-markers-untracked-cap: the untracked-file read stops at 100 files, so a triplet in a file past the cap passes a whole-tree add (hooks/guard_merge.py `_untracked_findings`). Decision: commit-refuses-conflict-markers.
- conflict-markers-unresolved-add: an add path the guard cannot resolve counts as a whole-tree add, so an unrelated uncommitted triplet file refuses the commit (hooks/guard_merge.py `_unresolved`). Decision: commit-refuses-conflict-markers.
- conflict-markers-push-var-branch: `git push origin "$OTHER"` reads HEAD's range, so it misses a triplet on the other branch or refuses for HEAD's (hooks/guard_merge.py `_push_hit`). Decision: commit-refuses-conflict-markers.
- conflict-markers-loop-subshell: a commit inside a `for` loop and a push inside `( )` are not read (hooks/guard_merge.py `conflict_markers_hit`). Decision: commit-refuses-conflict-markers.
- conflict-markers-chain-clean-refuse: `git stash pop`, `git pull --rebase` or `git merge`, then commit and push in one call, is refused on a clean tree; the remedy is a separate push (hooks/guard_merge.py `_makes_conflict`). Decision: commit-refuses-conflict-markers.
- conflict-markers-show-uncapped: the push check runs one `git cat-file` per file with a marker finding, with no cap, and no cost case holds a finding (hooks/guard_merge.py `_kinds_of`). Decision: commit-refuses-conflict-markers.
- conflict-markers-stale-tracking: a tracking ref left at a markers tip after the remote was rewound hides those commits from `git push origin <branch>` (hooks/guard_merge.py `_push_hit`). Decision: commit-refuses-conflict-markers.
- review-record-shell-edit-head-unread: the shell edit route (`gh api -X PATCH repos/O/R/issues/comments/<id>`) is judged for form only, so a record edited in place can carry a hand-typed head (hooks/guard.py `review_post_hit`). Fix: once PR #1687 (branch mcp-safety-gaps) lands, judge the head through its comment-to-PR read (hooks/guard_merge.py `comment_pull_request`, then `review_record_hit`). Decision: pr-review-record.
- stale-cites-value-top-level-only: py-facts `assigned` reads top-level Name targets only, so a constant in a module-level `if`, or a tuple target, makes a value cite of it a false alarm or a miss. STALE002's `names_in` reads into if and try. Decision: stale-cites-at-merge. Step: value-cites-nested-binds
- stale-cites-value-span-wraps: a code span wrapped across a line break is missed, since a span is read one line at a time. Decision: stale-cites-at-merge. Step: value-cites-wrapped-span
- restore-staged-no-guard: a builder ran git restore --staged in its own worktree; it only unstages, nothing is lost. Fix: none — rare and harmless
- verdict-pipe-lookup-redirect: `command -v verdict 2>/dev/null | head` is refused, because a redirect token hides the lookup from `_is_lookup` (hooks/guard_stream.py `verdict_pipe_hit`). Decision: verdict-pipe-refused.
- verdict-pipe-paren-unpinned: no case or mutant pins the glued-paren read that refuses `(verdict x | tail)` (hooks/guard_stream.py `verdict_pipe_hit`). Decision: verdict-pipe-refused.
- stale009-idiom-false-alarms: STALE009 fires on idioms such as "tool calls", "calls for" and "check runs" (docs/decisions/hook-inputs-carry-token-counts.md:64). Round 1 of #1539 sampled 10 of 226 full-tree hits: 8 real, 1 borderline, 1 false (lint/stale_cites.py `CODE_CLAIM`). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- stale009-misses-subject-forms: STALE009 misses plural subjects ("hooks read"), a backticked noun ("The `ledger` reads"), an anchored file subject ("`hooks/guard.py:12` refuses"), and the verbs blocks, allows and sends (lint/stale_cites.py `CODE_CLAIM`). Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- stale009-unknown-path-untested: STALE009's unknown line for a .mjs, .js, .cjs, .ts or .sh cite has no test. Round 1 of #1539 ran it in memory only. Decision: claims-carry-backing (the claim-backing record).
- red-run-shared-helper-copy: a changed tracked helper that a new test imports by name (lint/_test_git.py, testing/*.py) cannot be copied into the red checkout under a new name. An overwrite leaves it dirty, so the remove is refused and the checkout stays (agents/test-author.md red-run paragraph). Decision: when-a-worktree-may-be-removed
- red-run-trailer-names-copy: the Red-run trailer's command names the renamed red copy (test_x_red.py), which exists nowhere in the repo, so a replay of the trailer fails; nothing replays it now (agents/test-author.md). Decision: red-run-trailer-on-test-commits
- mutate-shard-pop-unpinned: no test pins the MUTATE_SHARD pop in hooks/mutate_guard.py run_suite (:2888). hooks/test_mutate_shared.py pins only the pop in run_suite_file. PR #1466. Decision: ci-job-caps (each CI job's cap leaves its slowest run under 80%).
- sweep-reads-mutate-shard: janitor/mutate_sweep.py calls mutate_shared.run_mutants (:1602), and run_mutants reads MUTATE_SHARD from the environment (hooks/mutate_shared.py:295). A MUTATE_SHARD set in a dev shell shards the sweep. PR #1466. Decision: ci-job-caps (each CI job's cap leaves its slowest run under 80%).
- shard-caps-no-provisional-tag: the -b and -c job caps in .github/workflows/checks.yml (:1226, :1250) have a "Provisional" comment but no `# provisional` tag. The new-job caps at :577 and :1023 have the tag. No check on main reads the tag. PR #1466. Decision: ci-job-caps (each CI job's cap leaves its slowest run under 80%).
- shard-times-unmeasured: the mutate shard step times are unmeasured, so the 60 min caps on the -b and -c jobs stay provisional until 5 green runs (.github/workflows/checks.yml :1224). PR #1466. Decision: ci-job-caps (each CI job's cap leaves its slowest run under 80%).
- ack-docstring-lags-asks: the last_incoming_index docstring (lint/_transcript.py :296-300) says a peer message after a send is an ack that starts no turn. A later sentence says a message that asks starts a turn. PR #1451. Decision: questions-go-to-owner (questions go to the owner).
- ack-frame-fallback-untested: no test covers the frame fallback in peer_body (lint/_transcript.py :277-290), which reads the text from the message frame when origin.body is missing. PR #1451. Decision: questions-go-to-owner (questions go to the owner).
- report-guard-lazy-import: lint/report_guard.py :952 imports peer_body inside _turn_acts. The module already imports from _transcript at :133. PR #1451. Decision: questions-go-to-owner (questions go to the owner).
- alias-allow-mutants-unpinned: the import-alias reader has no allow-side mutants pinned. The T11 header in skills/plain-english-map/tests/tested-by.test.mjs (:39) names two mutants, T11c and T11c2, and both are fail-side. PR #1472. Decision: map-tested-by-line (the map names a file's tests on a checked tested-by line).
- t11b-label-reused: the label T11b names a test in skills/plain-english-map/tests/fact-lines.test.mjs (:802) and another in tested-by.test.mjs (:500). PR #1472. Decision: map-tested-by-line (the map names a file's tests on a checked tested-by line).
- join-literal-reads-as-path: on PR #1462 (branch map11-path-joins), MAP11 (the map check on tested-by lines) reads an all-literal path join, os.path.join("a", "b"), as the string "a/b". The coordinator kept this so the join form and the string form agree (ruling 2026-11-07 15:51 CST, pick A). Six temp-tree fixture paths then count as loads: lint/test_check_claude_md_size.py -> .claude/CLAUDE.md; lint/test_repo_colour.py -> .claude/layout-extras.json; lint/test_rule_audit.py -> hooks/guard.py and hooks/config_watch.py (fixture dict keys); lint/test_check_agent_models.py -> docs/decisions/agent-model-cap.md; lint/test_retired_repos.py -> docs/design/repo-colours.json. Fix: none — rare and harmless. Until: 2026-11-21. Decision: map-tested-by-line (the map names a file's tests on a checked tested-by line).
- split-sentence-eight-copies: the worktree-isolation split sentence is copied into 8 agent files; its one home could be COMMON in hooks/agent_brief.py, and claude-code-guide gets no copy (agents/*.md). Decision: none
- split-sentence-command-term: the split sentence says "command" for a whole shell call, which GLOSSARY.md names a shell call (agents/*.md). Decision: none
- designer-context-test-one-kind: the designer context case accepts informed or directed, so a sentence that names only one kind stays green (hooks/test_blind_brief.py near line 571). The harm is a refused call, not an unsafe act. Decision: design-skills
- answered-reason-test-phrase-only: the reason-text case asserts only the phrase "inline code span", so a reason that invites a claim into a code span stays green (lint/test_report_guard.py near line 4314). Decision: answered-needs-source
- verdict-zero-unittest-pass: bin/verdict reads no unittest "Ran N tests" count, so a run by path of a TestCase file with no __main__ block reads PASS with zero tests run. No such test file exists now (bin/verdict). Decision: none
- credential-words-colon-residual: the `credential-name` shape lets a `:` form letters-only value that reads as words pass, such as `SECRET_KEY: supersecretpassword`, and about 3.6% of random 16-letter lower-case strings (PR #1460 round 3 sample, 200,000 strings); main refused them (skills/waiting-page/export_page.py `credential_is_no_secret`). Fix: none — Claude rarely writes a real secret as plain lower-case words after a credential label, and round 3 judged the net value zero. Until: 2026-11-21. Decision: waiting-data-in-repo.
- map-dirname-dotdot-place: `dirname` of a name bound to text ending in `..` returns the parent of the normalized place. The shell returns HERE. So with `UP="$HERE/.."` and `DIR=$(dirname "$UP")`, `$DIR/x` resolves from the wrong folder. Code: skills/plain-english-map/scripts/lib/fact-readers.mjs:254, PR #1625 (branch map11-shell-binds). Decision: map-tested-by-line (the tested-by line decision).
- map-dirname-root-dot: `dirname` of a name resolved to the repo root returns "." where the real parent is outside the repo. This holds for `$(cd "$HERE/.." && pwd)`, `realpath` and `readlink -f`. It is a false load only when the tail names a tracked root path. Code: skills/plain-english-map/scripts/lib/fact-readers.mjs:254, PR #1625 (branch map11-shell-binds). Decision: map-tested-by-line (the tested-by line decision).
- map-rebind-forms-unseen: a bind after `if`, `elif`, `while`, `until` or `!` is not seen. Neither is a second name after `export` or `readonly`, `A=1 ROOT=/tmp`, or `printf -v`, `mapfile`, `getopts`, `let` or `eval`. So the name keeps its first place. A bind inside an earlier value, such as `X=$(ROOT=/tmp; ..)`, is seen. Code: skills/plain-english-map/scripts/lib/fact-readers.mjs:263-282, PR #1625 (branch map11-shell-binds). Decision: map-tested-by-line (the tested-by line decision).
- map-declare-later-name-voids: a later name on a `declare`, `typeset` or `local` line voids the name at any place, even the same place. The record keeps a same-place bind. The void is conservative: it can only miss a load, which is a false fail. Code: skills/plain-english-map/scripts/lib/fact-readers.mjs:266-271, PR #1625 (branch map11-shell-binds). Decision: map-tested-by-line (the tested-by line decision).
- map-shellloads-comment: the comment above `shellLoads` omits `typeset`, which `bindPattern` reads. Its line 288 runs 192 characters. Code: skills/plain-english-map/scripts/lib/fact-readers.mjs:284-289, PR #1625 (branch map11-shell-binds). Decision: map-tested-by-line (the tested-by line decision).
- tested-by-t11-narrates-history: the test comments at skills/plain-english-map/tests/tested-by.test.mjs:1306 and :1384 say T11aa and T11af were re-pointed to FAIL at lane pick 2026-11-07 16:38 CST. Their asserts already expect no load (`mustNotLoad`), so the wording narrates history. The block header at :1292 repeats it. Code: PR #1615 (branch map11-anchor-forms). Decision: map-tested-by-line (the tested-by line decision).
- cloud-comment-edit-refused: in a cloud lane session, the auto-mode classifier refused `Bash` running `gh api -X PATCH` on an existing issue comment ("External System Writes"). So a lane cannot edit its own posted PR comment (PRs #1472, #1512, 2026-11-07 17:57 CST). The owner decides whether to allow it. Decision: refusals-reach-the-owner.
- hand-written-review-records: when `merge --review-record` could not page the PR comments, a lane wrote review records by hand on PRs #1472 and #1512 (2026-11-07). Records come only from the merge tool (coordinator ruling 2026-11-07 17:56 CST). Fix: none — cause fixed by #1634; #1601 checks the head. Until: 2026-11-21. Decision: pr-review-record.
- retire-excuse-silent-refusal: when the silent-undo check refuses a Retire-run cite, it prints only the delete finding, never why the cite failed (lint/check_silent_undo.py `retire_excuses`). The debt-close check prints the reason in the same merge run. Decision: debt-close-runs-retire.
- retire-excuse-green-test-no-run-read: a cited debt whose Retire test runs green is excused without a read of the cited run, because the test on the head is the proof (lint/check_debt_retire.py `retired`). Decision: debt-close-runs-retire.
- merge-pre-put-read-stop: the pre-PUT read in `Host.merge`, `self.pr(n)`, raises a plain Stop from `Host.pr` on failure. The claim stays, but the stop reads as a refusal, not as "unknown, rerun" (merge/merge.py `Host.merge`). Fix: in `Host.merge`, wrap the pre-PUT `self.pr(n)` read so a Stop from `Host.pr` is raised as `Unproven`, with the rerun remedy. A 401/403 read (`HTTP_DENIED`) stays a plain Stop, as `unknown_unless_denied` does. Decision: standing-merge-grant (the repo's merge grant, its rule that only a proven refusal reverts a claim).
- unread-reads-revert-claim: three reads that could not run still revert the claim after its push. Each raises a plain Stop or returns a plain string, so the caller treats it as a refusal. The first is a lint run that exits non-zero with unknown lines, and the second is a `ci-scope.sh` exit, both in `small_base_move`. A `stamp --check` that exits 1 also reverts, in `prove`, which cannot tell a crash from a taken number. A RuntimeError out of `host.merge` reverts too, because `except Exception` sends it to `fail`, though the merge may have landed. Each needs a deterministic fault. Fix: split each case. In `small_base_move`, the lint run returns UnreadText unless a `FAIL` line is printed, and a `ci-scope.sh` non-zero exit or a run with no flags returns UnreadText. In `prove`, `stamp --check` exit 1 is a refusal only when stamp prints a problem line, and a stack trace or no output is `unproven`. stamp.mjs prints its problems with a bare `console.error`. The fix adds a `PROBLEM:` prefix to each problem line in the `--check` branch of `actions/stamp/stamp.mjs`, and `prove` reads only prefixed lines as refusals. A Node stack trace has no prefixed line. In `confirm`, the `except Exception` after `host.merge` calls `unproven` when `merging` is true, as the OSError branch does. Decision: standing-merge-grant (the repo's merge grant, its rule that only a proven refusal reverts a claim).
- reviewed-head-push-config-silent: when git cannot read the push remote (corrupt config), the guard lets the push through with no stderr line, though its record says one line (hooks/guard_merge.py `reviewed_head_push_hit`). Decision: pr-review-record.
- reviewed-head-push-multi-source: a push of several refspecs asks unless every source equals the PR head, so `git push origin feat other` asks about feat's PR even when feat sends nothing (hooks/guard_merge.py `_sends_nothing`). Decision: pr-review-record.
- reviewed-head-push-cases-thin: no case sets `branch.<b>.pushRemote`, `remote.pushDefault` against an origin branch remote, several refspecs or a detached HEAD, so a mutant of the remote order may live (hooks/test_guard.py). Decision: pr-review-record.
